(b) ko ha mahuʻinga ke totongi ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘i he Konga V ‘oku makatuʻunga ‘i ha peseti ‘o e ngaahi paʻanga hū mai ala ngāueʻaki ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni,

‘oku ‘ikai toe lahi ange ‘i he 2% ‘o e pa’anga hū mai ala ngāueʻaki ‘a taha maʻu laiseni.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(1), ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

## KONGA VIII - TU‘UTU‘UNI FAKATEKINIKALE

VAHE I - FAKAFIKA MO E FAKATU‘ASILA ʻILEKITULŌNIKA

#### 68 Fatongia ki he fakafika

Kuo pau ke tokanga‘i ‘e he Sino Pule ‘a hono pule‘i, palani, fakahoko ngāue, pule‘i mo hono vahevahe ‘o e fakafika ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki.

#### 69 Fatongia ke fakatuʻasila ‘ilekitulōnika

(1) Kuo pau ki he Minisitā ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (3), ke ne fokotuʻu ha taha pe ko ha niʻihi tokolahi ange ke tali mo fatongiaʻaki ‘a e fakatuʻasila

‘ilekitulōnika.

(2) ‘E ngofua ko e taha kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ko e Sino Pule.

(3) Kuo pau ko hono tali ‘o e fatongia ki he fakatuʻasila ʻilekitulōnikaʹ ‘e fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakangofua tuʻupau mo e loto ki ai ‘a e Mafai ki he Vahevahe ‘o e Ngaahi Fika ‘Initaneti.

(4) Kuo pau ki he taha kuo fokotuʻuʹ ke ne faipau ki he ngaahi founga, ngaahi tuʻunga ‘o e mo e ngaahi alea mo e Mafai ki he Vahevahe ‘o e Ngaahi Fika

‘Initaneti.

(5) Kuo pau ki he Minisitā ke ne tokangaʻi ‘a e faipau ‘a e taha kuo fokotuʻu ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e kupuʹ ni.

(6) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vaheʹ ni —

(a) ‘ʻfakatuʻasila ʻilekitulōnika’’ ‘oku ‘uhinga ki he lesisita mo hono vahevahe ‘o e ngaahi hingoa fakaʻēlia ‘i he fika ‘initaneti fakafonua levolo-maʻolunga-faka’elia (ccTLD) ‘.to’ ;

(b) ‘ʻMafai ki he Vahevahe ‘o e Ngaahi Fika ‘Initaneti’’ ‘oku ‘uhinga ki he kautaha ‘okuʹ ne tokangaekina ‘a e fengāueʻaki fakavahaʻapuleʻanga

‘o e tefito DNS (DNS Root), Fakatuʻasila IP mo e ngaahi naunau talite

‘initaneti fakalukufua kehe pea kau ki ai ha fetongi ki he kautaha ko ia ; mo e

(c) ʻʻtaha kuo fokotuʻu’’ ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha taha kuo fokotuʻu ‘e he Minisitā ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1).

#### 70 Palani fakafika mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakafika

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke fokotuʻu ha palani fakafika fakafonua ki hono fakafika ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘a ia kuo pau ke ngāueʻaki ki he taha maʻu laiseni ‘i he Laoʹ ni.

(2) Kuo pau ko e palani fakafika kuo fokotuʻu ‘e he Sino Pule —

(a) ke fakamahinopau ‘a e ngaahi fika ke ngāueʻaki felaveʻi mo e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutakiʹ pea ‘e ngofua ke fakamahinopau mo e ngaahi fika kehekehe ke ngāueʻaki felaveʻi mo e fakahoko ‘o e faʻahinga kehekehe

‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ;

(b) ke fakakau ‘i hono fakakaukauʻi mo ha palani fakafika lolotonga mo hono vahevahe ‘o e ngaahi fika ; pea

(c) ke tatau mo e ngaahi fiemaʻu ‘o ha aleapau fakavahaʻapuleʻanga, ngaahi fatongia, pe ko ha ngaahi konivesio ‘a ia ‘oku kau ki ai ‘a e Puleʻanga pe kuo fakamoʻoni ki ai.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne faʻu ‘a e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku fakamahinopau ai —

(a) ‘a e founga ki hono fakahū atu ‘o ha tohi kole ki hano vaheʻi ‘o e ngaahi fika ;

(b) ‘a e tuʻunga ki he taha ‘oku taau ke fakahū atu ha tohi kole ;

(c) ‘a e founga ki hono vaheʻi ‘o e ngaahi fika ‘e he Sino Pule ;

(d) ha totongi ‘e ala hilifaki ;

(e) ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki hono ngāue’aki ‘o e ngaahi fika pe ha ngaahi fakangatangata ki ha fika pau ;

(f) ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku fakahaaʻi ai ‘a e ngaahi fika ‘oku fevahevaheʻaki ai mo e puleʻi ‘o e anga hono ngāueaki ;

(g) ngaahi tuʻunga ‘e lava ke tukuange ai ‘a e ngaahi fika, kaniseli pe fakafoki pea mo e ngaahi founga ‘e lava ke hoko ai ‘eni ; mo

(h) ha meʻa kehe ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakafika ‘a ia ‘oku pehē ‘e he Sino Pule

‘oku feʻunga pe totonu ki he pe ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakahoko ‘o hono ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

(4) Kuo pau ko e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (3) ‘e fakatatau ki he palani fakafika pea kuo pau ke fakapapauʻi ko e ngaahi fika ‘oku vaheʻi ‘i he makatuʻunga ‘oku ‘ikai filifilimanako.

(5) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakapapauʻi ko ha vahe ‘o ha ngaahi fika ‘oku fakatatau ki he palani fakafikaʹ mo ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ‘i he kupuʹ ni.

(6) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea fokotu’u ‘a e palani fakafika fakafonua pe faʻu‘ohangaahi tuʻutuʻuni‘ihe kupuʹni,ke nefakahoko ha alea‘i he kupu 23.

#### 71 Lesisita ‘o e ngaahi vahevahe fakafika

KuopaukiheSinoPulekefokotuʻumotauhihalesisita‘oengaahivahevahefakafika

‘oku kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fakaikiiki ‘o e ngaahi vahevahe fakafika kotoa.

#### 72 Ngaahi telefoni fakafokifā

(1) ‘EngofuakiheSinoPulekefakahokohatuʻutuʻuni‘okufekauʻaki moengaahi telefoni ‘oku fakahoko ki he ngaahi kautaha ngāue fakafokifā ‘i he Puleʻanga

‘o ngāueʻaki ‘a e ngaahi fika ngāue fakafokifā.

(2) ‘E ngofua ke kau ki he ngaahi meʻa ‘oku fekauʻaki mo ha tuʻutuʻuni‘i he kupu- siʻi (1), ka kuo pau heʻikai fakangatangata ki —

(a) hono fakapapauʻi ‘o e ngaahi fika ngāue fakafokifā mo e ngaahi kautaha ngāue fakafokifā koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o e Laoʹ ni ;

(b) hono fiemaʻu ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni ke fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi ngāue ‘e malava ai ‘e he kakai ‘oku nau ngāueʻakiʹ ke ngāueʻaki ‘a e ngaahi fika ngāue fakafokifā ke telefoni ki he ngaahi kautaha ngāue fakafokifā ;

(c) hono fakapapauʻi ‘o ha taha fakahoko ngāue telefoni fakafokifā, ‘a ia te ne tokangaekina ‘a hono tali mo e ngāue ki he ngaahi telefoni ki ha fika ngāue fakafokifā mo fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi telefoni pehē ki he kautaha ngāue fakafokifā taau ; mo

(d) hono hilifaki ‘o engaahi fatongia ki he kau maʻulaiseni pekau fakahoko ngāue telefoni fakafokifā fekauʻaki mo e taimi ‘oku tali ai ‘a e ngaahi telefoni ki he ngaahi fika ngāue fakafokifā.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa ‘i hono fakahoko ‘o ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu- siʻi (1), ke fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

#### 73 Malava ke ‘ave holo ‘a e fika

(1) ‘Oku ngofua ki he Sino Pule ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (2) ki he (5), ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e malava ke ‘ave holo ‘a e fika.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, kimuʻa pea ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e fika ‘oku malava ‘ave holo —

(a) fakahoko ha alea fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu 23 ; pea

(b) kuo pau ke teuteu ha lipooti ki he Minisitā ‘o fakahā ‘a e ngaahi ola ‘o e alea pea mo e ngaahi faleʻi ‘a e Sino Pule pe ‘e ngāueʻaki ‘a e fika ‘oku malava ‘ave holoʹ pea mo ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻutuni kuo fokotuʻu ki hono ngāueʻaki ‘o e fika ‘oku malava ‘ave holo.

(3) Kuo pau heʻikai ke fokotuʻu ‘e he Sino Pule ‘a hono ngāueʻaki ‘o e fika ‘oku malava ‘ave holo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2) tukukehe, ‘i hano ‘osi fakakaukauʻi ‘o e ngaahi ola ‘o e alea ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2), ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Sino Pule ko e —

(a) ‘oku ʻi ai ‘a e fekau fakapotopoto ‘e ngalingali ke hoko ki he fika ‘oku malava ke ‘ave holo ; mo e

(b) ko e ngaahi lelei ‘o hono fuofua tuku atu ‘o e fika ‘oku malava ‘ave holo

‘oku mamafa ange ‘i he ngaahi fakamole.

(4) ‘E ngofua ‘o kapau ‘e fokotuʻu ‘e he Sino Pule, ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2), ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ke faʻu fekauʻaki mo e fika ‘oku malava ‘ave holo, ki he Minisitā ke,

‘i ha tuʻutuʻuni, fakangofua pe ‘ikai tali (pe ‘i he kakato pe konga) ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a e Sino Pule.

(5) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ʻikai faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e fika ʻoku malava ‘ave holo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), tukukehe ko e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko ia ‘oku faitatau mo e ngaahi fokotuʻu kuo fakangofua ‘e he Minisitā ‘i he kupu- siʻi (4).

(6) Kuo pau ki he Minisitā ke fakahā ki he Sino Pule ‘i ha tohi ‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ki ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4), pea kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke pulusi

‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ko ia ‘i he uepisaiti.

#### 74 Ngaahi Tohi Fakahinohino

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku ngāue ki hono pulusi

‘o e ngaahi tohi fakahinohino telefoni pea mo hono fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi ngāue

‘o e ngaahi tohi fakahinohino.

(2) ‘E ngofua ke fakakau ‘a e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku fekauʻaki mo e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ka kuo pau ke ʻikai fakangatangata ki —

(a) hono tānaki, ngāueʻaki mo e tonu ‘o e fakamatala kuo tānaki mei he kastioma ‘a ia ‘e ngofua (fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi fika ‘ikai tohi hokohoko) ke pulusi ‘i ha tohi fakahinohino telefoni pe ngāueʻaki ‘i ha tohi fakahinohino fakamatala ngāue ;

(b) he ngaahi fika kuo tohi hokohoko mo ‘ikai tohi hokohoko ;

(c) hono fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi ngāue tohi fakahinohino ki he fakamatala ; mo

(d) hono pulusi ‘o e ngaahi tohi fakahinohino telefoni.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea ne fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(1), ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

#### 75 ‘Ikai ha ngaahi totonu ki he koloa ‘i he ngaahi fika

Kuo pau heʻikai maʻu ‘e he kau maʻu laiseni, kau kasitomā mo e kakai ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi ha ngaahi totonu koloa ‘i he ngaahi fika.

VAHE 2 - NGAAHI TU‘UNGA FAKATEKINIKALE

#### 76 Ngaahi tu‘unga fakatekinikale

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘i he tuʻutuʻuni —

(a) ke faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻunga fakatekinikale ; pea

(b) ngāue‘aki ‘o e ngaahi tu‘unga fakatekinikale ‘e fanongonongo ‘e he tuʻunga fakavaha‘apule‘anga ‘oku nau fokotu‘u ha ngaahi sino pe ngaahi maʻu mafai pule ‘o e ngaahi fonua kehe,

‘a ia kuo pau ke ngāueʻaki ki he kau maʻu laiseni mo ha taha kehe kuo fakamahinopau ‘i he tuʻunga fakatekinikale totonu.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea ne faʻu pe ngāueʻaki ha ngaahi tuʻunga fakatekinikale ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

#### 77 Ngaahi me‘a ki he ngaahi tu‘unga fakatekinikale

‘E ngofua ke fakakau ki he ngaahi tuʻunga fakatekinikale kuo faʻu pe ngāueʻaki ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu 76, ka kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai fakangatangata ki he —

(a) ngaahi fiemaʻu fakatekinikale ki he ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki, ngaahi netiueka fetuʻutaki, ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki mo e ngaahi meʻangāue kasitomā ;

(b) ngaahi pōto‘i ngāue ‘o e kau fokotu‘u naunau fetu’utaki mo e me‘angāue kasitomā (ka ‘oku ‘ikai kau ki ai ‘a e fakauaea ‘a e kasitomā) ;

(c) fakalakalaka ‘a e malu mei he ivi‘i ‘uhila fakamakinito mo ‘aonga;

(d) fakalakalaka ‘a e malu; pe

(e) ha toe meʻa kehe fakatekinikale ‘a ia ‘oku pehē ‘e he Sino Pule ‘oku fiemaʻu mo totonu ki he pe felaveʻi mo e fakahoko ‘o hono ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

#### 78 Ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakatekinikale

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e meʻangāue kasitomā ‘okuʹ ne fakamahinopau —

(a) ‘a e faʻahinga ‘o e meʻangāue kasitomā ‘a ia kuo pau ke fakangofua kimuʻa pea fakahoko ki ha netiueka fetuʻutaki ‘i Tonga ;

(b) ‘a e makatuʻunga ki hono fakangofua ‘o ha meʻangāue kastioma ;

(c) ‘a e founga ki hono fakamoʻoniʻi ‘o e muimui ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga ko ia ;

(d) ‘aefounga‘emalavake‘iloʻiai‘eheSinoPulemongāueʻaki‘aengaahi tuʻutuʻuni, ngaahi tuʻunga mo e ngaahi founga fakangofua ‘a e ngaahi fonua kehe ; mo

(e) ha ngaahi fokotuʻutuʻu makehe ki hono fakamoʻoniʻi ‘o e meʻangāue kasitomā kuo ‘osi ngāue’aki.

(2) ʻE ngofua ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ke toe fakamahinopau —

(a) ha founga ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakamoʻoniʻi ‘a e muimui ‘a e ngaahi sino sivi ‘i Tongaʹ ; mo

(b) ha toe meʻa kehe ‘oku felaveʻi mo e meʻangāue kasitomā pe ngaahi naunau netiueka ‘a ia ‘oku pehē ‘e he Sino Pule ‘oku fiemaʻu mo totonu ki he pe felaveʻi mo e fakahoko ‘o hono ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ki muʻa pea ne faʻu ‘a e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupuʹ ni, ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

#### 79 Lesisita ‘o e meʻangāue kasitomā

Kuopauki he SinoPule kefokotuʻumo tauhi halesisita ʻo efaʻahinga‘oemeʻangāue kasitomā kuo fakangofua.

#### 80 Ngaahi aleapau femahinoʻaki ‘oku ‘ilo

‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne kau ki ha ngaahi aleapau femahinoʻaki ‘oku ʻilo mo e ngaahi maʻu mafai ‘i he ngaahi fonua kehe ke fakahoko ‘a e femahinoʻaki ‘oku ‘ilo

‘o e, fakamoʻoni mo hono fakangofua ‘o e meʻangāue fetuʻutaki ‘i he ngaahi fonua kehe mo ‘i Tonga.

#### 81 Fakauaea ‘a e kasitomā

‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā, ‘i he faleʻi ‘a e Sino Pule, ke faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 186 ki hono tokangaʻi mo e laiseni ‘o e kau ngāue fokotuʻu ‘o e fakauaea ‘a e kasitomā.

#### 82 Tu‘usi ‘o e me‘angāue kasitomā

‘E ngofua ki ha taha ma‘u laiseni ke ‘ikai tali mei hano tufaki atu pe te ne ta‘ofi hono tufaki atu ha ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘o kapau ‘e fakakaukau ‘a e taha ma‘u laiseni ko e me‘angāue kasitomā ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ki he netiueka ‘a e taha ma‘u laiseni, ki hono faka‘aonga‘i ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki, ‘oku fakatu‘utāmaki pe ‘oku ngalingali ke fakatu‘utāmaki ki he malupe ki hefakahoko ngāue‘oengāue fetuʻutakipe kihe malu

‘a e mo‘ui ‘a ha taha pē ‘oku ‘ikai fakangofua.

#### 83 Kole ki he Sino Pule ke fakahinohino ki he taha ma‘u laiseni

‘E ngofua ʻoka ‘ikai tali ha taha ma‘u laiseni ke ne ‘oatu pe te ne ta‘ofi hono ‘oatu atu ha ngāue fetuʻutaki ki ha taha ‘i he kupu 82, ke kole ‘a e taha ko ia ki he Sino Pule ki ha fakahinohino ‘i he kupu 84.

#### 84 Ngofua ke fakahinohino ‘e he Sino Pule ke fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi ngāue

fetuʻutaki

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono maʻu ‘o ha tohi kole ‘i he kupu 83, ‘i ha fanongonongo tohi —

(a) ke fakahā ki he taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku kaunga ki ai ‘a e tohi kole; pea

(b) kuo pau ke fakaafeʻi ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ke fakahoko ha ngaahi fokotuʻu ki he Sino Pule ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 5 hili ‘a hono maʻu ‘o e fanongonongo pe ‘e fakahoko ha fakahinohino ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2).

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule hili ‘a hono maʻu ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu mei he taha maʻu laiseniʹ pe ko e ‘osi ‘o e vahaʻa taimi ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(1)(b), ke fakakaukauʻi ha ngaahi fokotuʻu kuo maʻu pea kapau, ‘i hono fakakaukauʻi ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu ko ia, ‘okuʹ ne fiemalie ko e fakahoko ‘o e meʻangāue kasitomā kuo ‘osi tuʻusi ‘i he kupu 82 ‘oku ‘ikai fakatu‘utāmaki pe

‘oku ngalingali kefakatu‘utāmaki ki hemalu pengaahingāue ‘aengaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki pe ki he malu ‘o ha taha pea ‘oku fakangofua, kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke fakahinohino ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni totonu, ‘i he fanongonongo tohi, ke ʻoatu ‘a e ngāue fetuʻutaki kuo kole.

(3) Kuo pau ki he taha maʻu laiseni ke faipau ki ha fakahinohino ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2).

VAHE 3 - PULE‘I ‘A E IVI FAKAMAFOLA FAKALETIŌ

#### 85 Pule‘i ‘e he Sino Pule ‘a e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne tokangaekina ‘a hono puleʻi, palani, fakahoko ngāue, puleʻi mo e laiseni ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘i he Puleʻanga.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono fakahoko ‘o hono ngaahi ngāue ‘i he Vaheʹ ni, ke ne fakapapauʻi ko hono puleʻi ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘i he Puleʻanga —

(a) ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he natula ‘oku ‘ikai filifilimanako pea ‘oku lelei fakaʻekonomika; pea

(b) ‘oku fakatatau mo e ngaahi tuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e Kautaha Fakafetuʻutaki Fakavahaʻapuleʻanga mo e ngaahi talite fakavahaʻapuleʻanga mo fakavahefonua, ngaahi tukupā, ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ngāue, mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘i he felotoi ki ai pe ngāueʻaki ‘e he Puleʻanga.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke fakakau, ‘o ‘ikai fakangatangata ‘a hono ngaahi fatongia ‘i he kupu 85(2), ‘i hono fakahoko ‘o hono ngaahingāue ‘i he kupu 87 ki he 88 —

(a) ‘a e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Laoʹ ni;

(b) ‘a e ola ‘o e Palani Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua pea mo e Tepile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua ‘i hono ngāueʻaki ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘i he lolotonga pea mo e kahaʻu; pea

(c) mo e ngāueʻaki maau mo lelei ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō.

#### 86 Palani Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke fokotuʻu, pea ke fakahoko ha vakaiʻi ke fakaaʻu ‘o

‘ikai toe siʻi hifo ‘i he tuʻo taha ‘i he taʻu ‘e 2 kotoa pē, ha palani ke ‘iloa ko e Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua kuo fokotuʻu ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu siʻi (1) ke tuku atu ‘a e Palani ‘a e Sino Pule ki hono puleʻi ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘o ‘ikai toe siʻi ‘i he vahaʻa taimi ko e taʻu ‘e 5 kau ki ai —

(a) ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ngāue ki he founga hono ngāueʻaki pe vahevahe ‘o e ngaahi konga kehekehe ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ;

(b) ha ngaahi liliu kuo fokotuʻu ki he founga hono ngāueʻaki pe vahevahe ‘o e ngaahi konga ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō (kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fakaikiiki ‘o e ngaahi liliu mo e ngaahi taimi fakahoko ngāue); pea

(c) ko hono ‘fakaʻilonga ‘o ha konga ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘a ia ko ha koloa mahuʻinga fakapuleʻanga.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua kuo fokotuʻu pe vakaiʻi ke fakaaʻu ‘e he Sino Pule ke fakatatau ki he fakangofua ‘o etuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Minisitā.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea ne fokotuʻu pe vakaiʻi ke fakaaʻu ‘a e Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua, ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

(5) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne tauhi ‘a e Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua pea ke pulusi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i heʻene uepisaiti.

#### 87 Tuʻutuʻuni ha Tēpile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fokotuʻu ha tohi ngāue ke ‘iloa ko e Tēpile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua koeʻuhi ke puleʻi ‘a hono ngāueʻaki ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō.

(2) Kuo pau ko e Tēpile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua ‘e tauhi ‘e he Sino Pule pea ke pulusi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i heʻene uepisaiti.

#### 88 Kakano ‘o e Tēpile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua

Kuo pau ko e Tēpile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua kuo fokotuʻu ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu 87 ke fakahā ai ‘a e founga ki hano ngāueʻaki ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō pea ‘e ngofua ke fakakau ki ai kae ‘ikai fakangatangata ki he —

(a) ngaahi vahevahe filīkuenisī lolotonga mo ia kuo ‘osi palani ;

(b) ngaahi filīkuenisī lolotonga mo ia kuo ‘osi palani talifaki;

(c) ngaahi meʻa kehe ‘e ala fiemaʻu ‘e he Minisitā.

#### 89 Ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘o fakamahinopau —

(a) ‘a e faʻahinga ‘o e ngaahi laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘a ia ‘e ngofua ke tuku atu ‘i heʻene fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi tapui ‘o e ivi fakamafola pe ha ngaahi filīkuenisī pau ;

(b) ‘a e ngaahi tūkunga ‘a ia ‘e fakaʻatā ai ha meʻangāue fetuʻutaki fakaletiō mei he laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ;

(c) ‘a e founga ki hono fakahū atu ‘o ha tohi kole ki ha laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ;

(d) ‘a e makatuʻunga pe ko hai ‘oku taau ke fakahū atu ha tohi kole ;

(e) ‘a e ngaahi totongi ‘o e tohi kole ke totongi ki he Sino Pule (‘oka ‘i ai) ;

(f) ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki hono ngāueʻaki ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō pe ha ngaahi konga pau ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō, kau ki ai ha kaunoa ki he ngaahi fiemaʻ u;

(g) ha ngaahi tuʻunga laiseni mo ha ngaahi tuʻunga fakatekinikale ‘e ngāueʻaki ki ha ngaahi laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō pau pe ha ngaahi meʻangāue pau ;

(h) ha ngaahi totongi laiseni ‘a ia ‘e ala totongi ki he ngaahi laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ;

(i) ‘a e ngaahi tūkunga ‘a ia ‘e tukuange ai ‘a e ngaahi laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō, taʻofi fakataimi, fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe fakatonutonu ; mo e

(j) ngaahi founga ki ha tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo ha ngaahi fakakikihi felaveʻi mo e ngaahi fakangatangata ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ke fakamahinopau —

(a) ha founga ‘oku totonu mo hā mahino ki hono tuku atu ‘o e ngaahi laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ki he ngaahi koloa mahuʻinga fakapuleʻanga, ‘a ia ‘e malava ke fakakau ki ai ha founga feʻauʻauhi ;

(b) ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni pe ngaahi founga ki hono vahe ‘o ha ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ko ha konga ‘o e founga liliu maʻu laiseni kuo fakamahinopau ‘i he kupu 187 ; mo e

(c) meʻa kehe ‘oku felaveʻi mo e ivi fakamafola fakaletio ‘a ia ‘oku fakakaukauʻi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘oku fiemaʻu pe feʻunga ki he pe felaveʻi mo e fakahoko ‘o hono ngaahi ngāue ‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakapapau’i ko ha ngaahi totongi kuo tuʻutuʻuni

‘i he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) kuo pau ko e —

(a) totongi ki hono vaheʻi ‘o ha koloa mahuʻinga fakapuleʻanga ‘i he founga

‘o ha founga feʻauʻauhi – ko e lahi ‘o e paʻanga ‘e ala totongi ko e ola ‘o e founga feʻauʻauhi ; pea

(b) tukukehe, ko ha paʻanga tuʻupau kuo pulusi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i heʻene uepisaiti pe ko ha toe founga ‘e taha ‘oku pehē ‘e he Sino Pule ‘oku feʻunga.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa ‘i hono faʻu ‘o e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupuʹ ni, ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

#### 90 Ngaahi laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō

(1) Kuo pau ki ha taha ke ʻikai ke ne —

(a) fokotuʻu(establish), fakahoko ngāue pe ngāueʻaki ha ngāue fetuʻutaki fakaletiō ;

(b) fokotuʻu (install), fakahoko ngāue pe ngāueʻaki ha meʻangāue fakahoko fakaletiō ; pe

(c) fokotuʻu (establish), fakahoko ngāue pe ngāueʻaki ha meʻangāue pe ngāue fetuʻutaki fakaletiō ‘i ha feituʻu pe ‘i ha vaka pe vakapuna kuo lesisita ‘i he Puleʻanga,

‘ikai ‘i ai ha laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō kuo foaki ‘e he Sino Pule ‘o fakatatau mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō, tukukehe

‘i hano fakangofua hono ngāueʻaki ‘i he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō.

(2) ‘I ha kole ‘e ha taha ha laiseni ke ʻoʻona, fakahoko ngāue pe ngāueʻaki ‘a e ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki pe ʻoatu ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki pea ko hono fakahoko ngāue ‘o e ngaahi naunau netiueka totonu mo hono fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘oku fiemaʻu ‘a e hū ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō —

(a) kuo pau ki he taha ko ia ke kole ha laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘i he taimi tatau mo e tohi kole ki ha laiseni fakahoko ngāue netiueka ; pea

(b) kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakakaukauʻi fakataha ‘a e ongo tohi kole pea ‘e ngofua ke ‘ikai tali ‘a hono foaki ‘o ha laiseni fakahoko ngāue netiueka ‘o kapau ‘oku ‘ikai faipau ‘a e taha kole ki he ngaahi fiemaʻu ‘o e laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō.

#### 91 Ngaahi vahevahe ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakapapauʻi ko ha vahevahe ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘i ha laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘oku fakatatau ki he Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua, Tēpile Vahevahe Filīkuenisī Fakafonua mo ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fokotuʻu mo tauhi ha lesisita ‘o e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi vahevahe ‘o e ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ki he kau maʻu laiseni.

VAHE 4 - PULEʻI ‘O E NGAAHI TUʻUʻANGA SATELAITE

#### 92 Puleʻi ‘o e ngaahi tuʻuʻanga satelaite

(1) Kuo pau ki he Minisitā (pe ko ha taha pe niʻihi lahi ange kuo fokotuʻu ‘e he Minisitā) ke tali mo tokangaekina ‘a hono tokangaekina fakalukufua mo hono vahevahe ‘o e tuʻuʻanga satelaite ‘oku ‘i ai ‘a e totonu ki ai ‘a e Puleʻanga.

(2) ‘E ngofua ko e taha kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ko e Sino Pule.

## KONGA IX - TU‘UTU‘UNI FAKASŌSIALE

VAHE 1 - NGAAHI NGᾹUE FAKAHOKO KAKANO

#### 93 Ngāue‘aki ‘o e Vahe 1 ‘o e Konga VIII

Ko e Vahe ko eni ‘ataʻatā pē ‘e ngāueʻaki ki he kakano ‘oku fakahoko ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘aki ‘a e founga ‘o ha ngāue fakahoko kakano.

#### 94 Ngaahi tuʻunga ki he fakamafola mo e kakano

‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā, ‘i hano tuʻutuʻuni, ke faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻunga ki he ngaahi ngāue fakahoko kakano ‘oku ‘oatu ‘i he Puleʻanga ‘a ia kuo pau ke ngāueʻaki ki he kotoa ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni ‘oku nau fakahoko ha ngāue fakahoko kakano.

#### 95 Ngaahi meʻa ki he ngaahi tuʻunga fakamafola mo e kakano

‘E ngofua ke fakakau ki he ngaahi tuʻunga fakamafola mo e kakano kuo faʻu ‘e he Ministā ‘i he kupu 71 ka kuo pau ke ʻikai fakangatangata ki he —

(a) ngaahi fakangatangata pe ngaahi fakatapui ‘oku felaveʻi mo hono ‘oatu ‘o ha kakano ‘oku ʻikai taau pe ko ha faʻahinga pau ‘o ha kakano ;

(b) ngaahi founga ‘o hono fakakalakalasi ‘o e kakano ;

(c) ngaahi fakangatangata pe ngaahi fakatapui ‘oku felaveʻi mo e kakano tuʻuaki ;

(d) fakafofonga ‘o e ‘ulungaanga faka-Tonga mo e tala fakafonua ; mo e

(e) ngaahi fakangatangata ki he ngaahi houa ‘a ia ‘oku lolotonga fakahoko ai ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fakamafola pe ngaahi ngāue ki hono tufaki ‘o e kakano ke fakafaingofua ‘a e kau atu.

#### 96 Ngofua ke fakahinohino ‘e he Potungāue ha taha ma‘u laiseni ke faipau

‘E ngofua fakatatau ki he fakangofua ‘e he Minisitā, ki he Potungāue ke fakahinohino ki ha taha ma‘u laiseni pe ko ha kalasi ‘o ha kau maʻu laiseni ke —

(a) faipau ki ha tu‘unga ‘o ha kakano kuo ‘osi fakangofuape kupu 98,100,101,102, pe 104 — pe

(b) liliu ha ngāue fakahoko kakano kuo fakahoko koe‘uhi ke faipau ‘a e ngāue ko iá ki he ngaahi tu‘unga kakano pe kupu 98,100,101,102, pe 104.

#### 97 Potungāue ke ne maʻu ‘a e ngaahi mafai fakahoko ngāue ‘o e Sino Pule

Kuo pau ki he Minisitā koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o hono fakahoko ‘o e kupu 96, ke ne maʻu ‘a e ngaahi mafai kuo foaki ki he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu 159, 160, 161, 162, 163, 164 mo e 169 pea ko e ngaahi lave ki he Sino Pule ‘i he ngaahi kupu ko ia kuo pau ke lau ko e ngaahi lave ki he Minisitā.

#### 98 Sivi

Kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai ʻoatu ‘e ha taha ma‘u laiseni ha kakano ‘a ia —

(a) ‘oku kovi mo fakalielia;

(b) ‘oku hā ai ha fakamālohi fakalilifu;

(c) ‘oku lea kovi;

(d) ‘oku talisone pe angatu‘u;

(e) ‘oku lau‘ikovi; pe

(f) ‘oku fepaki mo e ngaahi Lao ‘o e Pule‘anga.

#### 99 Lao ki he Lau‘ikovi

Koe‘uhi ko e ngaahi taumu‘a ‘o e Lao ki he Lau‘i Kovi (Vahe 33), ko ha kakano ‘oku tuku atu ‘e ha taha ma‘u laiseni, kapau ko ha —

(a) kakano ongo, kuo pau ke lau ‘oku ‘i he fōtunga ‘o e ongo pe lea; mo e

(b) kakano fanongo mo mamata, kuo pau ke lau ‘oku ‘i he fōtunga ‘o e mamata, lea mo e ongo.

#### 100 Lotu

ʻE ngofua fakatatau ki he fakangofua ‘e he Kapineti, ki he Minisitā pe ha taha kuo ne fakamafai‘i ‘e ia, ke netuʻutuʻuni ke fiemaʻu ki ha taha ma‘u laiseni, ke ne ‘oatu, ʻikai ha totongi, ha kakano fakalotu pe ko ha kakano kehe ‘oku ‘o ha natula fakalotu lolotonga ‘a e ngaahi vaha‘a taimi ‘e tu‘utu‘uni ‘e he Minisitā, pe ko ha taha te ne fakamafaiʻi ki ai hono mafai.

#### 101 Lelei fakafonua

ʻE ngofua fakatatau ki he fakangofua ‘e he Kapineti, ki he Minisitā pe ko ha taha te ne fakamafaiʻi ‘e ia, ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ke fiemaʻu ki ha taha maʻu laiseni, ke ne ʻoatu

‘ikai ha totongi, ha kakano felaveʻi mo e ki he ngaahi me‘a ki he lelei fakafonua, ‘a ia ‘e tu‘utu‘uni ‘e he Minisitā, pe ko ha taha te ne fakamafaiʻi ki ai hono mafai.

#### 102 Ako

‘E ngofua fakatatau ki he fakangofua ‘e he Kapineti, ki he Minisitā pe ko ha taha te ne fakamafaiʻi ‘e ia, , ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ke fiemaʻu ki ha taha ma‘u laiseni, ke ne ‘oatu,

‘i ha totongi fakapotopoto pe ʻikai ha totongi, ha kakano fakaako, ‘a ia ‘e tu‘utu‘uni

‘e he Minisitā, pe ko ha taha te ne fakamafaiʻi ki ai hono mafai.

#### 103 Fakatu‘utāmaki Fakafokifā Fakafonua

Kuo pau ki ha taha ma‘u laiseni lolotonga ‘o ha vahaʻa taimi fakatu‘utāmaki fakafokifā fakafonua, ke ne fakahoko ‘a ‘ene ngaahi naunau ‘oku ngāue‘aki ki he fakamafola ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fakahoko kakano, ʻikai ha totongi, ke faka‘aonga‘i ‘e he Pule‘anga, pea kuo pau ke ne ‘oatu mo ha kakai taukei ke nau fakahoko ngāue ‘a e ngaahi naunau ko iá pea ke ne fakahoko ha ngaahi ngāue fakahoko kakano.

#### 104 Kakano Fakapolitikale mo fakakikihi‘i ‘uhinga

‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā ‘i ha fakangofua ‘e he Kapineti, ke tu‘utu‘uni, , ki he ngata‘anga mo e founga ‘e lava ai ‘e ha taha ma‘u laiseni ke ne fakahoko ha kakano fakapolitikale pe fakakikihi‘i ‘uhinga.

#### 105 Hia

Kuo pau ko ha taha ma‘u laiseni te ne ʻoatu ha ngaahi ngāue fakahoko kakano ‘oku fepaki mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e Laoʹ ni pe ko hono ngaahi lao fakalahi, pe ko ‘ene laiseni, ‘okuʹ ne fakahoko ha hia pea kuo pau, ‘i ha‘ane halaia, ke ala mo‘ua pa‘anga

‘o ‘ikai laka hake ‘i he $10,000 pe ko e ngāue nofo pōpula ‘o ‘ikai laka hake ‘i he taʻu

‘e 3 pe fakatou‘osi.

VAHE 2 - KAU FAKAHOKO NGᾹUE ‘INITANETI MO E KAU TUKU ATU FAKAHOKO NGᾹUE

#### 106 Ngaahi fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo

(1) ‘E ngofua kihe Potungāue, ‘i haʻane tali ki ha lāunga pe ko haʻane fokotuʻu meʻa foʻou, ke fakatotolo pe ‘oku tuku atu ‘e ha taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ha kakano kuo tapui.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he Potungāue, ‘o kapau, ‘e hili ange ha fakatotolo ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(1), ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Potungāue ko e kakano ‘oku tauhi ‘e ha taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ko e kakano ‘oku tapui, ke ne ‘ave ki he taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ha fakatokanga tohi (fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo) ke fakahinohino ‘a e taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ke fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga pehē ‘oku fiemaʻu ke fakapapauʻi ‘e taʻofi ‘e he taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ‘a hono tuku atu ‘o e kakano.

(3) Kuo pau ki he taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ke faipau ki ha fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo kuo fakahoko ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahoko, ka ‘i ha meʻa ange ke ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 hili ‘a hono maʻu ‘e he taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ‘a e fakatokanga.

(4) Kuo pau ki he taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue takitaha ke ‘i ai ha founga ki hono maʻu mai mo e tali ki ha ngaahi fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo, kau ki ai ‘a hono fokotuʻu ‘o ha taha ‘a ia ‘e ‘ave ki ai ‘e he Potungāue ‘a e ngaahi fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo.

(5) Kuo pau ki he taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala lava hili hano maʻu ‘o ha fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2), ke fakahā ki he taha naʻaʹ ne fakaʻatā ‘a e kakano ‘ikai taau ki he kakai ko e kakano ʻikai taau ‘oku tatali ki he fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo.

(6) Koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o e kupuʹ ni —

(a) “meʻatauhifakamatalafakakomipiuta” ‘oku‘uhingatataumo e

‘uhinga ‘oku hā ‘i he kupu 2 ‘o e Lao ki he Ngaahi Hia Komipiuta 2003;

(b) “kakano ʻikai taau” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki he kakano ‘e ala ke fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2);

(c) “kakano tapui” ‘oku ‘uhinga —

(i) ki ha kakano ‘oku ne maumauʻi ‘a e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘o e kupu 98 ‘o e Lao ki he Ngaahi Fetu’utaki;

(ii) ki ha kakano ‘oku kau ki aiha meʻa‘oku lauʻikovi ‘i heLao ki he Lau’ikovi (Vahe33); pe

(iii) ki ha kakano naʻaʹ ne faʻu ‘a e makatuʻunga ‘o e ngāue ‘i he kupu 8E ‘o e Lao ki he Ngaahi Hia Komipiuta 2003; pea

(d) “fakatokanga ke tuku-hifo” ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ‘a e ‘uhinga ‘oku hā ‘i he kupu- siʻi (2).

#### 107 Meʻa sivi To’o-ki-tu’a

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā ‘i hano ʻoatu ‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue ha ngāue

‘initaneti ki ha kasitomā ‘i he Puleʻanga, ke ne fakahinohino ki he taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ke faʻu ha meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili ‘o ‘atā ki he kau kasitomā.

(2) Kuo pau heʻikai ke tohi moʻua ‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ha kasitomā ki he meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmiliʹ ki ha mahuʻinga ‘oku lahi ange ‘i he fakakātoa ‘o e fakamole ki he taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ki hono maʻu, ʻoatu mo e tauhi ‘o e meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili.

(3) ‘E ngofua ki ha kasitomā ke ne kole ki ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ‘okuʹ ne ‘oatu ‘a e ngāue ‘initaneti ki he kasitomā ko ia ke taʻofi ‘a e meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili ʻi hono ngāueʻaki ki he ngāue ‘initaneti ko ia.

(4) ‘O kapau‘emaʻu‘ehatahafakahoko ngāue‘initaneti ha kole‘i he kupu-siʻi (3) —

(a) kuo pau ki he taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga fakapotopoto ke fakamoʻoniʻi ko e kole naʻe haʻu mei he kasitomā pea ko e kasitomā ko ia naʻe ‘ikai toe siʻi ange ‘i he taʻu 18 ‘i he taimi ‘o e kole; pea

(b) kuo pau ke taʻofi ‘o kapau ‘e fiemalie ‘a e taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ki he ngaahi meʻa ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he palakalafi (a), ‘a e meʻa sivi

‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili ‘oku ngāueʻaki ki he ngāue ‘initaneti totonu pea kuo pau heʻikai tohi moʻua ki he kasitomā ha totongi ‘e laka ange ‘i he ngaahi totongi fakapotopoto ‘a e taha fakahoko ngāue

‘initaneti ki hono taʻofi ‘o e meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili.

(5) Kuo pau ki he kau fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ke nau fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga fakapotopoto kotoa ke fakapapauʻi ko e meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili ‘okuʹ ne fakaʻikaiʻi pe fakangatangata ‘a e hū ki he kakano ‘i he ‘initanetiʹ ‘a ia ‘oku ʻikai fakalao ke maʻu, hū, tufaki pe pulusi ‘i he Ngaahi Lao ‘o e Pule’anga, pea ‘i heʻene fakahoko pehē kuo pau ki he kau fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ke nau fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga fakapotopoto ke fakapapauʻi ‘a e hū ʻikai fakangatangata ki he ngaahi fakamatala moʻoni fakasaienisi, fakafaitoʻo mo emoʻui lelei.

(6) Kuo pau ki he taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu 109, ke

‘ikai ngāueʻaki ha fakamatala ‘a ia —

(a) ‘okuʹ ne maʻu lolotonga hono fakahoko ‘o e meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili ki ha kasitomā; pea

(b) ‘oku felave’ʻi mo ha kakano ‘oku hū ki ai ‘a e kasitomā pe ko e kakano ‘o e ngaahi fetuʻutaki ‘a e kasitomā ko ia, ki ha taumuʻa kehe ange mei hono fakahoko ‘o e meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili maʻae kasitomā ko ia.

(7) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupuʹ ni —

(a) “sivi” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki he koloa pe fokotuʻu kuo ngaohi ke ne fakangatangatapefakaʻikaiʻi‘aehūkihapeesi‘iheopepekohakakano kehe ‘oku ‘atā ‘i he ‘initaneti;

(b) “meʻa sivi ‘oku anga fakakaumeʻa ki he fāmili” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha meʻa sivi kuo fokotuʻu ke ne fakaʻikaiʻi pe fakangatangata ‘a e hū ki he kakano ‘i he ‘initaneti ‘i he meʻa ‘oku ‘ikai fakalao ke maʻu, hū ki ai, tufaki pe pulusi ‘a e kakano ko ia ‘i he Ngaahi Lao ‘o e Pule’anga.

#### 108 Meʻa sivi tuʻupau

(1) ‘EngofuakihePotungāue,‘ihanotuʻutuʻuni,kefakapapauʻihapalani ketaʻofi

‘a e hū ki he ‘ata ʻikai taau ‘o e tamasiʻi ‘a ia ‘oku ‘atā ‘i he ‘initaneti.

(2) Kuo pau ki he taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ke faipau ki ha palani ‘i he kupu- siʻi (1).

(3) Kuo pau ki he Potungāue ‘i hono fakapapauʻi ha palani ‘a ia ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai

‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke fakakau ki ai ‘a e —

(a) ngaahi lelei ‘o e tauʻataina ‘a e fetuʻutaki mo e hū ki he fakamatala ‘i he

‘initaneti;

(b) ngaahi ‘amanaki ‘a e komiunitī mo e ‘ulungaanga faka-Tonga mo e ngaahi ngeia fakafonua;

(c) ko e malava ke fakahoko fakatekinikale ‘a e palani;

(d) ko e ngaahi fakamole mo e ngaahi uesia ki he kau fakahoko ngāue

‘initaneti mo e kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi ‘o hono fakahoko ‘o e palani; mo e

(e) fiemaʻu ke fakapapauʻi ‘oku malava ‘o ‘hā mahino ‘a e palani ʻe ala lava pea ‘oku ‘i ai ‘a e ngaahi feituʻu taau ke fakapapauʻi ko e hū ki he ngaahi

‘ēlia ‘oku ‘ikai ‘i ai ha ‘ata ʻikai taau ‘o e tamasiʻi ‘oku kei hoko atu ‘a e

‘ikai ke fakangatangata.

(4) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahitaumuʻa ‘oe kupuʹ ni, “‘ataʻikaitaau ‘oe tamasiʻi” ‘oku

‘uhinga tatau mo e ‘uhinga ‘oku hā ‘i he kupu 115A ‘o e Lao ki he Ngaahi Hia.

#### 109 Kuo pau ki he kau fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti ke lipooti ‘a e ‘ata ʻikai taau

‘o e tamasiʻi Kapau ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti pe taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue —

(a) ‘okuʹ ne ‘ilo ko e ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue

‘initaneti pe taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ‘oku lava ‘o ngāueʻaki ke hū ki ha meʻa ‘i ha peesi ‘i he ope pau, uepisaiti pe tuʻasila ki he founga fakaʻinitaneti

‘a ia ‘oku ‘i ai ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga ‘oku pehē ai ‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue

‘initaneti pe taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ke tui fakapotopoto ko e ‘ata ʻikai taau ‘o e tamasiʻi; pe

(b) ‘oku faleʻi ‘o ha peesi ‘i he ope, uepisaiti pe tuʻasila founga fakaʻinitaneti ‘a ia ʻe ngofua ke tauhi ai ha ‘ata ʻikai taau ‘o e tamasiʻi pe fakaʻatā ki he kakai, kuo pau ko e kau tuku atu fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti pe taha fakahoko ngāue

‘initaneti, ‘i loto ‘i ha vahaʻa taimi fakapotopoto ‘o hono ‘iloʻi pe ko hono fakahoko ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fakaikiiki, ke lipooti ‘a e ngaahi fakaikiiki ko ia ki he Polisi Tonga.

## KONGA X - TU‘UTU‘UNI FAKA‘EKONŌMIKA

VAHE I - FOUNGA FE‘AU‘AUHI FAKALUKUFUA VAHESIʻI A - FAKALUKUFUA 110 ʻUhingaʻi Lea Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vaheʹ ni —

(a) "taha maʻu laiseni" ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha taha pe kautaha ‘a ia ‘oku ngāueʻaki ki ai ‘ a e Vaheʹ ni ‘o fakatatau mo e kupu 111 pea fakakau ‘a e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi kautaha felaveʻi mo e taha pe kautaha ;

(b) "ngāue fetuʻutaki" ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha ngāue ‘okuʹ ne fakatahatahaʻi ‘a e ngaahi ngāue lalahi pe tekinolosia, ko hono maʻu, fakahoko ngāue pe ko hono ngāueʻaki ‘a ia ‘oku fiemaʻu ha laiseni kuo foaki ‘i he Laoʹ ni ;

(c) "maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki" ‘oku ‘uhinga, ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu 112, ha maketi ‘i he lotoʻi Puleʻanga ki hono ʻoatu pe maʻu mai ‘o ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ;

(d) "kautahafekauʻaki"‘okuʹnemaʻu‘ae‘uhinga‘okuhā‘iheLaokiheNgaahi Kautaha 1995 ;

(e) "taha totonu" ‘oku kau ki ai ha taha pe kalasi ‘o ha niʻihi ; mo e

(f) "koloa pe ngāue totonu" ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha ngāue fetuʻutaki, pe ko ha koloa pe ngāue ‘oku ngāueʻaki ‘o fekauʻaki mo e ngāue fetuʻutaki.

#### 111 Ngāueʻaki ‘o e Vaheʹ ni

Ko e Vaheʹ ni ‘e ngāueʻaki ki ha —

(a) taha maʻu laiseni pe taha kuo foaki ki ai ha laiseni ‘o fakatatau mo e Laoʹ ni ; mo

(b) ha taha pe kalasi ‘o ha niʻihi ‘oku nau kau ‘i he ngāue ‘oku —

(i) fiemaʻu ki ai ha laiseni ‘o fakatatau mo e Lao ; pe

(ii) ‘e ala fiemaʻu ha laiseni ‘o hangē ko e ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (a) ka ki ha —

(A) fakaʻatā mei he fiemaʻu ke maʻu ha laiseni kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘o fakatatau mo e Lao ; pe

(B) tuʻutuʻuni mei he Minisitā ki he taha pe kalasi ‘o e niʻihi ‘oku fakaʻatā mei he fiemaʻu ke maʻu ha laiseni ‘i he Laoʹ ni.

#### 112 Ngaahi maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki

(1) Kuo pau ki he ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vaheʹ ni, ko e ‘uhingaʻi lea ‘o e maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ki ha ngāue fetuʻutaki pau pe ko ha koloa pe ngāue ‘oku ngāueʻaki ‘i heʻene felaveʻi mo ha koloa pe ngāue totonu (ngāue ala ngāueʻaki) ke kau ki ai —

(a) ngāue ala ngāueʻaki ; mo e

(b) kotoa‘oengaahikoloapengaahingāue,‘aia‘okumalavakefakafetongi

‘aki fakapotopoto ‘a engāue ala ngāueʻaki , kae fakakau ke fakakaukauʻi

‘a e —

(i) natula mo e fatongia ‘o e ngāue ala ngāueʻaki ;

(ii) ngaahi ‘ēlia fakasiokalafi ‘a ia ‘oku ʻoatu pe maʻu ai ‘a e ngāue ala ngāueʻaki; mo e

(iii) niʻihi totongi ke maʻu pe ʻoatu ‘a e ngāue ‘oku ala ngāueʻaki.

(2) Kuo pau ki he ngaahi meʻa ni ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (3) pea ‘ikai fakangatangata ki he kupu-siʻi (1), ke fakakaukauʻi takitaha ko e ngaahi maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vaheʹ ni —

(a) tamateʻi ‘o e talanoa telefoni ‘i he leʻo fakalukufua ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he Puleʻanga ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ki ha kau fakahoko ngāue netiueka kehe pe kau fakahoko ngāue ; mo e

(b) hū fakalotofonua fakalukufua ‘i he Puleʻanga.

(3) ‘EngofuakiheSinoPulekoeʻuhikoengaahitaumuʻa‘oeVaheʹni,‘ofakakau

‘a e ngaahi fiemaʻu ‘o e kupu-siʻi (1), ke fakapapauʻi, ‘i ha tuʻutuʻuni, ko ha —

(a) kuo pau ki ha maketi pau ke lau ko ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ; pe

(b) kuo pau ko e maketi ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2)(a), (2)(b) pe (3)(a) heʻikai ke lau ko ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa ‘i haʻane fakahoko ‘o ha fakahā ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(3), ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

(5) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4) pe —

(a) ʻi heʻene fuofua fokotuʻu; pe

(b) kapau ko e Sino Pule —

(i) kuo ne maʻu ha tohi kole mei ha taha ‘o kole ‘a e Sino Pule ke fakahoko ha alea pehē ; pea

(ii) ‘okuʹ ne fakapapauʻi ke fakahoko ha alea hili haʻane fakakau ki he —

(A) pe ko e kole ‘i he palakalafi-siʻi (i) naʻe fakahoko ‘i he faitotonu ; mo

(B) ha meʻa kehe ‘okuʹ ne pehē ‘oku totonu.

(6) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kapau ʻe maʻu ha tohi kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (5)(b)(i), ki he Sino Pule ke —

(a) ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10, ke pulusi ‘a e fakamatala ‘i heʻene uepisaiti

‘o fakahā kuo ne maʻu ‘a e kole ; pea

(b) kapau ‘e tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Sino Pule heʻikai ke fakahoko ha alea, ke pulusi ha fakamatala ‘o fakahā ‘a ‘ene tuʻutuʻuni mo e ngaahi ‘uhinga ki he tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 113 Mafai Lahi ki he Maketi

(1) Kuo pau ke fakakaukauʻi koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vaheʹ ni, ‘i hono fakapapauʻi pe ‘oku ‘i ai ha mafai lahi ki he maketi ‘o ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ;

(a) ‘a e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘o ha laiseni ‘i he Laoʹ ni ;

(b) ko e ngataʻanga ‘a ia ‘e fakangata mei ai ‘a e ʻulungaanga ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ‘aki —

(i) ha kau feʻauʻauhi, pe malava ko ha kau feʻauʻauhi, ‘o e taha maʻu laiseni ; pe

(ii) ha taha ‘oku hanga ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘o —

(A) ʻoatu ki ai ‘a e ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngāue ke; pe

(B) maʻu mai ha ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngāue mei ;

(c) ko e ‘inasi ‘o e taha maʻu laiseni mei he maketi ‘i he maketi ko ia pe ko ha maketi ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ;

(d) ‘a e mafai ‘oku maʻu mei ha aleapau, fokotuʻutuʻu pe femahinoʻaki kuo hū ki ai ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni mo e Sino Pule, ko e Puleʻanga ‘o Tonga, ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha pe ko ha taha kehe ; pea

(e) pe‘oku‘iai hahū‘aetahamaʻulaiseni kihatekinolosiapengaahingāue lalahi ‘a ia ‘oku ‘ikai ‘atā ki ha toe taha kehe.

(2) Kuo pau ko e tohi hokohoko ‘o e ngaahi meʻa ‘e fakakau ki hono fakakaukauʻi

‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ‘oku ‘ikai ke ne taʻofi ‘a hono fakakaukauʻi ‘o ha meʻa kehe

‘oku totonu ki hono fakapapauʻi pe ‘oku ‘i ai ha mafailahi ki he maketi ‘o ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

(3) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘i he ‘ikai ke ‘i ai ha fakamoʻoni feʻunga ki he maumau, kuo pau ke pehē ‘oku ‘i ai ‘a e mafai lahi ki he maketi ‘o ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘o kapau ko e paʻanga hū mai kimuʻa ‘i he tukuhau ‘oku maʻu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni mei he ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngāue

‘oku ʻoatu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ko ia ‘oku lahi ange pe tatau ki he peseti ‘e 60 ‘o e fakakātoa ‘o e paʻanga hū mai kuo maʻu ‘e he kotoa ‘o e kau mēmipa ‘o e maketi ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki totonu.

(4) ‘E ‘ikai ha meʻa ‘i he kupuʹ ni te ne taʻofi hano faʻu ko ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘e 2 pe lahi ange ‘oku nau maʻu ‘a e mafai lahi ki he maketi ‘i loto ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki tatau.

(5) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupuʹ ni —

(a) pa’anga hū mai kimuʻa ‘i he tukuhau ‘o ha taha maʻu laiseni kuo pau ke fikaʻi ko ha fakakātoa ‘o e mahuʻinga fakamaketi ‘o e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngāue kuo ʻoatu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki totonu ; mo e

(b) pa’anga hū mai fakakātoa ‘oku maʻu ‘e he kotoa ‘o e kau mēmipa maketi ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki totonu kuo pau ke fikaʻi ko e fakakātoa ‘o e mahuʻinga fakamaketi ‘o e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngāue kuo ʻoatu ‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki totonu.

#### 114 Ngaahi fakahinohino ki he feʻauʻauhi

‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke nefaʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘okuʹ ne fakakaukauʻi ʻokufiemaʻupe‘aongakihepe‘iheʻenefelaveʻimoefakahoko‘ohonongaahingāue

‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

VAHESI’I B – NGAAHI TAPUI

#### 115 Tapui Fakalukufua ‘o e fakafaingataʻaʻia ‘o e feʻauʻauhi

Kuo pau heʻikai ke kau ha taha maʻu laiseni ki ha ngāue ‘a ia ‘oku ‘i ai ‘ene taumuʻa pe ‘oku ‘i ai pe ‘oku ngalingali ke ‘iai ha ola‘oku ne fakasiʻisiʻi ‘o lahi ‘a e feʻauʻauhi

‘i he maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki.

#### 116 Ngaahi aleapau ke ‘oua ‘e fakafaingataʻaʻia

(1) ‘E maumauʻi ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e Vaheʹ ni ‘o kapau ‘e hū ki ha pe tokoniʻi ha aleapau, fokotuʻutuʻu pe femahinoʻaki mo ha taha pe ha kau maʻu laiseni tokolahi ange ‘oku ‘i ai ‘enau taumuʻa, pe ‘oku ‘i ai pe ‘oku ngalingali ke ne fakaʻaiʻai ‘a e —

(a) monomono, puleʻi pe tauhi maʻu ‘o e ngaahi mahuʻinga ki he, pe ‘o ha fakamaʻamaʻa, meʻaʻofa, moʻua pe totongi fakafoki ki he, ha koloa pe ngāue felaveʻi;

(b) vahevahe ‘i he vahaʻa ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni, pe ko ha taha ‘ia kinautolu;

(i) ‘a e kau kasitomā pe kakai ‘oku ngalingali ‘e hoko ko ha kau kasitomā;

(ii) ‘a e kau tuʻuaki koloa pe kakai ‘oku ngalingali ‘e hoko ko ha kau tuʻuaki koloa;

(iii) ‘a e ngaahi ʻēlia fakasiokalafi, ‘o ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki; pe

(c) taʻofi, fakangatangata pe puleʻi —

(i) ‘a hono tuku atu pe maʻu mai ‘o e koloa pe ngāue felaveʻi; pe

(ii) ‘a e hū ki he koloa pe ngāue felaveʻi, ‘a ha taha pe kalasi ‘o ha kakai;

(d) fakapapauʻi ‘oku, ‘i heʻene felaveʻi mo ha kole ki ha fokotuʻu pe talamahuʻinga mei ha taha ki hono ʻoatu pe maʻu mai ‘o ha koloa pe ngāue felaveʻi, ko ha taha pe tokolahi ange ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni ko ha faʻahi ‘e taha ki ha aleapau, fokotuʻutu’u pe femahinoʻaki —

(i) ‘oku ‘ikai ke ne fakahū atu ha fokotuʻu, pe ‘oku ‘ikai ke ne fakahū atu ha fokotuʻu ‘a ia ‘oku ngalingali ke ola lelei; pe

(ii) ‘oku fakahū atu ha fokotuʻu ‘a ia ‘oku ngalingali ke ola lelei ange

‘i ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a ia ko ha faʻahi ‘e taha ki he aleapau, fokotuʻutuʻu pe femahinoʻaki.

(2) Ko e kupu-siʻi (1) ‘oku ‘ikai ngāueʻaki ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku hū ki ha, pe tokoni ki ha, tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘oku —

(a) ‘i ha aleapau fakahoko fakataha ha ngāue (joint venture) ‘i he vahaʻa ‘o ha kau maʻu laiseni ‘e taha pe tokolahi ange ‘a ia ‘okuʹ ne puleʻi ‘a e fakahoko fakataha ha ngāue (joint venture) ki hono ʻoatu pe maʻu mai ‘o ha koloa pe ngāue totonu; mo e

(b) fiemaʻu ki he ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e fakahoko fakataha ha ngāue (joint venture) ‘a ia ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he palakalafi (2)(a).

#### 117 Tapui ‘o e ngaahi ngāue ‘oku fakafaingataʻaʻia ki he feʻauʻauhi

(1) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ʻikai ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ‘i he tuʻunga ko e taha ‘oku ne maʻu ‘a e koloa pe ngāue totonu kuo pau ke ne loto ki he, pe kuo pau —

(a) ke maʻu ha koloa pe ngāue kehe mei ha taha kehe; pe

(b) ‘e ‘ikai ke maʻu ha koloa pe ngāue mei ha taha kehe.

(2) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ʻoatu ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ki ha taha totonu ‘i he tuʻunga ko e taha totonu kuo pau ke ʻikai —

(a) ke ne ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ki ha taha pe kalasi ‘o ha niʻihi; pe

(b) ke maʻu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu mei ha taha pe kalasi ‘o ha niʻihi.

(3) Kuo pau ki he taha maʻu laiseni ke ʻikai tali ke ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ki ha taha totonu ‘aki ‘a e ‘uhinga ko e taha totonu —

(a) kuo ne ʻoatu pe maʻu mai; pe

(b) kuo ‘ikai ke loto ke ʻoatu pe maʻu mai, ha ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngāue mei ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha.

(4) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ʻikai ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu, ki ha taha mo e fiemaʻu kuo pau heʻikai ke toe ʻoatu ‘e he taha ‘a e koloa pe ngāue totonu ko ia ‘i ha mahuʻinga —

(a) ‘o kehe mei he; pe

(b) maʻulalo ange ‘i he, mahuʻinga kuo fakamahinopau ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni.

(5) Kuo pau ʻe hilifaki ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ha fiemaʻu ki ha taha ‘a ia ‘oku maumauʻi ai ‘a e kupu-siʻi (4) ‘o kapau te ne —

(a) fakaʻaiʻai, pe feinga ke fakaʻaiʻai, ha taha ‘aki ‘a e founga fakakomesiale pe fakaʻaiʻai kehe;

(b) fakahoko ke ʻilo ‘e fakatatali ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘a hono ʻoatu ‘o ha koloa pe ngāue ‘o kapau ‘e ‘ikai loto ki ai ha taha; pe

(c) kuo pau ke ne fakahoko ke ʻilo ‘e faingataʻaʻia ha taha ‘i ha mole fakakomesiale ‘o kapau heʻikai ke loto ‘a e taha, ke ‘ikai toe ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ‘i ha mahuʻinga ‘a ia ‘oku —

(d) kehe mei he; pe

(e) maʻulalo ange,

‘i ha mahuʻinga kuo fakamahinopau ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni.

(6) ‘Oku ‘ikai ke maumauʻi ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e kupu-siʻi (1), (2), (3), pe

(4)‘ofekauʻakimohangāue‘okapau‘elava‘ehetahamaʻulaiseni‘ofokotuʻu ko e ngāue totonu naʻe ‘ikai hano taumuʻa, pe tokoni ki he pe ngalingali ke tokoni ki hono fakasiʻisiʻi lahi ‘o e feʻauʻauhi ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki.

#### 118 Tapui ‘a hono ngāue hala ‘aki ‘o e mafai lahi ki he maketi

(1) Kuo pau ko ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku ‘i ai hano mafai lahi ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki heʻikai ke ne ngāueʻaki ‘a e mafai lahi ki he maketi ko ia ki he taumuʻa ‘o hono —

(a) tamateʻi pe fakahoko ha maumau lahi ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha ‘i he maketi ko ia, pe ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki;

(b) taʻofi ‘a e hū ‘o ha taha kehe ki he maketi ko ia pe ko ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki kehe;

(c) taʻotaʻofi ki ha taha maʻu laiseni kehe mei heʻene kau ‘i ha ngāue feʻauʻauhi ‘i he maketi ko ia pe ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki kehe.

(2) ‘E ngoufa ‘i hano fakapapauʻi pe kuo maumauʻi ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e kupu-siʻi (1), ke fakakau ha ngāue naʻe kau ki ai ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni —

(a) ki honoʻoatu‘o ha koloa pengāue totonu‘i ha mahuʻinga ‘aia‘oku tatau ki he pe siʻisiʻi ange ‘i he fakamole ki he tahamaʻu laiseni ‘o hono ʻoatu

‘o e koloa pe ngāue totonu ‘i ha vahaʻa taimi tuʻumaʻu hokohoko;

(b) ‘i heʻene ʻikai tali ke ʻoatu ki ha taha ‘a e koloa pe ngāue totonu ‘a ia

‘oku mahuʻinga ki hono ʻoatu ‘o ha koloa pe ngāue totonu ‘e taha ‘i he maketi tatau pe ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki kehe;

(c) ‘i hono ʻoatu ki ha taha ‘a e koloa pe ngāue totonu ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he palakalafi (2)(b) ‘i he ngaahi teemi ‘a ia te ne taʻofi ‘a e taha mei hono ʻoatu‘ohakoloapengāue‘etaha‘ihemaketitataupe‘ihamaketingāue fetuʻutaki ‘e taha ‘i ha mahuʻinga feʻauʻauhi; pe

(d) ‘i heʻene ‘ikai tali ke ʻoatu ‘i ha vahaʻa taimi feʻunga ‘a e ngaahi fakamatala fakatekinikale pehē ‘a ia ‘oku fiemaʻu ki ha taha ke ʻoatu ha koloa pe ngāue totonu.

(3) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘i hano tuʻutuʻuni, ʻuhingaʻi lea “vahaʻa taimi hokohoko” ki he ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu-siʻi (2).

#### 119 Tapui ‘o e filifilimanako

(1) Kuo pau ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (3), ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku ‘i ai hano mafai lahi ki he maketi ‘i ha maketi ngāue fetuʻutaki ke ‘ikai, ‘i hono ‘oatu ‘o ha koloa pe ngāue totonu (ʻoatu totonu), ke ʻoatu ‘a e ʻoatu totonu ki ha taha

‘i he ngaahi teemi ‘oku ʻikai fuʻu manakoa ‘i he ngaahi teemi ‘a ia kuo fakahoko ‘aki ‘a e ʻoatu totonu tatau ki ha taha kehe.

(2) ‘E ngofua koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o hono fakapapauʻi pe kuo maumauʻi

‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e kupu-siʻi (1), ke fakakau ‘a e ngaahi teemi ko eni ‘a ia kuo fakahoko ai ‘a e ʻoatu totonu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ki ha taha —

(a) ha totongi pe tohi moʻua ki he ʻoatu totonu ;

(b) ko e ngaahi anga fakahoko ngāue ‘o e ʻoatu totonu ;

(c) ko e tauhi taimi ʻa ia ‘oku fakahoko ai ‘a e‘oatu totonu ; mo

(d) ha ngaahi tuʻunga pe ngaahi fakangatangata ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ai ‘a e ʻoatu totonu ki ha taha ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni.

(3) Heʻikai ha meʻa ‘i he kupuʹ ni te ne taʻofi ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku ‘i ai haʻane mafai lahi ki he maketi mei haʻane fakahoko ha ʻoatu totonu ki ha taha pau ‘i he ngaahi teemi ‘oku kehe mei he meʻa ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ‘aki ‘a e ‘oatu totonu ki ha taha kehe ‘i he ngaahi tūkunga ‘a ia ko e ngaahi faikehekeheʹ ko e ola ia ‘o e fakaʻatā fakapotopoto mei he taha maʻu laiseni ki he —

(a) faikehekehe ‘i he lahi ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ai ‘a e ʻoatu totonu;

(b) faikehekehe ‘i he ngaahi ivi fakamafola ‘oku fiemaʻu ki he ‘oatu totonu;

(c) faikehekehe ‘i he tuʻuʻanga fakasiokalafi ‘oku fakahoko ki ai ‘a e ʻoatu totonu;

(d) vahaʻa taimi ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ai ‘a e ʻoatu totonu ; pe

(e) ha ngaahi faikehekehe ‘i he ngaahi ʻulungaanga fakahoko ngāue ‘o e ʻoatu totonu .

#### 120 Ngaahi fakamāʻopoʻopo

(1) Kuopaukihetahamaʻulaisenike‘ikai,fakahangatonupeʻikai fakahangatonu, ke ne maʻu ha totonu (totonu kuo tapui) ‘i ha —

(a) taha maʻu laiseni kehe; pe

(b) ngaahi koloa ‘a ha taha maʻu laiseni kehe, ‘o ‘ikai ‘i ai ʻa e tohi fakangofua kimuʻa ʻa e Sino Pule.

(2) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu-siʻi (1), ko ha totonu kuo tapui ‘oku kau ki ai ha totonu fakalao pe lelei kuo maʻu ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘okuʹ ne fakakakato ‘a e ngaahi fiemaʻu ‘o e kupu-siʻi (1)(a) pe (1)(b).

(3) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha maʻu laiseni pe ko ha taha ‘oku ngāue maʻae taha maʻu laiseni ke ne fakahoko ha tohi kole ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakahoko ‘a ‘ene tohi ngofua ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke maʻu ha totonu kuo tapui.

(4) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala kuo pau ke fakakau ki ha tohi kole koeʻuhi ke lau ko ha tohi kole

‘oku fakalao ki ha tohi ngofua ‘o fakatatau mo e kupu-siʻi (3).

(5) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono maʻu ‘o ha tohi kole ‘oku fakalao, ke ne fakahoko ‘a ‘ene tohi ngofua ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ne maʻu ha totonu kuo tapui tukukehe ‘o kapau ‘oku tui ‘a e Sino Pule ko hono maʻu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e totonu kuo tapui —

(a) kuo pau ke ‘i ai ‘a e tokoni ki ai; pe

(b) ‘oku ngalingali lahi ‘a ‘ene uesia, ‘o hono fakasiʻisiʻi lahi ‘o e feʻauʻauhi

‘i ha maketi ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki.

(6) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(4), ke fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

#### 121 Fakamafai

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘o fakatatau ki he Vaheʹ ni, ‘i ha tohi kole ‘a ha taha maʻu laiseni, ke foaki ‘a e fakanmafai ki he taha maʻu laiseni ‘o fekauʻaki mo ha ngāue ‘a ia pe ‘e malava ke maumauʻi ai ‘a e Vaheʹ ni, ‘o kehe mei ha ngāue

‘a ia pe ‘e malava ke maumauʻi ai ‘a e kupu 118(1) pe kupu 120.

(2) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ʻo‘ikai ke ne maumauʻi ‘a e Vaheʹ ni ‘o fekauʻaki mo ha ngāue ‘a ia ko e kaveinga ia ‘o ha fakamafai kuo ‘osi foaki ki he taha maʻu laiseni ‘o fakatatau ki he kupuʹ ni.

(3) Kuo pau ki ha tohi kole ki ha fakamafai ‘o fakatatau mo e kupuʹ ni ke —

(a) fakahū atu ki he Sino Pule ‘i he founga pea ‘i ha foomu;

(b) ke fakahū fakataha ‘aki ‘a e ngaahi tohi ngāue pehē mo e fakamatala poupou; mo

(c) fakahū fakataha ‘aki ‘a e totongi totonu (‘o kapau ‘e ‘i ai),

‘o hangē ko hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ‘o fakatatau mo e kupu-siʻi (4).

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o fakamahinopau ‘a e founga ke muimuiʻi ki hono fakapapauʻi ‘o ha tohi kole ki ha fakamafai ‘o fakatatau mo e kupuʹ ni.

(5) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa pea ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(4), ke fakahoko ha alea ‘i he kupu 23.

(6) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke tauhi ha lesisita ‘o e kotoa ‘o e —

(a) ngaahi tohi kole ki he ngaahi fakamafai kuo maʻu;

(b) ngaahi fakamafai kuo foaki; mo e

(c) ngaahi fakamafai kuo fakataʻeʻaongaʻi,

‘e he Sino Pule.

(7) Kuo pau ke ‘ikaifoaki ‘e he Sino Pule ha tohi kole ki ha fakamafai kuo fakahū atu ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘o fakatatau mo e kupuʹ ni tukukehe ‘oka fiemalie

‘a e Sino Pule, ‘i he ngaahi tūkunga kotoa, ko e lelei ‘a e kakai ke maʻu mei he ngāue ‘a ia ko e kaveinga ‘o e tohi kole fakamafai ‘oku ngalingali ke lahi ange

‘i he ngaahi nunuʻa ki he kakai ‘a ia ‘oku ngalingali ke maʻu mei he ola ko ia.

(8) Kuo pau ke kamata ha fakamafai kuo foaki ‘e he Sino Pule ‘o fakatatau mo e kupuʹ ni ‘i he ‘aho kuo fakamahinopau ‘i he fakamafai pea ke hokohoko atu ‘i ha vahaʻa taimi ‘o e —

(a) taʻu ‘e 2; pe

(b) ki ha vahaʻa taimi ‘oku siʻi hifo ‘i he taʻu ‘e 2 ‘o hangē ‘e ala tuʻutuʻuni

‘e he Sino Pule ‘i heʻene loto ki ai.

(9) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule lolotonga ‘a e vahaʻa taimi ‘oku ngāueʻaki ai ha fakamafai, ke ne liliu ‘a e fakamafai —

(a) ‘i ha tohi kole ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ‘a e fakamafai; pea

(b) kapau ‘oku tui ‘a e Sino Pule ‘oku taau ke fakahoko pehē, ‘i hano fakakakauʻi ‘o e ngaahi meʻa ‘i he kupu-siʻi (7) mo ha ngaahi meʻa kehe felaveʻi mo ia.

(10) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule lolotonga ‘a e vahaʻa taimi ‘oku ngāueʻaki ai ha fakamafai, ke fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ha fakamafai ‘o kapau ‘oku tui ‘a e Sino Pule —

(a) naʻe foaki ‘a e fakamafai ‘omakatuʻunga ‘i he fakamoʻoni pe fakamatala

‘a ia naʻe loi pe takihala ‘i ha founga felaveʻi;

(b) ko ha tuʻunga ‘a ia na’e fakahā ʻaki ‘a e fakamafai ke foaki naʻe ‘ikai, pe

‘oku ‘ikai toe, faipau mo e; pe

(c) kuo ‘i ai ha liliu mahuʻinga ‘i he ngaahi tūkunga naʻaʹ ne tataki ai ʻa e Sino Pule ki hono foaki ‘o e fakamafai.

(11) ‘Oku ‘ikai ha meʻa ‘i he kupuʹ ni te ne taʻofi ha taha maʻu laiseni mei haʻane kole ki ha fakamafai‘o ha ngāue ‘a ia ko ha tefito ‘o ha fakamafai ‘a ia —

(a) kuo ‘osi hono taimi; pe

(b) kuo fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘o fakatatau mo e kupu-siʻi (10).

VAHE 2 - HŪ KI HE NGAAHI NGᾹUE KUO TUʻUTUʻUNI VAHESIʻI A – NGAAHI NGᾹUE KUO TUʻUTUʻUNI

#### 122 Ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vahe 2

Ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vahe 2 —

(a) ke fakalakalaka ‘a e ola ‘o e feʻauʻauhi ‘i he ngaahi maketi maʻae ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ki he lelei ‘oku tolonga ma‘ae kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘i loto ‘i Tonga ‘aki hono ʻoatu ‘o e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ʻoatu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki pau ‘i he vahaʻa ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni ;

(b) ke fakalakalaka ‘a e ngāueʻaki totonu fakaʻekonomika ‘o e, pea mo e ‘inivesi totonu fakaʻekonomika ‘i he, ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki ‘a ia ‘oku ala ʻoatu mei ai ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ; mo

(c) fakahoko ‘a e hū ‘i ha tuʻunga fakapotopoto mo ‘ikai filifilimanako mo felālāveʻi faka-e-mole ki he kau maʻu laiseni, pea mo fakalakalaka ‘a e fehokotakiʻaki ngataʻanga-ki he-ngataʻanga ‘o e ngaahi netiueka fetuʻutaki kehekehe.

#### 123 Fakaʻekeʻeke Fakapuleʻanga ki he ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga ki he pe —

(a) kuo pau ke tuʻutuʻuni ha ngāue ala ngaueʻaki ‘i he kupu 125 (kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi teemi taau ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni ko iaʹ) ; pe

(b) kuo pau ko ha tuʻutuʻuni lolotonga ‘i he kupu 125 pe 127 ‘oku totonu ke fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe liliu (kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi teemi taau ‘o e fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe liliu).

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) pe —

(a) ko haʻane fokotuʻu meʻa foʻou ; pe

(b) kapau ko e Sino Pule —

(i) kuo ne maʻu ha tohi kole mei ha taha ‘o kole ‘a e Sino Pule ke fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga pehē ; pea

(ii) kuo ne tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga hili

‘a hono fakakau ‘a e —

(A) ngaahi taumuʻa ‘i he kupu 122 ;

(B) kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (i) pe naʻe fakahoko ‘i he faitotonu ; mo

(C) ha meʻa kehe ‘okuʹ ne pehē ‘oku totonu.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ‘i hano maʻu ‘e he Sino Pule ha tohi kole ‘i he kupu- siʻi (2)(b)(i), ke —

(a) pulusi ha fakamatala ‘i heʻene uepisaiti ‘o fakahā kuo ne maʻu ‘a e kole,

‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 ; mo

(b) pulusi ha fakamatala ‘o fakahā ‘ene tuʻutuʻuni pea mo e ngaahi ‘uhinga ki he tuʻutuʻuni, ‘o kapau ‘e fakakaukau ‘a e Sino Pule ke ‘ikai fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga.

(4) Kuopauki heSino Pulekefakahoko hafakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga ‘ihe kupu- siʻi (1) ‘i hano maʻu ‘o ha tohi fakahinohino mei he Minisitā ‘o fakahinohino

‘a e Sino Pule ke fakahoko pehē.

(5) Hili hano fakapapauʻi ke fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga ‘i he kupu- siʻi (2) pe maʻu ‘o ha tohi fakahinohino ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4), ko e Sino Pule —

(a) kuo pau ke pulusi ha fakatokanga ki he —

(i) meʻa ‘okuʹ ne fakahoko ‘a e fakaʻekeʻeke ;

(ii) vahaʻa taimi ‘a ia ‘e lolotonga fakahoko ai ‘a e fakaʻekeʻeke ;

(iii) natula ‘o e meʻa ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakaʻekeʻeke ;

(iv) vahaʻa taimi, pea mo e founga ‘a ia, ‘e ala fakahoko ai ‘e he kau mēmipa‘oekakaiʹhangaahifokotuʻukiheSinoPule‘ofekauʻaki mo e meʻa ko ia ;

(v) ngaahi meʻa ‘a ia ‘e fiemaʻu ʻe he Sino Pule ke ngāue ki ai ; mo e

(vi) tuʻasila pe ngaahi tuʻasila ‘a ia ‘e tufa ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu.

(b) ‘e ngofua ke ne pulusi ha pepa fealea’aki ‘oku —

(i) fakaʻilonga ai ‘a e ngaahi meʻa (issues) ‘a ia, ‘i he fakakaukau ‘a e Sino Pule, ‘oku totonu ki he meʻa ko ia ; mo

(ii) tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala puipuituʻa fekauʻaki, mo e fakamatalaʻi

‘o e, ngaahi meʻa (issues) ko ia koeʻuhi ko e pehē ‘e he Sino Pule

‘oku taau.

(c) kuo pau ke ne fakakaukauʻi ‘a e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a ia kuo ‘osi maʻu ‘i loto ‘i he vahaʻa taimi, pea ‘oku lahi ʻi he foomu,kuo fakahā ‘i he kupu si’i (5)(a)(iv) ; mo e

(d) kuo pau hili hono fakakaukauʻi ‘a e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a ia ‘oku fakafiemalie ‘a e kupu-siʻi 5(c), ke pulusi ‘a e lipooti ‘o fakahā ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu kuo maʻu ko e ola ‘o e fakaʻekeʻeke mo ‘ave ha tatau ‘o e lipooti ki he Minisitā.

(6) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahitaumuʻa ‘oe kupu-siʻi (5), ko e vahaʻataimi ‘aia‘engofua ki he kau mēmipa ‘o e kakai ke fakahoko ha ngaahi fokotuʻu ki he Sino Pule kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai toe siʻi hifo ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20.

#### 124 Fokotuʻu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘i ha fakatokanga ʻi he tohi, ke ne fokotuʻu ki he Minisitā ke tuʻutuʻuni ha ngāue ala ngāueʻaki pau pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe liliu ha tuʻutuʻuni lolotonga ‘o kapau —

(a) kuo ‘osi fakahoko ‘e he Sino Pule ha fakaʻekeʻeke fakapuleʻanga ‘i he kupu 123 ‘o fekauʻaki mo e fokotuʻu ke fakahoko, fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe liliu ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni (‘i hono fiemaʻu) ;

(b) kuo faipau ‘a e Sino Pule ki he ngaahi fiemaʻu ‘o e kupu 123 ; pea

(c) ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Sino Pule ko hono faʻu, fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe liliu ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni (‘i hono fiemaʻu) ‘okuʹ ne fakafiemalie ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono fakakaukauʻi pe ‘oku fakatatau ‘a hono faʻu pe liliu ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni mo e makatuʻunga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1)(c), ke fakakau pe ko hono faʻu pe liliu (‘i hono fiemaʻu) ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku taau ke ne lavaʻi ‘a e mafai lahi ki he maketi ‘o ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha pe lahi ange ‘i ha maketi ki ha ngāue ala ngāueʻaki.

(3) Kuo pau ki ha fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ke fakahoko ‘i loto ‘i he mahina ‘e 6

‘o hono pulusi ‘o e lipooti ‘i he kupu 123(5)(d).

(4) Kuo pau ke fakamahinopau ‘i ha fokotuʻu ke faʻu pe liliu ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ‘a e ngaahi teemi ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni (kau ki ai ‘a e vahaʻa taimi ‘a ia

‘e tuʻutuʻuni ai ko ha ngāue ala ngāueʻaki).

#### 125 Tuʻutuʻuni fakaminisitā ‘o ha ngāue ala ngāueʻaki

(1) Kuo pau ki heMinisitā‘i hano maʻu ‘o ha fokotuʻutuʻutuʻuni mei he SinoPule,

‘o ‘ikai kimuʻa ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 hili ‘a hono maʻu ‘o e fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni, pe —

(a) ‘aki hono tuʻutuʻuni, ke tali ‘a e fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni pea (‘i hono fiemaʻu) —

(i) ke fakamahinopau ‘a e ngāue taau ala ngaue’aki ke hoko ko ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni i he ngaahi teemi kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu 124 ;

(ii) ke liliu ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni lolotonga ‘i he ngaahi teemi kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu 124 ; pe

(iii) fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni lolotonga ; pe

(b) ʻikai tali ‘a e fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni pea ‘ikai ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e ngāue ala ngaueʻaki pe liliu pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni lolotonga (ʻi hono fiemaʻu).

(2) ‘I hano fakahoko ‘o ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ko e Minisitā —

(a) kuo pau ke ne fakakau ‘a e makatuʻunga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni pea mo e fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni, kau ki ai ha lipooti ‘i he kupu 123(5)(d) ; pea

(b) ‘engofuakefakakaukihengaahimeʻakehepehē‘aia‘okufakakaukauʻi

‘e he Minisitā ‘oku totonu, ka kuo pau ke fakaʻilongaʻi ‘e he Minisitā ‘a e ngaahi meʻa kehe ko ia ‘i he tuʻutuʻuni kuo pulusi ‘e he Minisitā ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4).

(3) Kapau heʻikai ki he Minisitā, ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 40 hili hono maʻu ‘o ha fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni, ke ne fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tali pe ʻikai tali ‘a e fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni, pea ‘e lau leva ko e Minisitā, ‘i he ngataʻanga ‘o e vahaʻa taimi ko e ‘aho ngaue ‘e 40 ko ia, kuo ne tali ‘a e fokotuʻu ko ia pea kuo faʻu ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Minisitā ke ne fanongonongo ki he Sino Pule ‘i he tohi ‘o ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala lava hili hano fakahoko ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni ko ia pea, ‘o kapau ko e tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Minisitā ke ʻikai tali ‘a e fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni, kuo pau ki he Ministā ke pulusi ‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ki he ʻikai tali ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20 ‘o e ʻikai tali ko ia.

#### 126 Makatuʻunga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni

Ko e ʻʻmakatuʻunga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni’’ ‘oku anga peheʹ ni —

(a) ko e tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngāue ala ngāueʻaki —

(i) te ne fakalahi ange ‘a hono lavaʻi ‘o e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘oku tuku atu ‘i he kupu 122 ;

(ii) ‘oku fiemaʻu ki hono fakalakalaka ‘a e ola lelei ‘o e feʻauʻauhi ‘o ‘ikai toe siʻi ange ‘i ha maketi ‘e taha ‘o kehe ange mei he maketi ki he ngāue ala ngāueʻaki ; mo

(iii) fakatatau ki ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ngāue fakalukufua kuo fanongonongo ki he Sino Pule ‘e he Minisitā ‘i he kupu 21 ;

(b) ‘i he meʻa ‘o e ngaahi ngāue ala ngāueʻaki ‘a ia ko e ngaahi ngāue hū fakalukufua —

(i) ko e ngāue hū fakalukufua ‘oku tuku atu kakato pe konga ‘o fakafou ‘i ha naunaufetuʻutaki ‘aia heʻikai lava ke fakafetongi, koeʻuhi ko ha meʻa moʻoni fakakomesiale, ‘o fakafou ‘i ha naunau fakafetuʻutaki ‘e taha koeʻuhi ke fakahoko ‘a e ngāue hū fakalukufua ko ia ;

(ii) ngofua ki he hū kihengāuehū fakalukufua(ko hanunuʻa‘oetuʻutuʻuni)

‘oku malava fakatekinikale ‘i hono fakakau ‘o e tekinolosia ‘oku ‘atā pe ngalingali ke ‘atā, ko e fakapotopoto ‘o e ngaahi fakamole ‘oku kau ki ai mo e tokoni ki hono ‘oatu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ʻi he anga-tonu, fakahoko ngāue pe fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki pe ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki kehe ; mo (iii)hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngāue hū fakalukufua ‘a ia ‘e ‘ikai ke ne fakasiʻisiʻi ‘a e ngaahi fakaʻaiʻai ke fakalelei pe ko e ngaahi fakaʻaiʻai ha ‘inivesi lelei ‘i ha naunau fetuʻutaki

‘a ia ‘e ngofua ke tuku atu mei ha ngāue hū fakalukufua ;

(c) ‘i he meʻa ‘o e ngaahi ngāue ala ngāueʻaki ‘a ia ko ha ngaahi naunau ngāue hū —

(i) ‘oku ngalingali ke ‘i ai ‘a e fiemaʻu lahi mei he kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi pe niʻihi ‘e ngalingali ko ha kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘a ia ‘oku fiemaʻu ‘a e hū ki he naunau ‘ikai fakahoko ngāue ;

(ii) ‘oku mahuʻinga ‘a e naunau ‘ikai fakahoko ngāue ki hono fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ;

(iii) ko e ngofua ki he ngāue hū ki he ngaahi naunau te ne taʻofi ‘a e toe hiki tatau ‘o e ngaahi naunau tefito ‘a ia ‘oku ala vahevahe lelei ;

(iv) ko e siʻisiʻi ‘a e hū ki he naunau ‘ikai fakahoko ngāue ‘okuʹ ne fakahoko

‘a e fakangatangata ki he hū ki he maketi ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni foʻou pe ko e fakalahi ‘o e taha maʻu laiseni lolotonga ; mo

(v) tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngaahi ngāue hū ki he ngaahi naunau ‘e ‘ikai ke fakasiʻisiʻi lahi ‘a e ngaahi fakaʻaiʻai ke fakalelei pe ko e ngaahi fakaʻaiʻai ke ‘inivesi lelei ‘i ha naunau ‘ikai fakahoko ngāue ‘a ia ‘oku kumi hū ki ai.

#### 127 Lau ko ha ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala lava hili ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni, ‘i hano tuʻutuʻuni, ke lau ‘a e ngaahi ngāue ko eni ko ha ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni —

(a) ‘i he ngaahi teemi pehē ‘okuʹ nepehē ‘oku feʻunga ‘o fakakau ki ai ‘a e makatuʻunga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni —

(i) ko ha ngāue hū fakangatangata fakalotofonua tuʻumaʻu ;

(ii) ko ha ngāue tamate hū ‘oku ‘ave holo ki he fāmili ;

(iii) hū ki he mo e fehokotakiʻaki mo e ngaahi uaea malohi tu’apule’anga (international cable capacity)

(iv) ‘atā ki he kaunga-tuʻuʻanga mo e hū fakasino ki he ngaahi tuʻunga ki he tauʻanga uaea fakavahaʻapuleʻanga (kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi ngāue mahuʻinga ‘i he ngaahi tuʻunga ko ia)

(v) feʻaveaki fakalukufua ‘i he faipa backhaul mei ha tuʻunga ki he tauʻanga uaea fakavahaʻapuleʻanga ki he fuofua fakafetongi ‘a ia

‘oku ʻoatu ai ‘a e fehokotakiʻaki; mo e

(vi) ko ha ngāue hū ki he ngaahi naunau ki he ngaahi taua, ngaahi pou, ngaahi pou (masts) pe ngaahi faʻunga tatau ‘a ia ‘oku ngāueʻaki ke tuku atu ha ngāue fetuʻutaki fakaletiō ; mo

(b) ‘i he ngaahi makatuʻunga ‘a ia ‘e fakahā ‘i he aleapau ngāue fakalukufua totonu, ha ngāue hū ki he ngaahi naunau ki he ngaahi naunau ki he ngāue fakalukufua,

(2) Ko hano fakahaa’i ‘o ha ngāue ala ngāueʻaki ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘e lava ke liliu pe fakataʻeʻaongʻi ‘i he kupu 123 ki he 125, kae ‘i he hili pē ha taʻu ‘e 5 mei he kamata ‘o e kupuʹ ni.

#### 128 Lesisita ‘o e ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni

(1) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne tauhi ha lesisita ‘oku felaveʻi mo e ngaahi fakahā

‘i he kupu 34.

(2) Kuo pau ki he lesisita ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) —

(a) ke fakakau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi meʻa ni —

(i) ngaahi fakaikiiki ‘o ha ngaahi fakahā pehe (kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fakahā ‘a ia ‘okuʹ ne fakataʻeʻaongaʻi)

(ii) ngaahi fakaikiiki ‘o e ngaahi liliu mo e ngaahi fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘o ha fakahā pehē ; mo e

(iii) ngaahi tatau ‘o e ngaahi lipooti kuo teuteuʻi ‘i he kupu 123(5)(d) ;

(b) ke tauhi ‘aki e ngaahi founga fakaʻelekitulonika ; mo

(c) pulusi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i heʻene uepisaiti.

VAHESI’I B – TUKU ATU ‘O E NGAAHI NGᾹUE KUO TUʻUTUʻUNI

#### 129 Hū ki he ngaahi me’angaue mo e ngaahi ngaue

(1) Kuo pau fakatatau ki he kupu-si’i (2) mo e (3), ki he taha ma’u laiseni ke ‘oatu,

‘o kapau ‘e fiema’u ‘i ha tohi mei he taha kole ma’u laiseni, ke ‘oatu ki he taha kole ma’u laiseni ha ngāue kuo fokotu’u.

(2) ‘Oku ‘ikai fiemaʻu ki he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ke muimui pau ki he kupu- siʻi (1) tukukehe —

(a) ‘i ha kole ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ki he tuku atu ‘o ha ngāue hū ki he naunau, ko e ngofua ‘oku taumuʻa ‘ataʻata pē ki hono fakaʻatā ‘o e taha maʻu laiseni kole ke —

(i) fakahoko‘aengaahinaunaupengaahingāuefetuʻutakifeʻauʻauhi

(ii) fokotuʻu haʻane ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki;

(b) ‘i ha kole ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ki he tuku atu ‘o ha ngāue hū fakalukufua, ko e ngofua ‘oku taumuʻa ‘ataʻata pē ki hono fakaʻatā taha maʻu laiseni kole ke tuku atu ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki, pe ngaahi ngāue fakahoko kakano; pea

(c) ko e taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku kole ‘oku ne ‘oange ki he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ha fanongonongo fakapotopoto ko e taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku kole ‘okuʹ ne feimaʻu ‘a e fakaʻatā.

(3) ‘Oku ‘ikai ha meʻa ‘i he Vaheʹ ni ke ne hilifaki ha fatongia ki ha taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ha kole mei ha taha maʻu laiseni kole ki he tuku atu ‘o ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni —

(a) ki he fakangatangata ‘e ala hanga ai ‘e he fatongia ‘o —

(i) taʻofi ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko pe ko ha taha maʻu laiseni kuo ne ‘osi maʻu ha fakaʻatā ki he ngāue kuo ‘osi fakahaaʻi mei hono maʻu ‘o ha konga feʻunga ‘o e ngāue ke malava ‘o feau ‘a

‘enau ngaahi fiemaʻu naʻe hanganaki ki ai ‘i he mahina ‘e 24 hoko mai ‘o fuaʻaki ‘a e taimi naʻe fakahoko ai ‘a e kole; pe

(ii) taʻofi ha taha mei hono maʻu, ‘i hono ngāueʻaki ‘o ha totonu ‘i ha aleapau ngāue ‘a ia ne ngaueʻaki ‘i he taimi naʻefakahoko ai ‘a e kole, ha tuʻunga feʻunga ‘o e ‘atā ki he ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ke malava ‘o feau ‘a e ngaahi fiemaʻu moʻoni ‘a e taha ko ia; pe

(b) ‘a ia ‘e fiemaʻu ha taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ke tuku atu ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘o kapau ‘oku ‘i ai ha ngaahi makatuʻunga lelei ke tui ko e —

(i) taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘e ‘ikai lava, ki ha material extent, ke faipau ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘a ia ‘oku tuku atu ai ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ‘a e ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni; pe

(ii) koetahamaʻulaisenikole‘e‘ikailava,‘ihefelaveʻimoefatongia ko ia, ke maluʻi ‘a e ala falalaʻanga ‘o ha netiueka fetuʻutaki pe ko e malu ‘a e kakai taautaha ‘oku ngāue ‘i he, pe ngāueʻaki ‘a e ngaahi ngāue ‘oku tuku atu ‘aki ‘a e founga ‘o ha, netiueka fetuʻutaki pe ko ha naunau fetuʻutaki.

(4) Ko e ngaahi fakatata ‘o e ngaahi makatuʻunga ke fakahoko ai ha tui ‘i he kupu siʻi (3)(b)(i) ‘oku kau ki ai ‘a e —

(a) fakamoʻoni ko e taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘oku ‘ikai ke falalaʻanga ‘a hono tuʻunga fakakulētiti; pe

(b) koetoutou‘ikaikelava‘ehetahamaʻulaisenikolekefaipaukihengaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘a ia kuo fakahoko ai ‘a e hū tatau ( ‘o tatau ai pē mei he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko)

#### 130 Ngaahi fatongia tuʻupau ki hono tuku atu ‘o ha ngaahi ngāue kuo

tuʻutuʻuni

(1) Kuo pau ‘i hano fiemaʻu ‘o ha taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ke tuku atu ha ngāue kuo fakahā ki ha taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘i he kupu 129, ke —

(a) fakakakato ‘a e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi kole fakapotopoto ki hono fehokotakiʻaki ‘i ha poini ‘e malava fakatekinikale ‘a ia ‘e ‘ikai uesia ai

‘e he fehokotakiʻaki ‘a e malu, fakahoko ngāue pe lelei ‘o e netiueka ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko;

(b) tauhi ‘a e malava ke ngaue ngataʻanga-ki he-ngataʻanga ke tokoni ki hono fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni kole ki heʻene kasitomā neongo ko e kasitomā ‘oku fakahoko hangatonu ki he netiueka fetuʻutaki ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko;

(c) tuku atu ‘a e ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he ngaahi makatuʻunga

‘a ia ‘oku ‘ikai ke fakafaikehekeheʻi taʻetotonu ‘i he vahaʻa ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni ‘oku kole pea ‘oku tatau pe ‘i ha tuʻunga mo ha natula fakatekinikale ‘oku lelei ange ‘i he ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he kau maʻu laiseni kolemaʻa hono ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki pe ko ia ‘o hono ngaahi hoangāue;

(d) fakahoko ‘a e hū ki he ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki, ngaahi netiueka fetuʻutaki, polokanga mo e ngaahi ngāue (kau ki ai ‘a e kaunga tuʻuʻanga) —

(i) ‘o hangē ko hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki he taha maʻu laiseni kole ke fefakahokoʻaki mo fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ki heʻene ngaahi kasitomā; pea

(ii) ‘i ha natula ‘a ia ‘oku feʻunga ‘a e ‘ikai ke ‘uuni koeʻuhi ke ‘oua

‘efiemaʻu‘aetahamaʻulaisenikoleketotongi‘aengaahi naunau fetuʻutaki, ngaahi netiueka fetuʻutaki, polokalama mo e ngaahi ngāue ‘a ia ‘oku ‘ikai fiemaʻu ki ai ke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ki heʻene kau kasitomā;

(e) ke fai ‘a e ngaahi sitepu fakapotopoto kotoa ke fakapapauʻi ‘e maʻu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni kole, ‘i heʻene felaveʻi mo hono fakahoko ‘o e ngāue kuo fahaa’i ki he taha maʻu laiseni kole —

(i) ‘iloʻi, tokanga’i mo e fakatonutonu ‘o ha fehalaaki;

(ii) tuʻutuʻuni mo hono fakahoko,

‘o ha natula fakatekinikale mo fakangāue mo e taimi ‘a ia ‘oku tatau ki he meʻa ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko kiate ia pe ko hono ngaahi hoangāue;

(f) ngaueʻaki pe ‘a e fakamatala kuo maʻu mei ha taha maʻu laisni kole koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi kaveinga ‘a ia ne tuku atu ai ki he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko pea ‘ikai fakahoko ‘a e fakamatala pe ko hano ngāueʻaki ‘o e fakamatala ke maʻu ha lelei ‘o e feʻauʻauhi;

(g) fakaʻatā ki he, ‘i hano kole mei ha taha maʻu laiseni kole, ‘i ha taimi feʻunga ‘a e kotoa ‘o e fakamatala, kau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fakaikiiki fakatekinikale mo e ngaahi fakamatala fakakomesiale kehe ‘oku mahuʻinga, ‘a ia ‘oku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki he fehokotakiʻaki pea ki he taha maʻu laiseni koleke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki ki heʻene kau kasitomā; mo

(h) fakahoko, ‘i ha kole mei ha taha maʻu laiseni kole, ha fakamatala moʻua pehē ‘i heʻene fehokotaki mo e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku fakataha mo e; pe felaveʻi ki he, tuku atu ‘o e ngāue kuo tuʻutu’uni ‘a ia ‘oku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke malava ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni kole ke fakahoko ‘a e fakamatala moʻua ‘oku tonu pea taimi feʻunga ki he kau kasitomā ‘o fekauʻaki mo ‘ene ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki.

#### 131 Ngaahi makatuʻunga ‘o e hū

Ko e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e tuʻunga ‘o hono fakahoko ‘o ha ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 129 mo e 130 ‘e fakahā ia ‘i ha taha pe lahi ange ‘o e ngaahi founga ko eni —

(a) ‘aki ‘a e felotoi ‘i he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko pea mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole;

(b) ‘aki ha hū ki ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘i he kupu 138; pe

(c) ki he ngataʻanga ‘a ia ko e kole ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘i he kupu 129 ki ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘oku ‘ikai fakakau ‘i ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a ia kuo fakahū ‘e he kau maʻu laiseni fakahoko, ‘aki ‘a e fakatonutonu ‘i he kupu 133.

#### 132 Teuteuʻi ‘o e ngaahi tuʻutu’uni ki he fehokotakiʻaki mo e hū

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi lao fekauʻaki mo e fehokotakiʻaki mo e hū, ‘o fakaha —

(a) ‘a e ngaahi lao ki he fakamahuʻinga ki he ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘okuʹ ne fakaʻaiʻai ‘a hono aʻusia ‘o e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Lani pea mo e Kongaʹ ni, kau ki ai, ‘oka feʻunga, ‘a e ngaahi tuʻunga mahuʻinga taau;

(b) ha ngaahi tuʻutu’uni siʻisiʻi taha ki he ngaahi aleapau ki hono tuku atu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni;

(c) ha ngaahi fakangatangata ‘e ngofua ke ngāueʻaki ki he makatuʻunga ‘o e ngaahi aleapau pe ki he ngaahi konga ‘o ha ngaahi aleapau pehē;

(d) ha ngaahi tuʻunga fakatekinikale ki he fehokotakiʻaki mo e hū; mo e

(e) ngaahi meʻa kehe pehē ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi pea feʻunga mo e ngaahi kaveinga ‘o e fehokotakiʻaki mo e hū.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, kimuʻa peaʹ ne faʻu ‘a e ngaahi lao ‘i he kupu-siʻi(1), ke ne fakahoko ha fealealeaʻaki ‘i he kupu 23 ‘o e Laoʹ ni.

VAHESI’I C - FAKATONUTONU MO E NGAAHI ALEAPAU KUO ALEA’I

#### 133 Fakatonutonu

(1) Kapau —

(a) ‘e fiemaʻu ha taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ke ne tuku atu ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ki ha taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘i he kupu 129;

(b) heʻikai lava ke felotoi ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘o hono tuku atu ‘o ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni; pea

(c) ko e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘oku ‘ikai felaveʻi mo hono tuku atu ‘o ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘oku fakakau ‘i ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a ia kuo fakahū ‘e he kau maʻu laiseni fakahoko,

‘e ngofua ki ha taha pe ‘i he ongo taha maʻu laiseni, ‘i he faitohi, ke kole

‘a e Sino Pule ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘i ha fakatonutonu ‘i he kupuʹ ni.

(2) Kuo pau heʻikai ke fakahoko pe ‘e he Sino Pule ha fakatonutonu ‘i he kupuʹ ni

‘i he meʻa ‘oku ne tui kuo ‘i ai ha faingamalie feʻunga ki he kau maʻu laiseni ke aleaʻi ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ki hono tuku atu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni pea ko e ngaahi alea fakakomesiale pehē ‘i he vahaʻa ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni kuo ‘ikai ola lelei.

(3) Kapau ‘e tali ‘e he Sino Pule ‘a e kole ki ha fakatonutonu ‘i he kupu (1) mo e (2) —

(a) kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne fakahā ki he ongo faʻahi fakatouʻosi ‘i he faitohi ‘a ‘ene tali ‘o e kole ki he fakatonutonu;

(b) kuo pau ki he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole ke

‘i he māhina ‘e 2 mei he ‘aho ‘o e fakanongonongo ‘i he palakafi (a) (tukukehe ‘oka tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Sino Pule ha meʻa kehe, ‘aki ‘a e fakangofua ‘a e kau maʻu laiseni) ke fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu ki he Sino Pule;

(c) kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ‘oange ki he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole ha ngaahi tatau ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a e taha taki taha;

(d) kuo pau ki he taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole ke

‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 mei hono maʻu ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha ‘i he palakalafi (c) (tukukehe ‘o ka tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Sino Pule ha meʻa kehe, ‘aki ‘a e fakangofua ‘a e kau maʻu laiseni) ke ‘ave ki he Sino Pule ha tali kuo tohi ki he fokotuʻu ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha; pea

(e) kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, ‘i loto ‘i he māhina ‘e 6 ‘o e fanongonongo ‘i he palakalafi (a), ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘o hono tuku atu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 131(c) pea ke fakahā ki taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘o ‘ene faituʻutuʻuni pea mo e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ko ia.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘i he kupu-siʻi (3)(e), ke vakaiʻi ‘a e —

(a) ngaahi fokotuʻu kuo tohi mo e ngaahi tali kuo fakahoko ‘e he ngaahi faʻahi ki he fakatonutonu ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi(3);

(b) ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he fehokotakiʻaki mo e hū;

(c) ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e kupu 129 mo e 130;

(d) ha ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a ia ‘oku feʻunga kuo fakahū ‘e he taha fakahoko hū pe ko ha taha maʻu laiseni kehe ‘i he Vahesiʻi D; mo

(e) pe ko e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘oku taau ‘i hono vakaiʻi ‘o e ngaahi meʻa ko eni —

(i) ngataʻanga ‘o e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘e ngalingali ke fakalahi ki hono aʻusia ‘o e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu 122;

(ii) ko e ngaahi lelei fakapisinisi ‘oku fakalao ‘o e kau maʻu laiseni

‘oku kau ki ai, pea mo e ‘inivesi ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ‘i hengaahinaunau‘okungāueʻakiketukuatu‘aengāuekuofakahā

‘oku kau ki ai;

(iii) ko e ngaahi lelei ‘a ha kakai ‘oku ‘i ai ‘enau totonu ke ngāueʻaki

‘a e ngaahi ngāue kuo fakahā ‘oku fekauʻaki mo ia;

(iv) ko e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakangāue mo fakatekinikale ‘oku taau ki he malu mo e falalaʻanga ‘o e ngāue ‘o e ngaahi ngāue mo e naunau fetuʻutaki; mo e

(v) ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘a ia ‘oku fokotuʻu ki he ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki tatau ‘i he ngaahi mafai kehe.

(5) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu (3) mo e (4), ‘i ha fanongonongotohi,kefakahā‘a engaahilao‘okungāueʻakikihafakatonutonu

‘i he kupuʹ ni.

(6) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ki he hū ‘i he kupuʹ ni koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu 131(c), ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘a ia kuo pau ke ngāue mei he ‘aho ‘e kole ai ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e Sino Pule ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga ‘i he kupu 133(1).

(7) Kuo pau ki he taha maʻu laiseni ke faile ha tatau ‘o ha aleapau ‘a ia ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 ‘o e hū ki he aleapau.

#### 134 Ngaahi aleapau kuo aleaʻi

(1) ‘O kapau ‘e tuku atu ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘e taha ‘o fakatatau mo ha aleapau ‘a ia naʻe ‘ikai —
