(a) ko ha aleapau ‘i he ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e ngaahi tuʻunga kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 113; pe

(b) hū ki ai ‘o fakatatau mo e kupu 131(b),

‘e ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘i ha fanongonongo tohi, ke fakahinohino ‘a e kau maʻu laiseni ke liliu ‘a e aleapau ke tatau mo e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku

‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu 133(4)(b) ki he (c).

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, kimuʻa ‘i hono ‘oatu ‘o ha fanongonongo tohi ‘i he

(a) ke fakahā ki he ongo faʻahi ki he aleapau ‘okuʹ ne fakakaukauʻi ‘a hono ngāueʻaki hono mafai ‘i he kupu-siʻi(1);

(b) ‘oangekiheongofaʻahikihafaingamaliefeʻungakefakahokohangaahi fokotuʻu ki he Sino Pule, mo

(c) fakakaukauʻi ‘a e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a ia kuo ‘osi maʻu ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi(2)(b).

(3) Kuo pau ki he ngaahi faʻahi ki he aleapau ke faipau ki ha fanongonongo ‘i he kupu-siʻi(1)

(4) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘a ia ko ha faʻahi ‘e taha ki ha aleapau ‘a ia

‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi(1) ke faile ha tatau ‘o e aleapau ko ia ki he Sino Pule;

(a) kapaukoealeapaunaʻehūkiai kimuʻa‘ihekamatangāueʻaki‘oekupuʹ ni, ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20 ‘o e kamata ngāueʻaki ‘o e kupuʹ ni;

(b) kapau ko e aleapau ‘oku felaveʻi mo ha ngāue kuo fakahā ‘a ia na’e ‘ikai ko ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he taimi naʻe fakahoko ai ‘a e hū ki he aleapau, ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20; pea

(c) ‘i ha toe meʻa kehe, ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 mei he hū ki he aleapau.

VAHESI’I D – NGAAHI TOHI TUʻUAKI

#### 135 Fakahū atu ‘o e ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘aki ha fanongonongo tohi, ke ne fakahinohino ha taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko ke faʻu mo fakahū atu (‘i loto ‘i ha taimi kuo fakamahinopau) ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ki ha ngāue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku faitatau mo e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu 133(4)(b) ki he (e).

(2) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha maʻu laiseni, ‘i heʻene kamataʻi pē ‘e ia, ke ne fakahū atu ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ki ha ngāue ala ngāueʻaki.

#### 136 Vakaiʻi ‘o e ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu

(1) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ‘e he Sino Pule ha talanoa mo e kakai fekauʻaki mo ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ‘a ia kuo fakahu atu ‘e ha taha laiseni ‘i he kupu 135 pe 137.

(2) Kuo pau ‘i hono fakahoko ‘a e alea fakapuleʻanga ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke pulusi

‘e he Sino Pule ha fanongonongo ‘o e —

(a) moʻoni ‘okuʹ ne fakahoko ‘a e alea;

(b) vahaʻa taimi lolotonga ‘a ia ‘e fakahoko ai ‘a e alea;

(c) natula ‘o e meʻa ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e alea;

(d) vahaʻa taimi ‘i loto ‘a ia, mo e sīpinga ‘a ia, ‘e ngofua ai ‘a e kakai ke fakahoko ‘a e fokotuʻu ki he Sino Pule fekauʻaki mo e meʻa ko ia;

(e) ngaahi meʻa ‘e fiemaʻu ‘e he Sino Pule ki he ngaahi fokotuʻu ke ngāue ki ai; mo e

(f) tuʻasila pe ngaahi tuʻasila ‘a ia ‘e ala ‘ave ki ai ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu.

(3) ‘E ngofua koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu-siʻi (2), ko e vahaʻa taimi ‘i loto ‘a ia ‘e fakahoko ai ‘e he kakai ‘a e fokotuʻu ki he Sino Pule kuo pau heʻikai ke toe siʻi hifo ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20.

(4) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule hili hono fakakakato ‘a e talanoa mo e kakai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) mo e (2) pe maʻu ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (6),

‘aki ‘a e fanongonongo tohi ki he taha maʻu laiseni ke ne —

(a) tali ‘a e tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu;

(b) ‘ikai tali ‘a e tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu; pe

(c) fakahinohino ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahokoʹ ke fakahū atu ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotu’u ‘oku ‘osi sivi ‘i he kupu 135(1) ‘i loto ‘i ha taimi pau.

(5) Kuo pau ‘i hono fakapapauʻi ‘a e founga ke fakahoko ai ‘ene mafai ‘i he kupu 136(4), ke fakakau ‘e he Sino Pule ‘a e —

(a) ngaahi fokotuʻu kotoa —

(i) kuo maʻu ‘i loto ‘i he vahaʻa taimi ‘oku fanongonongo ‘i he kupu siʻi (2)(d); pea

(ii) ‘oku ‘i he sīpinga moʻoni ‘oku fanongonongo ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(2)(d); mo e

(b) ngaahi meʻa ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu 133(4)(b) ki he (e).

(6) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ‘o kapau heʻikai faipau ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ki he kupu 135(1) pe 136(4)(c) ‘i he loto lelei, ‘aki ‘a e fanongonongo tohi ki he taha maʻu laiseni ke —

(a) fakapapauʻi ha tohi tuʻuaki fakaangaanga ke ngāueʻaki ki he taha maʻu laiseni ko ia, ‘o fakakau ʻa e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu siʻi (5); pe

(b) fakahinohino ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ke fakahoko ‘a e ngāue pau (hangē ko hono fakangāueʻi ha fakafofonga ke ne teuteuʻi ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ke toe siviʻi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4)).

(7) Kuo pau ke faipau ha taha maʻu laiseni ki ha fakahinohino ‘i he kupu-siʻi (6)(b) tukukehe kapau ko e taha maʻulaiseni ‘oku ‘ikai ke netoe ‘oatupelotoke ‘oatu

‘a e ngaahi ngāue ‘oku tuʻutuʻuni (kiate ia pe ko ha taha kehe).

(8) Kuo pau ke pulusi ‘e he Sino Pule ‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ki he ‘ikai tali ‘o ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4)(b), fakapapauʻi ‘o ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘i he kupu-siʻi (6)(a) pe ha fakahinohino ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4)(c) pe (6)(b).

#### 137 Toe siviʻi mo fakafaikehekeheʻi ‘a e ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule, ‘i he kole ‘a ha taha pe ‘i heʻene kamataʻi pe ‘e ia, ke toe siviʻi ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a ia kuo fakahū atu ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni

‘o kapau ko e Sino Pule‘okuʹ ne tui fakapotopoto ko etohituʻuaki kuo tali‘oku faikehekehe mo e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu 133(4)(b) ki he (e).

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule mo e tahamaʻu laiseni ‘o kapau ‘e tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Sino Pule ke toe siviʻi ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘i he kupu siʻi (1), ke na faipau ki he founga ‘i he kupu 136 ‘o hangē pē ko e tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ko ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu naʻe fakahū atu ‘i he kupu 135.

(3) ‘E ngofua ki he taha maʻu laiseni ke fakahū ha kiʻi liliu ki ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a ia kuo fakahū atu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ko ia, ‘a ia —

(a) kuo pau ki he Sino Pule mo e taha maʻu laiseni ‘o fakatatau ki he palakalafi (b), ke na faipau ki he founga ‘i he kupu 136 ‘o hangē pē ko e liliu ko ha tohi tu’uaki kuo fokotuʻu naʻe fakahū atu ‘i he kupu 135; pe

(b) kapau ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Sino Pule ko e liliu te ne maʻu pē ha ola kovi siʻisiʻi ki he kau maʻu laiseni kole pe kau maʻu laiseni kehe, ‘e ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne tali ‘a e liliu ‘o ‘ikai ke toe ngāue ki ha alea ‘i he kupu 136, ka kuo pau ke ne ‘oatu ki he kau maʻu laiseni ‘okuʹ ne fakakaukauʻi

‘e ala uesia ha faingamalie fakapotopoto ke lau ki he liliu kuo fokotuʻu.

#### 138 Pulusi mo e hū ki he ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali

(1) Kuo pau ko ha taha maʻu laiseni te ne —

(a) pulusi ‘a e kotoa ‘o ‘ene ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘i heʻene uepisaiti; pea

(b) kapau ‘e kole ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ha tatau ‘o ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali, ke ‘oatu ki he taha maʻu laiseni ko iaʹ ha tatau ‘o e tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ko iaʹ ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 5 mei hono kole.

(2) Kuo pau ‘o kapau ‘e fanongonongo ‘e hakau maʻu laiseni kole ki ha kau maʻu laiseni fakahoko ‘okuʹ loto ke hū ki ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a ia kuo fakahū atu

‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ko ia, ko e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko —

(a) ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10‘i hono maʻu ‘o e fanongonongo mei he taha maʻu laiseni kole te ne hū ki ha aleapau mo e taha maʻu laiseni kole ‘i he sīpinga ‘o e tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko; pea

(b) ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 ‘o e hū ki he aleapau ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he palakalafi (a), ke faile ‘a e aleapau ko ia mo e Sino Pule.

VAHESI’I E – NGAAHI LESISITA

#### 139 Lesisita ‘o e ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali

Kuo pau ke tauhi ‘e he Sino Pule ha lesisita ‘a e kotoa ‘oe ngaahi tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali.

#### 140 Lesisita ‘o e ngaahi aleapau hū

Kuo pau ke tauhi ‘e he Sino Pule ha lesisita ‘a e kotoa ‘o e ngaahi aleapau hū.

#### 141 Lesisita ‘o e ngaahi aleapau ki hono tuku atu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue ala

ngāueʻaki

(1) Kuo pau ke faile ‘e ha taha maʻu laiseni fakahoko mo e Sino Pule ‘a e ngaahi aleapau kotoa ki hono tuku atu ‘o e ngaahi ngāue ala ngāueʻaki (tukukehe ‘a e ngaahi aleapau hū) ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 hili ‘enau hū ki ai.

(2) Kuo pau ke tauhi ‘e he Sino Pule ha lesisita ‘o e ngaahi aleapau kotoa pē ‘oku

‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ka kuo pau heʻikai ke ‘ai ‘a e lesisita pe ngaahi aleapau ko iaʹ ‘o fakaʻatā ki he kakai.

VAHE 3 - TOKANGAʻI ‘A E NGᾹUE ‘O E SEKITOA FETUʻUTAKI

#### 142 Ko e Sino Pule ke ne tokangaʻi ‘a e kau maʻu laiseni

Kuo pau ko e Sino Pule te ne tokangaekina ‘a hono tokangaʻi ‘a e fakahoko ngāue mo e ngaahi ngāue ‘a e kau maʻu laiseni.

#### 143 Lipooti ki he Minisitā

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā ke ne fakahinohino ‘a e Sino Pule ‘aki ‘a e fanongonongo tohi ke teuteuʻi mo fakahū atu ha lipooti ki ai ‘i he ngāue (performance) pe ngaahi ngāue (activities) ‘a e kau maʻu laiseni.

(2) KoeSinoPulekuopaukenefaipaukihafanongonongotohi‘ihekupu-si’i (1).

#### 144 Kakano ‘o e lipooti

‘E ngofua ke kau ha lipooti ‘oku teuteuʻi ‘i he kupu 143 ka kuo pau heʻikai fakangatangata ki he —

(a) ngaahi meʻa fakalakalaka ki ha kautaha ‘i hono fakalukufua;

(b) ngaahi ngāue ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he kau maʻu laiseni;

(c) ngaahi tuʻunga ngāue lelei ‘aupito; mo

(d) ha meʻa kehe ‘e ala fiemaʻu ‘e he Minisitā.

VAHE 4 - NGAAHI FAKAʻEKEʻEKE MO E NGAAHI FAKATOTOLO

#### 145 Ngofua ki he Minisitā ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Potungāue pe Sino Pule ke

fakahoko ‘a e fakaʻekeʻeke

‘E ngofua ke fakahinohino ‘e he Minisitā ki he Potungāue pe Sino Pule, ‘i ha fanongonongo tohi, ke fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke ‘i he ngaahi meʻa —

(a) ‘oku ‘i ha natula fakalukufua ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakahoko ‘o e Laoʹ ni,

(b) ‘oku felaveʻi mo ha meʻa mahuʻinga pau fekauʻaki mo hono fakahoko ‘o e Laoʹ ni; pe

(c) ‘a ia te ne fakaaʻu ‘a e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Laoʹ ni.

#### 146 Ngofua ke kamata 'e he Potungāue ha faka’eke’eke

ʻE ngofua ke kamata ʻe he Minisitā ha fakaʻekeʻeke kapau ʻokuʹ ne fiemalie 'oku mahuʻinga ʻa e meʻa ko ia ʻo tatau pē ki he kakai pe ki he kau maʻu laiseni lolotonga pe fakaangaanga.

#### 147 Ngofua ki he Minisitā ke ne faʻu ‘a e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e fakaʻekeʻeke

‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā ke fakahoko ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he anga ‘o ha fakaʻekeʻeke ‘i he kupu 145 mo e kupu 146.

VAHE 5 - NGᾹUEʻAKI ‘O E LAO KI HE MALUʻI ‘O E KONISIUMA

#### 148 Lao ki he Maluʻi ‘o e Konisiuma

(1) Kuo pau heʻikai ngāueʻaki ‘a e Lao ki he Maluʻi ‘o e Konisiuma ki ha ngāue ‘a ia ‘oku ngāueʻaki ki ai ‘a e Kongaʹ ni pe Konga VI, pe ‘e ngāueʻaki ka ki ha fakaʻatā pe fakamafai ‘i he Vaheʹ ni.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule mo e Vaʻa Tokangaekina ‘o e Konisiuma ke hū ki ha tohi femahinoʻaki fakalao pe fokotuʻutuʻu kehe ‘okuʹ ne tuku atu ‘a e vahevahe

‘o e ngaahi fatongia ‘i he ongo sino ‘e ua mo fakamahinopau ‘a e ngaahi founga ngāue ki he ngāue fakataha, vahevahe ‘o e fakamatala pea mo solova ha hope pe taʻepau ‘e ala hoko felaveʻi mo ‘enau ngaahi fatongia mo e ngaahi mafai taki taha ‘i he Laoʹ ni pea mo ha lao kehe ‘okuʹ ne uesia ‘a e sekitoa fetuʻutaki (kau ai ‘a e Lao ki he Maluʻi ‘o e Konisiuma).

(3) ‘I he hoko hano faʻu ‘o ha lao ke fakatonutonu pe fakapekia ‘a e Lao ki he Malu’i ‘a e Konisiuma fakakātoa pe kongokonga, kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, fakataha mo e tali ‘a e Minisitā, ke ne fakahoko ‘a hono vakaiʻi ‘o e Vaheʹ ni ke fakapapauʻi pe ‘oku fiemaʻu ha ngaahi fakatonutonu ‘o e nunuʻa ki he Vaheʹ ni.

## KONGA XI – NGAAHI MEʻA KEHEKEHE

VAHE 1 - FOKOTUʻU ʻO E NGAAHI NAUNAU FETU’UTAKI, HŪ KI HE KELEKEKELE, ETC.

#### 149 Mafai ke hū

Ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka pe ko hono kau fakafofonga —

(a) ‘e ngofuake nau hūki he ngaahi kelekeleʻihetaumuʻake fokotuʻu,toʻo, monomono, liliu pe sivi ha naunau fetuʻutaki pea ʻe ngofua ke ne nofo atu ai ʻi ha taimi ʻoku fakapotopoto mo fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi ngāue kotoa pē mo e ngaahi meʻa kotoa pē ʻe ʻaonga ki he ngaahi taumuʻa 'o e Laoʹ ni;

(b) ʻe ngofua ke ne saveaʻi mo fua ʻa e maʻolunga ʻo e kelekele ko ia pe ko e konga ʻo ia pea mo fakaʻataʻatā mo toʻo ha faʻahinga kelekele, maka, kelekele, ʻoneʻone mo e makamaka mo e ʻulu ʻakau ʻe ʻaonga ki hono langa, liliu pe monomono ʻo ha naunau netiueka pehē pe ko ha konga ʻo ia pe ko ha ngaahi ngāue kehe ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e naunau fetuʻutaki ko ia;

(c) ʻe ngofua ke ne tuʻusi mo fakaʻataʻataaʻi ha foʻi ʻata ʻe ʻikai lahi hake ʻi he mita ʻe 20 ʻi he ngaahi tafaʻaki kotoa pē 'o ha naunau fetuʻutaki fakaangaanga pe lolotonga ʻa e ʻulu ʻakau mo e vao loloʻakau kotoa pē ʻoku ngalingali ʻe fakaʻefiʻefi ki hono foʻu mo e lele lelei ʻo ha naunau fetuʻutaki pehē;

(d) koeʻuhi ke lava ʻo hū ki ha naunau fetuʻutaki ʻe ngofua ke ne felotoi mo e taha ʻoku ʻaʻana pe ko e taha ʻokuʹ ne nofo ʻi ha kelekele ʻoku kolosi atu pe ʻoku pipiki ki ai ha naunau fetuʻutaki ke fokotuʻu ha matapā ʻi he kelekele ko iaʹ pea kapau kuo fakahoko ha felotoi pehē ʻe ngofua kiate ia pe ko hono fakafofonga ke ne fokotuʻu, tauhi mo fakaʻaongaʻi 'a e matapā ko iaʹ 'i he taumuʻa ke ne hū ai ki he naunau fetuʻutaki;

(e) ʻengofuakenefokotuʻu,foʻumotauhihangaahinaunaufetuʻutaki,ʻoku ʻi he tafaʻaki, fakalavalava, ʻolunga pe ʻi lalo ʻi he hala, hala loto kolo pe halalalahi pea ki hangaahitaumuʻa pehē ʻe ngofua kene fakaava hahala mo liliu ʻi he lalo hala ko iaʹ ha tuʻuʻanga ʻo ha paipa (kehe mei he paipa lahi tufaki vai pe kasa).

#### 150 Ke ʻoua ʻe taʻofi ʻa e feʻaluʻaki ʻi he hala Sinoʻi Puleʻanga

(1) Ka ʻi ai ha taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka te ne fokotuʻu pe foʻu ha naunau fetuʻutaki —

(a) fakalavalava pē ʻi ʻolunga ʻi ha hala, kuo pau ki he taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka ke ne fokotuʻu ʻa e naunau fetuʻutaki ko iaʹ 'i ha maʻolunga ʻe ʻikai ke uesia pe fakaʻefiʻefi ki he ʻaluʻanga tangata pe meʻalele ʻi he hala;

(b) ʻi ʻolunga pe ʻi lalo ʻi ha vai ʻoku faiʻanga fefolauʻaki, kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka ʻa e naunau fetuʻutaki ko iaʹ ʻi he founga ʻe ʻikai uesia pe fakaʻefiʻefi ki he fefolauʻaki 'i he vai ko ia.

(2) Ka liliu, monomono pe toʻo ʻe ha taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka ha naunau fetuʻutaki kuo fokotuʻu fakalavalava pe ʻi 'olunga ʻi ha hala pe ʻi ʻolunga pe ʻi ha lalo vai ʻoku faiʻanga fefolauʻaki, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke fakatupu ‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka hono fakaʻefihiaʻi ʻa e feʻaluʻaki pe ko e ngāueʻaki ʻo e hala pe vai 'oku faiʻanga fefolauʻaki ko ia.

#### 151 Hū ʻi he taimi ʻaho mo e fakahoko ha fanongonongo

Fakatatau ki he kupu 152 mo e 153, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai hū ha taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka pe ko hono kau fakafofonga ki ha ngaahi kelekele kehe mei he ngaahi kelekele ʻo e Sinoʻi Puleʻanga —

(a) ʻo taumuʻa ke foʻu pe toʻo mei ai ha faʻahinga naunau fetuʻutaki tukukehe ʻa e taimi ʻaho; pea

(b) kaeʻoua kuo fakahoko ha fanongonongo ʻaho pisinisi ʻe 5 ki he taha ʻoku ʻoʻona, pe nofo ʻi he kelekele pe ko hono fakafofonga ʻo fekauʻaki mo e teuteu ke foʻu ʻa e ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki ʻi he kelekele ko ia.

#### 152 Fanongonongo ʻo e hū ʻoka ʻikai maʻu ʻa e taha ʻoku ʻoʻona

Kapau ʻoku ʻikai ke maʻu ʻa e taha ʻoku ʻoʻona pe taha ʻoku nofo ʻi he kelekele ʻoku teuteu ke fakahoko ki ai ʻa e hū koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke fokotuʻu pe tauhi ha naunau fetuʻutaki hili hano fakahoko ha fakaʻekeʻeke ʻoku fakapotopoto kuo pau ke feʻunga pē ki he taha fakahoko ngāue netiuekaʹ ke ne fokotuʻu ʻi ha feituʻu ʻilonga ngofua ʻi he kelekele ko iaʹ ha fanongonongo tohi ʻo tohiʻi ai ʻa e loto ‘a e taha fakahoko ngāue netiuekaʹ ke hū ki he ʻapi ko iaʹ ʻi he ʻosi ʻa e ʻaho pisinisi ʻe 5 mei he ʻaho ʻo e fanongonongo. ʻI he ʻosi ʻa e taimi kuo lau ki ai ʻi he fanongonongoʹ ʻe ngofua ki he taha fakahoko ngāue netiuekaʹ pe ko hono fakafofongaʹ ke ne ngāueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi mafai kotoa pē kuo foaki ʻe he Laoʹ ni ʻo hangē pē naʻe ‘osi tufa ʻa e fanongonongoʹ ki he taha ʻoku ʻoʻonaʹ pe taha ʻoku nofo ʻi he kelekele ko iaʹ pe ko hono fakafofonga.

#### 153 Hū ʻikai fanongonongo ke sivi mo monomono

Neongo ʻa e kupu 111 ʻe ngofua ki he taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka pe ko hono fakafofonga, ‘ikai toe ʻoatu ha fanongonongo, ke ne hū ʻi ha ngaahi taimi ʻoku fakapotopoto ki he ngaahi kelekele ʻi he taumuʻa ke sivi, liliu pe monomono ha faʻahinga naunau fetuʻutaki.

#### 154 Totongi Huhuʻi

(1) ʻI hono fokotuʻu mo tauhi ha naunau fetuʻutaki, kuo pau ki he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka pe ko hono kau fakafofonga ke nau fakahoko ʻa e maumau fakapotopoto siʻisiʻi taha.

(2) Ka ʻi ai ha taha —

(a) ʻoku ʻi ai haʻane ngaahi totonu pe kaunga ki he ngaahi kelekele kuo maʻu 'i he ngaahi taumuʻa ke fokotuʻu pe tauhi ai ha naunau fetuʻutaki; pe

(b) ʻoku uesia kovi pe ʻoku maumau haʻane koloa tupu mei hono fokotuʻu pe tauhi ʻe he taha fakahoko ngāue netiuekaʹ pe ko hono fakafofonga ha naunau fetuʻutaki, kuo pau ke ʻi ai ha totonu ʻa e taha ko iaʹ ki ha totongi huhuʻi. Kuo pau ke fakapapauʻi ʻa e totongi huhuʻi ko iaʹ ʻe ha ongo fakamaau fakatonutonu ʻe toko ua kuo pau ko e taha ʻiate kinaua ʻe fokotuʻu ia ʻe he taha ʻeke totongi huhuʻi pea ko e taha ʻe he taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka.

(3) Kuo pau ke fokotuʻuʻe heongo fakamaau fakatonutonuʻetoko ua hafakamaau fakatonutonuhonotoluʻaiaʻehokokoefakamaauaofangatuku.Kapauʻeʻikai ke felotoi ʻa e ongo fakamaau fakatonutonu ki he lahi ʻo e totongi huhuʻi ke foaki ʻo kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, kuo pau ke fakakaukauʻi ʻe he fakamaau aofangatuku ʻa e fakamoʻoniʹ mo fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ki he meʻa ko ia.

(4) Ko e tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ongo fakamaau fakatonutonuʹ pe fakamaau aofangatuku, ʻo hangē ko ia ʻe hoko, kuo pau ke aofangatuku mo fakalao.

(5) Kapau kuo fakahoko ha ngāue pe ha meʻa ʻi he loto ki ai ʻa e taha ʻeke totongi huhuʻi, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai foaki pe totongi atu ha totongi huhuʻi ngata pe pea tukukehe kapau ko ha taʻetokanga lahi, ‘ikai fakahoko 'i he loto kovi pe kuo ʻosi fakahoko ha aleapau tohi ki ha totongi huhuʻi.

#### 155 Toʻo ʻa e ʻulu ʻakau etc maumau ki he ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki

Kapau ʻoku fakakaukau ha taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka ʻoku ʻi ai ha naunau fetuʻutaki ʻoku pe ngalingali ʻoku maumau pe ʻefihia ʻi ha faʻahinga founga pe ʻi ha fuʻu ʻakau pe ha ngoue ʻakau iiki ʻo tatau ai pē pe ko e ʻakau teuteu pe ʻoku tupu pe tuʻu ʻi ha ʻapi ʻoku pipiki atu ki he naunau fetuʻutaki ko iaʹ pe ʻoku ʻalu pe taki ʻi ʻolunga ai ʻa e naunau fetuʻutaki ko ia, ʻe ngofua ke toʻo ʻe he taha fakahoko ngāue netiuekaʹ pe ko hono fakafofonga ʻa e fuʻu ʻakau pe ngoue ʻakau iikiʹ pe ko ha konga ʻo ia pea kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke fakahoko ha fakaʻilo ʻi he laoʹ ʻi he toʻo ko iaʹ kapau kuo fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi ngāue pehē ʻi ha founga ʻe ʻikai hoko ai ha maumau noaʻia.

#### 156 Maumau fakatuʻupakē ki he ngaahi naunau fetuʻutaki

Ka ʻi ai ha taha te ne fakatupu ʻi ha faihala pe taʻetokanga ha maumau pe te ne fakaʻauha ha naunau fetuʻutaki pe ko ha konga ‘oha naunau fetuʻutaki ko ha meʻa pe kelekele ʻoku fekauʻaki mo ha naunau fetuʻutaki kuo pau ʻoka halaia, ke ala moʻua paʻanga ʻo ʻikai laka hake ʻi he $5000 pe ka ʻikai totongi, ki he ngāue nofo pōpula ʻo ʻikai laka hake ʻi he māhina ʻe 3 pe fakatouʻosi.

#### 157 Maumau tupu ʻi he fakaʻuli taʻetokanga

Ka fakatupu ha maumau ʻa ha naunau fetuʻutaki ʻe ha saliote mīsini pe ʻe ha saliote ʻoku toho ʻe ha hoosi pe ha monumanu kehe (“saliote toho”), pe ʻe ha faʻahinga meʻa ʻoku uta ʻi ha saliote misini pe saliote toho, kuo pau ʻe lau ʻa e fakaʻuli pe ko e taha ʻokuʹ ne puleʻi ʻa e saliote mīsiniʹ pe saliote toho ko iaʹ ʻoku halaia ki he hia ʻi he kupu 156 ʻikai toe fakamoʻoniʻi naʻe hoko ha taʻetokanga pe faihala, ka kuo pau ʻe ʻi ai ha totonu ʻa e taha ko iaʹ ke ne fakafepakiʻi ha fakakaukau ʻikai fakamoʻoniʻi pehē.

#### 158 Tānaki ki he tautea ʻa hono fakaleleiʻi ha maumau

Ko e taha kotoa pē te ne fakatupu ha maumau ki ha naunau fetuʻutaki kuo pau ke ne fakaleleiʻiʻa e maumau ki ha mahuʻinga ʻe tuʻutu'uni ʻeha Fakamaauʻanga ʻoku maʻu mafai feʻunga neongo kapau kuo ʻosi tautea paʻanga pe tautea nofo pōpula ʻi he laoʹ ni ki he maumau ko ia.

VAHE 2 - TAUTEA MO E NGAAHI HIA

#### 159 Tautea fakalukufua

(1) Ko e ʻikai ngāue pe ʻikai ke faipau, mo e ngāue kotoa pē kuo fakahoko pe naʻe feinga ke fakahoko ʻo fepaki mo e Laoʹ ni pe faʻahinga meʻangaue ‘oku faʻu ‘i he Laoʹ ni (kehe mei he ngaahi fakahinohino) kuo pau ke lau ia ko e hia ʻi he Laoʹ ni.

(2) Ki he hia pehē kotoa pē, kapau ʻoku ʻikai ha tuʻutuʻuni fakamahinopau ki ai, kuo pau ke moʻua paʻanga ʻa e taha faihia ʻoka halaia ʻo ʻikai laka hake ʻi he $10,000 pe ko e ngāue nofo pōpula ki he vahaʻa taimi ʻe ʻikai laka hake ʻi he ta’u ʻe 3 pe fakatouʻosi pea ʻikai ngata ai ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ha Fakamaauʻanga ʻoku maʻu mafai feʻunga ke maʻu ha meʻa kuo puke.

(3) ‘E ngofua ki he Talatalaakiʹ ‘i he Laoʹ ni ke ‘oatu —

(a) ki he pe ko e fakafofonga ‘o e ‘Ateni Seniale; pe

(b) ki he pe ko e fakafofonga ‘o e Sino Pule.

(4) Ko ha taha ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (3)(b), pe ko ha fakafofonga lao pe

‘ikai, ‘e ngofua ke ne hilifaki, fokotuʻu pe fakahoko ha fakaʻilo, fakamatala, lāunga pe ngāue kehe ʻi he Laoʹ ni, fakatatau ki ha ngaahi fakahinohino ‘oku

‘oatu ‘e he ‘Ateni Seniale.

#### 160 Ngaahi hopo sivile

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne fokotuʻu ha hopo ki hono toe maʻu ‘o ha tautea fakapaʻanga kuo hilifaki ki ha taha ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2) ‘iha Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku maʻu mafai feʻunga.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki ha Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku maʻu mafai feʻunga ke tuʻutuʻuni ki ha taha ke totongi ‘a e tautea fakapaʻanga ‘o kapau ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Fakamaauʻanga ko e taha ko ia —

(a) kuo ne maumauʻi ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e Laoʹ ni pe ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he Laoʹ ni (kehe mei he ngaahi fakahinohino); pe

(b) kuo ne —

(i) feinga ke maumauʻi;

(ii) tokoni (aided) pe tokoni’i (abetted) ha taha kehe ke ne maumauʻi;

(iii) fakalotoʻi, pe feinga ke fakalotoʻi, ha taha kehe ke ne maumauʻi; pe

(iv) kuo kau ki ha faʻahi ke, pe kau ‘i he ‘ilopau ‘i hono, maumauʻi ‘e ha taha kehe ha, tuʻutu’uni ‘a e Vahe 1 ‘o e Konga IX.

(3) Ko e tuʻunga fakamoʻoni ‘i ha hopo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ko e tuʻunga fakamoʻoni ia ‘i he ngaahi hopo sivile.

(4) Kuo pau ko ha hopo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ‘e kamata ‘i loto ‘i he māhina ‘e 3 hili

‘a e maumau totonu kuo fakahoko.

(5) Kuo pau ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (6), ko ha tautea fakapaʻanga ‘i he kupu- siʻi (2) ko e lahi ‘o e paʻanga ‘oku fakapapauʻi ‘e he Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku taau

‘o fakakau ki ai ‘a e ngaahi meʻa totonu kotoa pē kau ai —

(a) ‘aenatulamoefakangatangata‘oemaumaupeamohamolepemaumau kuo hoko ‘a ia ko e ola ‘o e maumau;

(b) ko e ngaahi tūkunga ‘a ia naʻe hoko ai ‘a e maumau; pea

(c) kuo maʻu ‘a e taha ko ia ‘e ha Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku maʻu mafai feʻunga

‘i ha hopo ‘i he Laoʹ ni kuo ne ngāue ʻi ha anga ‘oku ngāue tatau.

(6) Ko e tautea fakapaʻanga ‘e ala totongi ‘i he kupu-siʻi (5) —

(a) ‘ehatautaha,kuopau‘e‘ikailakahake‘ihe$5,000kihafuofuamaumau pe, ko ha maumau ‘e toki hoko, ko e lahi ange ‘o e $10,000 pe tuʻo 3 ‘o e mahuʻinga ‘o e lelei kuo maʻu ‘e he tautaha fakahangatonu pe ‘ikai fakahangatonu mei he maumau; mo

(b) ha sino fakakautaha, kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai laka hake —

(i) ‘i he meʻa ‘o ha maumau ha teemi pe tuʻunga ‘o ha laiseni pe ko e ngaahi fatongia ‘oku tuku atu ‘i he Vahe 2 pe Konga IX, ko e lahi ange ‘o e $20,000 pe tuʻo 3 ‘a e mahuʻinga ‘o e lelei kuo maʻu ‘e he sino fakakautaha fakahangatonu pe ‘ikai fakahangatonu mei he maumau; pe

(ii) ‘i ha meʻa kehe, $10,000 ki ha fuofua maumau pe, ki ha maumau

‘e toki hoko, $20,000.

(7) Kapau ko e anga ‘o e ngāue ‘oku kau ai ha ngaahi maumau lahi ‘o e Lao, ‘e ngofua ke kamataʻi ha hopo ‘i he kupuʹ ni ki ha taha fekauʻaki mo e maumau takitaha, ka ‘oku ‘ikai moʻua ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ki ha tautea fakapaʻanga ‘e taha ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘o felaveʻi mo e ngāue tatau.

(8) Ko e ngaahi tautea fakapaʻanga ‘e ala totongi ‘i he Laoʹ ni ke totongi ki he Paʻanga Ngāue Fakalukufua.

#### 161 Ngaahi fakalelei kehe

(1) Kapau ‘oku fiemalie ha Fakamaauʻanga kuo maumauʻi ‘e ha taha ‘a e Laoʹ pe kohatuʻutuʻuni‘okufakahoko‘iheLaoʹni(kehemei hengaahifakahinohino),

‘e ngofua, ‘i he tohi kole ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ke —

(a) tuʻutuʻuni ha ngaahi totonu;

(b) tuʻutuʻuni ha ‘akauni ke ‘ave;

(c) fakafoki ha aleapau;

(d) tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e paʻanga ke toe fakafoki ki ha taha;

(e) tuʻutuʻuni ko ha ngaahi tupu ‘oku maʻu pe maʻu lahi mei hano maumauʻi

‘o e Laoʹ ni ke tuku atu ki he Paʻanga Ngāue Fakalukufua;

(f) fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ki ha faʻahinga fekau pau pe fakangatangata, pe

‘oku fakataimi pe tuʻumaʻu;

(g) kapau ko e taha ko iaʹ kuo ne maʻu ha totonu ki hono maumauʻi ‘o e kupu 120, liliu kinautolu mei he totonu ko ia;

(h) tuʻutuʻuni ke pulusi ‘e he taha ko ia, ‘i he fakamole pē ‘a e taha ko ia, ‘a e fakamatala pehē ‘i hono fakakaukauʻi ‘e he Fakamaauʻangaʹ ‘oku taau

‘i ha tuʻuaki ‘oku ‘i he sīpinga mo ‘i he founga kuo fakamahinopau ‘e he Fakamaauʻanga;

(i) tuʻutuʻuni ‘aetaha ko iaʹkenefokotuʻuhapolokalama akoako pe faipau koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke fakapapauʻi ko e taha ko iaʹ mo ‘ene kau ngāue, kau fakafofonga (agents), mo e kau fakafofonga (representatives) kehe, ‘oku ‘ikai ke nau fakahoko ha toe ngaahi maumau ange; pe

(j) fakahoko ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku ‘ikai tautea kehe ‘oku fakakaukauʻi

‘e he Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku taau.

(2) ‘Ihanotuʻutuʻuni‘aetuʻungataaumoengaahiteemi‘ohafakalelei‘ihekupuʹ ni, ‘e fakakau ‘e he fakamaauʻanga ‘a e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakalukufua ‘oku fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi fakalelei ko iaʹ ‘i hono ngāueʻaki ‘e he ngaahi fakamaauʻanga ‘o e mafai sivileʹ mei he taimi ki he taimi.

#### 162 Ngaahi fakahā ‘o ha maumau

(1) ‘E ngofua ‘o kapau ‘oku ‘i ai ha ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘oku fakapotopoto ke tui ai ‘a e Sino Pule kuo maumauʻi ‘e ha taha ha laokupu fakahā ‘o ha maumau, ke

‘oatu ‘e he Sino Pule ha fakahā ‘o ha maumau.

(2) ‘Oku ‘ikai ngāue ha fakahā ʻo ha maumau kae ‘oleva —

(a) ‘oku ‘oatu ‘o felaveʻi mo ha maumau kuo tukuakiʻi ‘e taha ‘o ha kupu fakahā ‘o ha maumau;

(b) ‘oku ‘oatu ‘i loto ‘i he māhina ‘e 12 mei he hoko ‘o e maumau kuo tukuakiʻi ‘oku felaveʻi mo ia;

(c) ko e fakahā ‘o ha maumau pē ia ‘oku fekauʻaki mo e maumau kuo tukuakiʻi ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo ia; pea

(d) ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ‘a e fakamatala kuo fakamahinopau ‘i he kupu-siʻi (3).

(3) Kuo pau ke ‘i ai ‘i he fakahā ‘o ha maumau ‘a e —

(a) ngaahi fakaikiiki ‘o e maumau kuo tukuakiʻi ‘i he kupu fakahā ‘o ha maumau, kau ai ‘a e ‘aho kuo tukuakiʻi na’e hoko ai ‘a e maumau;

(b) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e founga ke fetuʻutaki ai ki he Sino Pule ʻo felaveʻi mo e fakahā ‘o ha maumau;

(c) fakamatala fekauʻaki —

(i) mo e lahi ‘o e tautea ‘e totongi ki he taha maʻu laiseni;

(ii) mo e taimi kuo pau ke totongi ai, ‘a ia kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai ke siʻi hifo

‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20 (vahaʻa taimi fakahā ‘o ha maumau); pea

(iii) mo e ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘e ala totongi ai; mo

(d) ha fakamatala kehe pehēʻe ngofua ke fiemaʻu ‘e he Minisitā ‘i he tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ‘i loto ‘i he houa ‘e 48 mei hono ‘oatu ha fakahā ‘o ha maumau ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke tuku ‘a e tatau ‘o e fanongonongo ‘i he Kalake

‘o e Fakamaauʻanga Polisi ki he vahefonua.

(5) Kapau ‘e ‘ikai maʻu ha totongi ‘i loto ‘i he vahaʻa taimi fakahā ‘o ha maumau —

(a) kuo pau ke ‘oatu ‘e he Kalake ‘o e Fakamaauʻanga Polisi ha tohi fekau ki he taha kuo tukuakiʻi ki ai ‘a e hia; pea

(b) kuo pau ke fakamaauʻi mo fakapapauʻi ‘e ha Fakamaau Polisi.

(6) Kapau ‘e totongi ‘e ha tahamaʻu laiseni ha tautea kuo fakamahinopau ‘i ha fakahā ‘o ha maumau ‘i loto ‘i he vahaʻa taimi fanongonongo —

(a) ‘e ‘ikai fakakau ‘oku maumauʻi ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e Laoʹ ni; pea

(b) ‘e ‘ikai ngofua ha ngaahi fakalelei pe ngaahi hopo kehe ke fakafepaki ki he taha maʻu laiseni ʻo felaveʻi mo e anga ‘o e ngāue ‘a ia ko e kaveinga

‘o e maumau kuo tukuakiʻi ki he kupu fakahā ‘o ha maumau ‘i he fakahā

‘o ha maumau.

(7) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ‘o fakamahinopau —

(a) ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e Laoʹ ni ‘e hoko ko e ngaahi kupu fakahā ‘o ha maumau; mo e

(b) lahi ‘o ha tautea ‘e ngofua ke totongi ‘i he malumalu ‘o e fakahā ‘o ha maumau ‘o felave’i mo ha kupu fakahā ‘o ha maumau.

#### 163 Ngaahi ngāue fakalao

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne tali ha ngāue tohi mei ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘o fekauʻaki mo ha ngāue ‘oku ngāueʻaki ‘e he Laoʹ ni, kau ai kae ‘ikai fakangatangata ki he ngaahi ngāue ‘a e taha ko ia —

(a) ‘e ngofua ke puke pe fakalelei ha ngaahi ngāue pe ʻikai ngāue kuo fakamahinopau ‘i he taimi ko iaʹ pea fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻunga ‘e fiemaʻu ‘e he Sino Pule;

(b) ‘e ngofua ke fakataha mo e kakai kehe, ‘i he ngaahi tuʻunga ‘e fiemaʻu ai ‘e he Sino Pule, koeʻuhi ke solova ha fakakikihi ‘i he ngaahi faʻahi;

(c) ʻe ngofua ke pulusi ha fakamoʻoni mo e kole fakamolemole ki ha ngaahi ngāue pe ʻikai ngāue kuo fakamahinopau ‘i ha nusipepa fakapuleʻanga feʻunga ‘i he sīpinga pehē pea ‘i he taimi pehē ‘e fiemaʻu ai ‘e he Sino Pule; pe

(d) ‘e ‘oatu ha ngaahi lipooti fakataimi ki he Sino Pule ke tokoni ki he Sino Pule ‘i hono tokangaʻi ‘a e faipau mo hono siviʻi ‘a e ola ki he ngaahi maketi fetuʻutaki, kau feʻauʻauhi mo e kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi ‘o e fetuʻutaki.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ‘o kapau ‘e ‘ikai faipau ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ki ha ngāue tohi kuo tali ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke ne tohi kole ki ha Fakamaauʻanga ki ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o fakatatau ki he kupuʹ ni.

(3) ‘I he tohi kole ‘e ha Sino Pule, kapau ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Fakamaauʻanga kuo

‘ikai faipau ha taha maʻu laiseni ki ha ngāue tohi kuo tali ‘e he Sino Pule ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ‘e ngofua ki he Fakamaauʻanga ke fakahoko ‘a e taha pe kotoa ‘o e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko eni —

(a) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke ne fakahinohino ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke faipau ki he ngāue tohi kuo tali ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ‘i he kupu-si’i (1);

(b) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke totongi ‘a e lahi ‘o e paʻanga ‘oku tatau ki he mahuʻinga

‘o ha tuʻumalie fakapaʻanga kuo maʻu ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘a ia ko e ola ‘o e ‘ikai ke faipau ki he ngāue tohi kuo tali ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1);

(c) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke totongi huhuʻi ha taha ki ha mole pe maumau kuo hoko

‘a ia ko e ola fakahangatonu ‘o e ‘ikai ke faipau ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni ki he ngāue tohi kuo tali ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ‘i kupu-siʻi (1); pe

(d) ha tuʻutuʻuni kehe ‘oku fakakaukauʻi ‘e he Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku taau.

#### 164 Tuʻutuʻuni tuku mo e ngaahi fakalelei fakahinohino

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Pule ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (2) mo e (3), ke ‘oatu ha tuʻutuʻuni tuku (‘o fekauʻi ke fakamamaʻo ha taha mei he anga ‘o e ngāue kuo fakamahinopau) pe ko ha fakalelei fakahinohino (‘o fekauʻi ke fakahoko ‘e ha taha ‘a e ngāue kuo fakamahinopau) ‘o kapau ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Sino Pule ko e taha ko ia —

(a) kuo ne maumauʻi, pe ‘oku ngāue ki hono maumauʻi ‘o e, Laoʹ ni pe ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku faʻu ‘i he Laoʹ ni (kehe mei he ngaahi fakahinohino);

(b) ‘okuʹ ne ngāueʻaki ha meʻangāue ‘oku ngalingali ke ne fakatupu ha kaunoaʻia ʻikai ha ʻuhinga ki ha ngāue fetuʻutaki fakaletiō koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o ha ngāue meʻa fakafokifā pe ki ha taumuʻa ‘a ia ko e malu ‘o ha taha pe ko ha vaka, vakapuna pe saliote mīsini ‘e fakafalala ki ai; pe

(c) ‘oku ne fakahoko ngāue ha naunau makehe pe ‘oatu ha ngāue makehe

‘oku ngalingali ke ne fakatupu ‘a e kaunoaʻia ‘a ia ‘e fakatuʻutamaki ki he moʻui pe malu pe te ne uesia ‘a e anga-tonu pe malu ‘o e netiueka.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule kimuʻa hono ‘oatu ha tohi tuku pe fakalelei fakahinohino ki ha taha, ke ne ‘oatu ha fanongonongo ki he taha ko ia —

(a) ‘o fakahā ‘a e ngaahi moʻoni ‘okuʹ ne fakatupu ‘a e ngāue kuo tukuakiʻi pea, ‘i heʻene taau, ‘a e hingoa ‘o e taha ‘oku tukuakiʻi ki ai; mo

(b) fakamahinopau ha vahaʻa taimi fakapotopoto ‘i loto ‘i he mo ha feituʻu

‘a ia ‘e fakahoko ai ha hopo ke ‘oatu ki he taha ko iaʹ ha faingamalie ke fakahā ‘a e ‘uhinga lelei ke tuku ai hono fakahoko ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni.

(3) Kapau ‘e ‘i ha hopo ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘a e kupu-siʻi (2)(b) —

(a) ko e taha ko iaʹ ‘oku ‘ikai lava ke ne fakahā ‘a e ‘uhinga lelei ke ‘ikai fakahoko ai ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni tukuʹ pe fakalelei fakahinohino, ‘e ngofua ki he Sino Puleʹ ke ne ‘oatu ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni pe fakahinohino ko ia; pe

(b) ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ko e taha ko iaʹ kuo ne fakahā ‘a e ‘uhinga lelei ke ‘ikai fakahoko ai ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni tukuʹ pe fakalelei fakahinohino, kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ‘ikai ‘oatu ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni pe fakahinohino ko ia.

(4) Kuo pau ko ha tuʻutuʻuni pe fakahinohino ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) —

(a) te ne maʻu ha fakamatala ‘o e ngaahi moʻoni ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2)(a);

(b) ‘ihemeʻa‘ohatuʻutuʻunituku,kefekauʻi‘aetahakoiaʹkefakamamaʻo mei he ngāue kuo fakamahinopau;

(c) ‘i he meʻa ‘o ha fakalelei fakahinohino, ke fekauʻi ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngāue kuo fakamahinopau;

(d) ke ‘oatu fakataha mo ha ngaahi tohi ko e poupou ki he tukuakiʻi; pea

(e) ke fakaaʻu ki he taha ‘a ia ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni tukuʹ pe fakalelei fakahinohino.

(5) Kuo pau ‘o kapau ‘e ‘oatu ‘e he Sino Pule ki ha taha ha tuʻutuʻuni tuku pe fakalelei fakahinohino ‘i he kupuʹ ni, ko e taha ko iaʹ ‘e faipau ki he tuʻutuʻuni tukuʹ pe fakalelei fakahinohino.

#### 165 Ngaahi ngāue ‘a e faʻahi hono tolu

(1) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha kuo hoko ki ai ha mole pe maumau ‘a ia ko e ola ‘o e anga

‘o e ngāue ‘a ha taha kehe ‘a ia naʻaʹ ne fakatupu ‘a hono maumauʻi ‘o e Laoʹ ni pe ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he Laoʹ ni (kehe mei he ngaahi fakahinohino), ‘i ha Fakamaauʻanga ‘oku maʻu mafai feʻunga, ke toe maʻu mai

‘a e lahi ‘o e mole pe maumau ‘i ha hopo ki he taha ko ia ‘oku ne maumauʻi ‘a e Laoʹ pe ko ha taha kehe ‘oku kau ‘i he maumau.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he ngaahi hopo ‘i he kupuʹ ni ke kamataʻi ‘e he Sino Pule, ‘a ia ko e fakafofonga ‘o ha faʻahinga kakai ‘oku hoko ki ai ‘a e moleʹ pe maumau.

(3) ‘E ngoufa ki he Sino Pule, ‘o fakatatau ki ha ngaahi tuʻunga kuo hilifaki ‘e he fakamaauʻanga, ke tuʻu ‘i vahaʻa ‘i ha hopo kuo kamataʻi ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1).

#### 166 Hia ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he sino fakakautaha

Kapau ‘oku fakahoko ʻe ha sino fakakautaha ha hia ʻi he Laoʹ ni pe ko hono lao fakalahi, ko e taha ʻi he taimi naʻe fakahoko ai ʻa e hiaʹ ʻa ia na'e Talēkita, ‘Ofisa Pule Ngāue, Sino’i Pule lahi, sekelitali pe ‘ofisa kehe pehē ‘i he sino fakakautaha pe naʻe ʻuhinga ke ne ngāue ʻi ha lakanga pehē pe naʻe kaunga ʻi ha faʻahinga founga pe faʻahinga lahi naʻaʹ ne tokangaekina hono puleʻi ʻo ha faʻahinga ngāue pe ʻa e sino fakakautaha ko iaʹ pe naʻe tokoni ki hono puleʻi pehē —

(a) ʻe lava ke kaungā fakaʻilo pe fakaʻilo kehekehe ia ʻi he hopo tatau mo e sino fakakautaha ko ia; pea

(b) kuo pau ‘o kapau ʻe halaia ʻa e sino fakakautaha ki he hia ko ia, ke lau ʻoku halaia ki he hia ko iaʹ tukukehe, ʻi hano fakakau ʻa e natula ʻo e ngāue ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ʻi he lakangaʹ pea ʻi he ngaahi tūkunga kotoa pē, kapau ‘e fakamoʻoniʻi ‘e he taha ko ia —

(i) naʻe fakahoko ʻa e hiaʹ ‘o ‘ikai ke ʻilo, loto pe ʻilo pē kae ʻikai lea ki ai ‘a e taha ko ia; pea

(ii) naʻe fakahoko ‘e he taha ko iaʹ ʻa e fakaʻehiʻehi feʻunga kotoa pē mo fakahoko ʻa e tokanga ʻoku taau ke taʻofi ha hoko ʻa e hia.

#### 167 Moʻua ʻi he ngāue ʻa e taha ngāue mo e fakafofonga

Kuo pau ‘oka ʻi ai ha taha ʻoku ala moʻua ʻi he Laoʹ ni ki ha moʻua pe tautea ki haʻane ngāue, ʻikai ngāue, taʻetokanga pe ‘ikai ngāue ʻi he loto kovi, ke ne ala moʻua ʻi he moʻua pe tautea tatau ki he ngāue kotoa pē, ʻikai ngāue, taʻetokanga pe ʻikai ngāue ʻi he loto kovi ʻa ʻene taha ngāueʹ pe ko hono fakafofonga, pe ko e taha ngāue ʻa ʻene fakafofonga, kapau ko e ngāue, ʻikai ngāue, taʻetokanga pe ʻikai ngāue ʻi he loto kovi naʻe fakahoko ia —

(a) ʻe he taha ngāue ʻa e taha ko iaʹ lolotonga ʻa e taimi ngāue ‘a e taha ko ia;

(b) ʻe he fakafofonga lolotonga ʻokuʹ ne ngāue maʻae taha ko ia; pe

(c) ʻe ha taha ngāue ʻa e fakafofonga lolotonga ʻoku fakangāueʻi ia ʻe he fakafofongaʹ pe maʻae fakafofongaʹ ʻi heʻene ngāue maʻae taha ko ia, tukukehe, ʻi hono fakakau ʻa e natula ʻo ʻene ngāueʻi he lakanga ko iaʹ pea ʻi he ngaahi tūkunga kotoa pē, kapau ‘e fakamoʻoni'i ‘e he taha ko ia —

(d) naʻe fakahoko ʻa e hia ‘ikai ke ʻilo, loto pe ʻilo kae ʻikai lea ki ai ‘a e taha ko ia; pea

(e) naʻe fakahoko ‘e he taha ko iaʹ ʻa e fakaʻehiʻehi feʻunga kotoa pē mo fakahoko ʻa e tokanga ʻoku taau ke taʻofi ʻa e hoko ʻa e hia.

#### 168 Mafai tānaki ‘o e fakamatala fakalukufua

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Puleʹ mei he taimi ki he taimi ‘i he fanongonongo tohi, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke fakahoko ‘ene ngaahi ngāue, fakahoko hono ngaahi mafaiʹ pe tukuange ‘ene ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Laoʹ ni, ke tuʻutuʻuni ha taha maʻu laiseni ke (‘i ha makatuʻunga tuʻo taha pe ‘i ha vahaʻa taimi) —

(a) tauhi ‘a e ngaahi lekooti kuo fakamahinopau pe teuteuʻi ‘a e ngaahi lipooti kuo fakamahinopau (kau ai ‘a e ngaahi lipooti kuo teuteuʻi ki he taumuʻa ke pulusi); mo

(b) fakahā ‘a e ngaahi lekootiʹ pe ngaahi lipooti ko iaʹ ki he Sino Pule.

(2) Kuo pau ke fakapapauʻi ‘e he Sino Pule ko e —

(a) fanongonongo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ‘okuʹ ne fakamahinopau —

(i) ha fakamatala ‘a ia ko e fanongonongo ko e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupuʹ ni;

(ii) ko e vahaʻa taimi (pe ngaahi vahaʻa taimi) kuo pau ke ‘oatu ai ‘e he taha maʻu laiseni ‘a e fakamatala; mo e

(iii) founga mo e sīpinga ‘a ia ‘oku tonu ke ‘oatu ai ‘a e fakamatala;

(b) koengaahivahaʻataimi(pengaahivahaʻataimi)‘ihekupu-siʻi(2)(a)(ii)

‘okuʹ ne ‘oatu ki he taha maʻu laiseni ha taimi ‘oku fakapotopoto ke teuteuʻi mo faʻu ha ngaahi lekooti pe ngaahi lipooti ‘oku fiemaʻu ‘e he fanongonongo.

(3) Kuo pau ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘i heʻene maʻu ha fanongonongo kuo ‘oatu ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (1) ke ne fakapapauʻi ‘oku faipau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e fanongonongoʹ ‘i loto ‘i he vahaʻa taimi kuo fakamahinopau.

(4) Fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (5) mo e (6), ko ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘okuʹ ne —

(a) maumauʻi ‘a e kupu-siʻi (3); pe

(b) ‘oatu ‘i he loto pe taʻetokanga lahi ha fakamatala ‘oku loi pe takihala ki heSinoPuleʹ‘ihaʻanetalikihafanongonongokuo‘oatu‘eheSinoPuleʹ

‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (1); kuo pau kuo ne maumauʻi ‘a e Laoʹ ni.

(5) Ko e kupu-siʻi (3) ‘oku ‘ikai ngāueʻaki ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ‘oku ‘ikai lava

‘o faipau ki ha fanongonongo kuo ‘oatu ki ai ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ‘i he kupu-siʻi

(1).

(6) ‘Oku ‘ikai ha meʻa ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘okuʹ ne tuʻutuʻuniʻi ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ne faʻu ha fakamatala te ne tuku atu ha fakamatala ko e kaveinga ‘o ha monū’ia ki he ngāue fakalao.

(7) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule —

(a) ke ne toki ngāueʻaki pē ‘a e fakamatala ‘oku maʻu ‘i he kupuʹ ni koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke fakahoko ‘ene ngaahi ngāue, fakahoko hono ngaahi mafaiʹ pe fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Laoʹ ni;

(b) fakatatau ki he palakalafi (c), ke ne tauhi ha fakamatala kuo maʻu ‘i he kupuʹ ni ke fakapulipuli pea ke ‘oua ‘e tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala ‘ikai loto ki ai ‘a e taha naʻaʹ ne ‘oatu, tukukehe —

(i) koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke fakahoko ‘ene ngaahi fatongia, fakahoko hono ngaahi mafaiʹ pe fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Laoʹ ni;

(ii) ‘i he loto ki ai ‘a e taha maʻu laiseni kuo ne ‘oatu ‘a e fakamatala; pe

(iii) kapau ‘oku fakangofua ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ‘i he Laoʹ ni pe ‘oku tuʻutuʻuniʻi ‘e he laoʹ ke tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala; mo

(c) ngāue ki he fakamatala pelepelengesi kuo maʻu ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu 24.

(8) ‘Oku ‘ikai fakaʻatā ha taha mei hono ‘oatu ‘o e fakamatalaʹ pe faʻu ha tohi ‘o fakatatau ki he kupuʹ ni ‘i he ‘uhinga ‘e ngofua ki he fakamatalaʹ pe tohiʹ ke ne fakahalaiaʻi ‘a e taha ko ia, ka ko e fakamatala pe tohi pehē ‘oku ‘ikai ke ala fakahū ‘i he fakamoʻoni ‘o fakafepaki ki he taha ‘i he hopo hiaʹ kae kehe mei he ngaahi hopo ‘i he, pe ‘oku fakatupunga, ‘i he kupuʹ ni.

#### 169 Mafai fakatotolo

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Sino Puleʹ kapau ‘oku tui fakapotopoto ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ‘oku malava ha taha ‘o ‘oatu ‘a e fakamatala, faʻu ‘a e ngaahi tohiʹ pe ‘oatu ‘a e fakamoʻoni ʻo felaveʻi mo ha maumau ‘e ala hoko ki he Laoʹ ni (maumau ‘e ala hoko), ke ‘oatu ki he taha ko iaʹ ha fanongonongo ‘o tuʻutuʻuniʻi ke —

(a) faʻu ‘a e ngaahi tohiʹ pe fakamatala tohi kehe; pe

(b) hā ‘i he ‘ao ‘o e Sino Puleʹ ke ‘oatu ‘a e fakamoʻoni ‘i he lea.

(2) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule ke ne —

(a) fakapapauʻi ko ha fanongonongo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1)(a) ‘okuʹ ne fakamahinopau —

(i) ha tohi ‘a ia ko e fanongonongo ko e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupuʹ ni;

(ii) ko e tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e Laoʹ ni ‘a ia ‘oku fekauʻaki ki ai ‘a e maumau

‘e ala hoko;

(iii) ‘a e vahaʻa taimi (pe ngaahi vahaʻa taimi) ‘a ia kuo pau ke ‘oatu ai

‘e he taha ko iaʹ ‘a e fakamatala; mo e

(iv) founga mo e sīpinga ‘a ia ‘oku tonu ke ‘oatu ai ‘a e fakamatala;

(b) fakapapauʻi ko ha fanongonongo ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1)(b) ‘okuʹ ne fakamahinopau —

(i) ‘a e ngaahi meʻa ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ai ‘i he palakalafi (a)(i) mo e (ii);

(ii) ha vahaʻa taimi lolotonga ‘a ia kuo pau ke hā ai ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ‘i he ‘ao ‘o e Sino Pule; mo e

(iii) founga mo e sīpinga ‘a ia ‘oku tonu ke ‘oatu ai ‘a e fakamatala;

(c) fakapapauʻi ko ha —

(i) vahaʻa taimi ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2)(a)(iii) ‘okuʹ ne ‘oatu ki he taha ko iaʹ ha taimi feʻunga ke faʻu ha ngaahi tohi pe fakamatala tohi ‘oku fiemaʻu ‘e he fanongonongo; mo e

(ii) vahaʻa taimi ‘i he kupu-siʻi (2)(b) ‘oku oatu ki he taha ko iaʹ ha faingamalie fakapotopoto ke teuteuʻi ai ‘a e fakamoʻoni, kumi ki ha fakafofonga lao mo hā ‘i he ‘ao ‘o e Sino Pule; mo

(d) fakaʻatā ha fakafofonga lao ke hā maʻae pe fakafofongaʻi ha taha ‘oku fiemaʻu ke hā ‘i he ‘ao ‘o e Sino Puleʹ ke ‘oatu ‘a e fakamoʻoni ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1)(b).

(3) Kuo pau ki ha tahaʹ ‘i haʻane maʻu ha fanongonongo kuo ‘oatu ‘e he Sino Puleʹ

‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (1) ke ne fakapapauʻi ‘okuʹ ne faipau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e fanongonongoʹ ‘i loto ‘i he vahaʻa taimi kuo fakamahinopau.

(4) Fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (5) mo e (6), ko ha taha ‘okuʹ ne —

(a) maumauʻi ‘a e kupu-siʻi (3); pe

(b) ‘oatu ‘i he loto pe taʻetokanga lahi ha fakamatala ‘oku loi pe takihala ki heSinoPuleʹ‘ihaʻanetalikihafanongonongokuo‘oatu‘eheSinoPuleʹ

‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (1), kuo pau kuo ne maumauʻi ‘a e Laoʹ ni.

(5) ‘Oku ‘ikai ngāueʻaki ‘a e kupu-siʻi (3) ki ha taha ‘oku ‘ikai lava ‘o faipau ki ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo ‘oatu ki ai ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1).

(6) ‘Oku ‘ikai ha meʻa ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘okuʹ ne tuʻutuʻuniʻi ha maʻu laiseni ke ne faʻu ha fakamatala ‘e ala tuku atu ‘a e fakamatalaʹ ‘a ia ko e kaveinga ‘o ha monū’ia ki he ngāue fakalao.

(7) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule —

(a) fakatatau ki he palakalafi (b), ke ne tauhi ‘a e fakamatala kuo maʻu ‘i he kupuʹ ni ke fakapulipuli pea ke ‘oua ‘e ngāueʻaki pe tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala ko iaʹ tukukehe —

(i) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o e —

(A) fakahoko ‘ene ngaahi mafai ‘i he kupu 159, 160, 161, 162, 163 pe 164 ‘o felaveʻi mo e maumau ‘e ala hoko; pe

(B) ko ha fakatotolo ki he maumau ‘e ala hoko;

(ii) ‘i he loto ki ai ‘a e taha naʻaʹ ne ‘oatu ‘a e fakamatala; pe

(iii) kapau ‘oku fakangofua ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ‘i he Laoʹ ni pe ‘oku tuʻutuʻuniʻi ‘e he laoʹ ke ne tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala; mo

(b) ngāue ki he fakamatala pelepelengesi kuo maʻu ‘i he kupuʹ ni ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu 24, ka ‘e ngofua ki he Sino Pule ke ne tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala pelepelengesi —

(i) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakahoko ‘ene ngaahi mafai ‘i he kupu 159, 160, 161, 162, 163 pe 164 ‘o e Laoʹ ni; pe

(ii) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o ha fakatotolo ki he maumau ‘e ala hoko, ka —

(A) kimuʻa honotuku atu‘ae fakamatala,‘ealea ‘a e Sino Puleʹ mo e taha naʻaʹ ne ‘oatu ‘a e fakamatala; pea

(B) ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he Sino Puleʹ ha ngaahi laka fakapotopoto ke fakapapauʻi ‘oku ‘ikai tuku atu ‘a e fakamatala pelepelengesiʹ ki ha taha feʻauʻauhi ‘o ha taha ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala pelepelengesi.

(8) ‘Oku ‘ikai fakaʻatā ha taha mei hono ‘oatu ‘a e fakamatalaʹ pe faʻu ha tohi ‘o fakatatau ki he kupuʹ ni ‘i he ‘uhinga ‘e ngofua ki he fakamatalaʹ pe tohiʹ ke ne fakahalaiaʻi ‘a e taha ko ia.

(9) Ko ha taha ‘oku ne tuku atu ‘ikai ‘i ai ha mafai fakalao —

(a) ‘a e moʻoni kuo fakahoko ‘a e fanongonongo ‘i he kupuʹ ni; pe

(b) faʻahingameʻakuofakahoko(kauai ‘aemoʻonikuo‘oatuhafakamatala ki he Sino Pule) ‘i ha fanongonongo ‘i he kupuʹ ni, kimuʻa ‘a e;

(c) kamataʻanga ‘o ha hopo ke fakamalohiʻi ‘i he kupu 159, 160, 161, 162, 163 pe 164 ‘o e Laoʹ ni ‘o felaveʻi mo e maumau ‘e ala hoko; pe

(d) maʻu ‘o ha fanongonongo tohi mei he Sino Puleʹ ‘a ia ko e fakatotolo ki he maumau ‘e ala hokoʹ kuo ngata,

‘okuʹ ne fakahoko ‘a e hia pea kuo pau ke moʻua ki he tautea paʻanga

‘oku ‘ikai laka hake ‘i he $10,000 pe ki he ngāue nofo pōpula ‘o ‘ikai laka hake ‘i he ta’u ‘e 3 pe fakatouʻosi.

#### 170 Totonu ke hū ‘o sivi

ʻE ngofua ki ha ʻofisa kuo fakamafaiʻi ‘e he Potungāueʹ pe Sino Pule, ʻaki ha tohi fakamafai mei ha Fakamaau Polisi, ke ne hū ki ha ʻapi pea ʻe ngofua ke ne —

(a) fakahoko ha kumi ʻi ha ʻapi;

(b) sivi mo faitā pe tā ha fakatātā;

(c) sivi ha faʻahinga tohi, lekooti tohi ngāue, tetapeisi, pe faile ʻilekitulōnika ʻoku tauhi ʻi he ʻapi ko ia; pe

(d) toʻo pe hiki tatau ha faʻahinga tohi, tetapeisi, pe ha faile ʻilekitulōnika, ʻo aʻu ki ha tuʻunga ʻoku fakapotopoto ke fakapapauʻi kuo fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e Laoʹ ni, Lao ki he Telekalafiʹ pe ngaahi lao kehe 'oku fekauʻaki mo ia.

#### 171 Hia fekauʻaki mo e kumi mo e puke

Ka ʻi ai ha ʻofisa kuo fakamafaiʻi ʻe he Potungāueʹ pe Sino Puleʹ te ne mahamahalo ʻi ha ngaahi ʻuhinga ʻoku fakapotopoto ngalingali ʻoku ʻi ai pe ʻoku ʻi loto ʻi ha ʻapi ha faʻahinga meʻa pau te ne ala ʻomai ha fakamoʻoni ʻofekauʻaki mo ha hia ʻi he Laoʹ ni, Lao ki he Telekalafi (Vahe 99), Lao ki he Kautaha Fetuʻutaki ʻa Tonga 2000 pe ko ha lao kehe, ʻe ngofua ki he ʻofisa kuo fakamafaiʻi, ʻaki ha tohi fakamafai kuo foaki ʻe ha Fakamaau Polisi, ke fakahoko ha kumi ʻi he ʻapiʹ mo puke ʻa e me'a ko ia.

#### 172 Lava ke fakamafaiʻi ‘e he Potungāue ‘a e tukuange ‘o e ngaahi meʻa kuo

puke

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakamafaiʻi ʻe he Potungāueʹ ha faʻahinga meʻa kuo puke ‘e ha

‘ofisa kuo fakamafaiʻi ‘e he Potungāueʹ 'i he kupu 171 ke tukuange atu ia ki he taha ʻoku ʻaʻana, pe ki he taha naʻe puke mei ai ʻa e meʻa ko ia, ʻo tatau pe ʻe

‘ikai toe ʻai ha tuʻunga ki ai pe ʻe fakatatau ki ha ngaahi tuʻunga ʻe pehē ʻe he Potungāueʹ 'oku taau.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakamafaiʻi ʻe he Sino Puleʹ ha faʻahinga meʻa kuo puke ʻi he kupu 171 ke tukuange atu ia ki he taha ʻoku ʻaʻana, pe ki he taha naʻe puke mei ai ʻa e meʻa ko ia, ʻo tatau pē ʻe ‘ikai toe ʻai ha tuʻunga ki ai pe ʻe fakatatau ki ha ngaahi tuʻunga ʻe pehē ʻe he Sino Puleʹ ʻoku taau.

VAHE 3 - NGAAHI MALU’I ‘O E TAULANGA KE MALU

#### 173 ‘Oatu ‘a e hū pe fakamafola

(1) ‘Oku ‘ikai moʻua ha taha fakahoko ngāue hū (ki ha hopo sivile pe hia) ‘i hono

‘oatu ‘a e hū ki he pe fakamafola ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta, ka heʻikai ki he taha fakahoko ngāue hū ke ne —

(a) kamataʻi ‘a hono fakamafola;

(b) fili ‘a e taha ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ‘a hono fakamafola; pe

(c) filipeliliu‘aefakamatalafakakomipiuta‘oku‘iloto‘ihonofakamafola.

(2) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu-siʻi (2), ko hono ‘oatu ‘o e hū ki he pe fakamafola ‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ‘oku kau ai ‘a hono tauhi ‘otometiki, lotoloto mo ‘ikai tolonga ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ki he tefitoʻi taumuʻa ke ‘oatu ‘a e hū ki he pe fakamafola ‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ka ‘oku

‘ikai tauhi ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ‘i ha vahaʻa taimi ‘oku lōloa ange ‘i hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki hono ‘oatu ‘o e hū ki he, pe fakamafola ‘o e, fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko ia.

#### 174 Kakano tuku atu

(1) ‘Oku ‘ikai moʻua ha taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue (ki ha hopo sivile pe hia) ‘o felaveʻi mo e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ‘oku tauhi pe fakamafola ‘i he kole ‘a ha taha ‘okuʹ ne ngāueʻaki, ka ko e taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue —

(a) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahokoʹ hili hono maʻu ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o ha Fakamaauʻanga feʻunga ke toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e hū ki ha fakamatala fakakomipiuta pau, ke toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e hū ki he fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko ia;

(b) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahokoʹ hili hono ‘ilo (kehe mei ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o ha Fakamaauʻanga feʻunga) ‘okuʹ ne tauhi ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ‘a ia ‘oku ‘i ai ha ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘oku fakapotopoto ke tui ‘oku ʻikai fakalao ke maʻu, hū, tufa pe pulusi ‘i he ngaahi lao ‘a e Puleʻanga, fanongonongo ki he Polisi ‘a Tonga ‘a e ‘i ai mo e tuʻuʻanga

‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko iaʹ (fakataha mo e ngaahi ‘uhinga kuo ne tataki ‘a e taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāueʹ ke ne fanongonongo ki he Polisi Tongaʹ fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko eni); pea

(c) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahokoʹ hili ‘ene maʻu ‘a e ‘ilo tonu, pe ‘ilo ki he ngaahi moʻoni pe ngaahi tūkunga ‘a ia ‘oku hā mahino, ‘okuʹ ne tauhi ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ‘okuʹ ne maumauʻi ‘a e ngaahi totonu ki he koloa fakaʻatamai ‘a ha taha, toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e hū ki he fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko ia.

(2) Kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai ke ngāueʻaki ‘a e kupu-siʻi (1) kapau ‘oku ngāue ‘a e taha ngāueʻaki totonuʹ ‘i he mafai pe pule ‘o e taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue.

#### 175 Tauhi

‘Oku ‘ikai moʻua ha taha fakahoko ngāue tauhi (ki ha hopo sivile pe hia) ‘i hono tauhi ʻotometiki pe fakataimi ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ‘oku haʻu mei ha taha kehe pe ko e fakamafola ‘o e fakamatala ko iaʹ ki ha kakai hono toluʹ ‘i he kole ‘a e kakai hono tolu ko ia, ka —

(a) ‘e ‘ikai ke liliu ‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue tauhi ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta;

(b) kapau ‘oku fakatatau ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ki ha ngaahi tuʻunga hū, hangē ko ha tuʻutuʻuni ke totongi ha paʻanga pe fakahū ha lea-tapu (password) ke hū ki he fakamatala fakakomipiuta, ‘oku ‘ikai ke fakahū

‘e he taha fakahoko ngāue tauhi ha konga mahuʻinga ‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko iaʹ ki he kakai ‘oku teʻeki ke nau aʻusia ‘a e ngaahi tuʻunga hū ko ia;

(c) ‘oku faipau ‘a e taha fakahoko ngāue tauhiʹ ki he ngaahi lao fekauʻaki mo e vakaiʻi ke fakaaʻu (updating) ‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiuta kuo fakamahinopau ‘i ha founga ‘oku ‘ilo lahia mo ngāueʻaki ‘e ha kautaha;

(d) ‘oku ‘ikai kaunoa ‘a e taha fakahoko ngāue tauhiʹ ‘i hono ngāueʻaki fakalao ‘o e tekinolosia, ‘ilo lahia mo ngāueʻaki ‘e he kautaha, ke maʻu mai ‘a e fakamatala ki hono ngāueʻaki ‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiuta — pea

(e) ‘oku ngāue fakavavevave ke toʻo pe ke fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e hū ki he fakamatala fakakomipiuta kuo ne tauhiʹ ‘i hono maʻu ‘a e ‘ilo tonu ‘o e moʻoni ‘a ia ko e fakamatala fakakomipiuta, ‘i he fuofua maʻuʻanga ‘o e fakamafola, kuo toʻo pe ko e hū ki aiʹ kuo fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe kuo tuʻutuʻuni ha Fakamaauʻanga feʻunga ‘a e toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaonga pehē.

#### 176 Fakafehokotaki

Ko ha taha ‘okuʹ ne fakafehokotaki ‘a e kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosiʹ ki he fakamatala fakakomipiuta kuo ‘oatu ‘e he kakai hono tolu, kau ai ‘i he ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘o ha tuʻasila fakafehokotaki (hyperlink), URL, ngaahi ola ‘o e fekumi, fakahinohino (directory), tohi hokohoko (index) pe fakahinohino kehe (taha fakahoko fehokotaki),

‘oku ‘ikai ke moʻua (‘i ha hopo sivile pe hia) ‘i hono ‘oatu ‘a e fakafehokotaki ko ia, ka ko e taha fakahoko fehokotaki —

(a) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahokoʹ hili hono maʻu ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o ha Fakamaauʻanga feʻunga ke toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e hū ki ha fakafehokotaki pau, toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e fakafehokotaki ko ia;

(b) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahokoʹ hili hono ‘ilo (kehe mei ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o ha Fakamaauʻanga feʻunga) ‘okuʹ ne ‘oatu ha fakafehokotaki ki he fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ‘a ia ‘oku ‘i ai ha ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘oku fakapotopoto ke tui ‘oku ʻikai fakalao ke maʻu, hū, tufa pe pulusi ‘i he ngaahi lao ‘a e Puleʻanga, fanongonongo ki he Polisi Tongaʹ ‘a e ‘i ai mo e tuʻuʻanga ‘o e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko iaʹ (fakataha mo e ngaahi ‘uhinga kuo ne tataki ‘a e taha fakahoko fehokotakiʹ ke ne fanongonongo ki he Polisi Tongaʹ fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ko eni) mo toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e fakafehokotaki ko ia; pea

(c) ‘i he vave taha ‘e ala fakahokoʹ hili ‘ene maʻu ‘a e ‘ilo tonu, pe ‘ilo ki he ngaahi moʻoni pe ngaahi tūkunga ‘a ia ‘oku hā mahino, ‘okuʹ ne fakafehokotaki ki he fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ‘okuʹ ne maumauʻi ha ngaahitotonu ki he koloafakaʻatamai ‘a ha taha,toʻo pefakataʻeʻaongaʻi

‘a e fakafehokotaki ko ia.

#### 177 ‘Ikai ke moʻua ‘i hono toʻo pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ‘a e hū

‘Oku fakaʻatā ‘a e kau fakahokoʹ mei he moʻua kotoa pē ki heʻenau kau ngāueʻaki fakaʻosi totonuʹ ‘o fekauʻaki mo hono toʻo pe fakataʻe’aongaʻi ‘a e hū ki he fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ‘i he loto lelei ‘o fakatatau ki he Vaheʹ ni pe fakatatau ki ha tu’utuʻuni ‘o ha Fakamaauʻanga feʻunga.

#### 178 ‘Ikai ha fatongia ke tokangaʻi

Tukukehe ‘o kapau ‘oku fiemaʻu ‘e he laoʹ pe ko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘o ha Fakamaauʻanga

‘oku feʻunga, ‘oku ‘ikai ‘i ai ha fatongia fakalukufua ‘a e kau fakahokoʹ ke nau tokangaʻi ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta ‘oku nau fakamafola, tauhi ko e fakafofonga

‘ohatahakehe,pefakafehokotakikihe,pea‘ikaike‘iaihaʻanaufatongiafakalukufua ke fekumi ki he ngaahi moʻoni mo e ngaahi tūkunga ‘oku ne fakahā ‘a e ngʻue ʻikai fakalao pe ngāue ‘okuʹ ne maumauʻi ‘a e ngaahi totonu ki he koloa fakaʻatamai ‘a ha taha ke fakaʻehiʻehi mei he moʻua.

#### 179 ‘Uhingaʻi Lea

Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Vaheʹ ni —

(a) “taha fakahoko ngāue hū” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha taha (kau ai ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘initaneti) ‘okuʹ ne ‘oatu, pe fokotuʻu ke ‘oatu, ha ngāue fetuʻutaki ki he kakaiʹ ‘aki hono fakahoko ‘a e fetuʻutaki kuo ‘oatu ‘e he pe ki ha taha ‘okuʹ ne ngāueʻaki ‘a e ngāue fetuʻutakiʹ pe ‘oatu ‘a e hū ki he netiueka fetuʻutaki;

(b) “taha fakahoko ngāue tauhi” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha taha ‘okuʹ ne ‘oatu ha ngāue

‘oku kau ki hono tauhi ‘otometiki mo fakataimi, ‘i he Puleʻanga, ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiuta naʻe ‘omai mei ha taha kehe koeʻuhi ke ala fakamafola ‘a e fakamatala fakakomipiutaʹ ki ha kakai hono toluʹ ‘i he kole ‘a e kakai hono tolu ko ia;

(c) “ngaahitotonu ki he koloafakaʻatamai” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha totonu ‘oku ‘oatu ki ha taha ‘i he kupu 6, 7, 22, 33 pe 25 ‘o e Lao ki he Totonu ‘o e Hiki Tatau 2002 mo e ngaahi totonu kehe pehē‘e ngofua ke fakapapauʻi ‘ehe Minisitā‘aki hono tuʻutuʻuni;

(d) “taha fakahoko fehokotaki” ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ‘a e ‘uhinga tatau ‘oku ‘oatu ‘i he kupu 176; mo e

(e) “taha fakahoko” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki ha taha fakahoko ngāue hū, ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue tauhi, ko ha taha tuku atu fakahoko ngāue pe ko ha taha fakahoko fehokotaki.

VAHE 4 - NGAAHI FOUNGA VAKAIʻI

#### 180 Ngaahi fili ki hono toe vakaiʻi

(1) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (2), ‘oku uesia fakahangatonu

‘e ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu ke ne tohi kole —

(a) ke toe fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 181;

(b) ke vakaiʻi ‘a e tuʻutu’uni ‘i he kupu 183; pe

(c) ki ha Fakamaauʻanga ke vakaiʻi faka-fakamaauʻanga ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu.

(2) ‘E ‘ikai ngofua ke tohi kole ha taha ki he Fakamaauʻangaʹ fekauʻaki mo ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu —

(a) kapau ‘oku teʻeki ke tohi kole ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ke vakaiʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni

‘i he kupu 183; pe

(b) kapau kuo tohi kole ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ke vakaiʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 183 pea ‘oku teʻeki ke fakahoko ‘e he penolo tangiʹ ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 183(9).

(3) Ko ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu ko e taha ‘o e ngaahi meʻa ko eni —

(a) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke ne ‘ikai tali ke foaki ha laiseni fakahoko ngāue netiueka

‘i he kupu 32;

(b) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke hilifaki ha tuʻunga laiseni makehe ‘i he kupu 34;

(c) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke taʻofi fakataimi pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi, pe fakatonutonu ‘a e ngaahi tuʻunga laiseni makehe ‘o ha, laiseni fakahoko ngāue netiueka

‘i he kupu 38;

(d) ha tuʻutu’uni ke neʻikai tali ke lesisitahatahako ha taha fakahoko ngāue

‘i he kupu 41;

(e) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke toʻo ‘a e lesisitaʹ pe taʻofi fakataimi ‘a e lesisita ‘o ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘i he kupu 44;

(f) ha fakahinohino ‘okuʹ ne fiemaʻu ha taha maʻu laiseni ke tuku atu ha ngāue fetuʻutaki kuo kole ‘i he kupu 84;

(g) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke taʻofi fakataimi, fakataʻe’aongaʻi pe ʻikai tali ke foaki ha laiseni ivi fakamafola fakaletiō ‘i he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he ivi fakamafola fakaletiō;

(h) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke ‘ikai foaki ha ngofua ki ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ne maʻu mai ha meʻa ‘oku tapui ‘i he kupu 120(5);

(i) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke ‘ikai tali ke foaki hano fakamafaiʻi ‘i he kupu 121(1);

(j) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahinohino ha taha maʻu laiseni ke ne fakatonutonu ha aleapau ‘i he kupu 134(1);

(k) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tali pe ‘ikai tali ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he kupu 136(4);

(l) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tuʻutuʻuni ha tohi tuʻuaki kuo tali pe fakahinohino ha taha maʻu laiseni ke fakahoko ‘a e ngāue kuo fakamahinopau ‘i he kupu 136(6);

(m) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tali ‘a e liliu ki ha tohi tuʻuaki ‘i he kupu 137(3)(b);

(n) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke ‘oatu ha fakahā ‘o ha maumau ‘i he kupu 162;

(o) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke ‘oatu ha tuʻutuʻuni tuku pe fakalelei fakahinohino ‘i he kupu 164; pe

(p) ha tuʻutuʻuni kehe kuo fakamahinopau ko ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu ‘i ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku fakahoko ‘e he Minisitā.

(4) Ko ha tuʻutuʻuni Fakaminisitā toe ala liliu ko e taha ‘o e ngaahi meʻa ko eni —

(a) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tali pe ‘ikai tali ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ‘i he kupu 73(4) (‘o felaveʻi mo e malava ke ‘ave holo ‘a e fika); pe

(b) ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tali pe ‘ikai tali ha fokotuʻu tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 181 Toe fakakaukauʻi ‘o e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Sino Pule

(1) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha ‘oku uesia fakahangatonu ‘e ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ke tohi kole ki he Sino Puleʹ ke toe fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuniʹ ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 10 mei he tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘oku fiemaʻu ke toe fakakaukauʻi lolotonga ‘oku fanongonongo ki he taha ko ia.

(2) Kuo pau ko ha tohi kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) ke ne fakamahinopau —

(a) ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ‘o felave’i mo e tohi kole;

(b) ‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘oku fiemaʻu ai ke toe fakakaukauʻi; mo e

(c) tuʻutuʻuni naʻe fiemaʻu ki hono toe fakakaukauʻi.

(3) Kuo pau ki he Sino Pule, ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20 mei hono maʻu ‘o ha tohi kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke ne fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tohi koleʹ pea fanongonongo ki he taha koleʹ pe ‘e toe fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ki he Sino Puleʹ ‘o kapau ‘oku teʻeki ke maʻu ‘e he taha koleʹ ha tali ki he tohi koleʹ ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20 hili ‘a hono faile ‘a e tohi koleʹ ke toe fakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni, ke lau kuo ne fakapapauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuniʹ ‘o fekauʻaki mo hono fakahoko ‘a e tohi koleʹ ke toe fakakaukauʻi.

(5) Kuo pau ‘o kapau ‘e tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ke ne toe fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni, ke faipau ki he ngaahi founga ngāue tatau (hangē ko hono aleaʻi mo teuteuʻi ‘o ha tuʻutuʻuni fakaangaanga) ‘oku tuʻutuʻuni ke faipau ki ai ‘i he taimi naʻe fuofua fakahoko ai ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni.

(6) Kuo pau ko hatuʻutuʻuni ‘ihono toe fakakaukauʻi ke hokohoko atu ‘ene ngāueʹ lolotonga ‘a hono toe fakakaukauʻiʹ tukukehe kapau ‘e fili ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ke taʻofi fakataimi ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuniʹ ‘aki hono fanongonongo ki he taha naʻaʹ ne fakahoko ‘a e tohi koleʹ ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) mo ha taha kehe

‘oku uesia fakahangatonu ‘i he tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e taʻofi fakataimi ko ia.

#### 182 Penolo Tangi

(1) Kuo pau ki he Potungāue, ‘i he fokotuʻu atu ‘a e ‘Ateni-Senialeʹ mo e ‘Eiki Fakamaau Lahi, ke fokotuʻu ha lesisita ‘o e kau mataotaoʹ ‘a ia kuo pau ke nau faingamalie ke ngāue ko e kau mēmipa ‘o ha penolo tangi ‘i he kupu 183.

(2) Kuo pau ke fakapapauʻi ‘e he Potungāueʹ ‘oku ‘i he lesisita ‘o e kau mataotaoʹ

‘i he taimi kotoa pē ha toko tolu ‘oku ‘i ai ‘enau mataotao fakakomesiale, fakatekinikale, fakaʻekonomika mo fakalao ‘i he malaʻe ‘o e fetuʻutaki.

(3) ‘E ngofua ki he Potungāueʹke ne fili pē ha taha ki he lesisita ‘o e kau mataotaoʹ kapau ko e taha ko ia te ne —

(a) aʻusia ‘a e tuʻunga taau ke fili ko ha Komisiona ‘i he Lao ki he Ngaahi Fetuʻutaki;

(b) ‘okuʹ ne maʻu ‘a e taukei fakavahaʻapuleʻanga ‘oku ‘ikai ke toe siʻi hifo

‘i he taʻu ‘e 10 ‘i hono puleʻi ‘o e ngaahi maketi fetuʻutaki; pea

(c) ‘oku ‘ikai ko ha mēmipa, ‘ofisa, taha ngāue, fakafofonga (agent), taha faleʻi pe fakafofonga (consultant) ‘o e Sino Pule, Potungāueʹ pe ha taha maʻu laiseni pea naʻe ‘ikai ko ha taha pehē ‘o ‘ikai siʻi hifo ‘i he māhina

‘e 12.

(4) Kuo pau ke toʻo ‘e he Potungāueʹ ha taha mei he lesisita ‘o e kau mataotaoʹ kapau ‘oku ‘ikai ke toe aʻusia ‘e he taha ko iaʹ ‘a e tuʻunga ki hono fili ‘i he kupu-siʻi (3) pe ‘o kapau ‘e toki kole ‘e he taha ko ia.

(5) Kuo pau ke ngāue ‘a e Potungāueʹ ko e sekelitali ki he penolo tangiʹ pea kuo pau ke pulusi ‘a e tuʻunga mēmipa ‘o e lesisita ‘o e kau mataotao.

#### 183 Toe vakaiʻi ‘o ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he penolo tangi

(1) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha ‘o fakatatau ki he kupu-siʻi (2), ‘oku uesia fakahangatonu

‘e ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu ke tohi kole ki he sekelitali ‘o e penolo tangiʹ ke vakaiʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) ‘E ngofua ke toki tohi kole ha taha ki he sekelitali ‘o e penolo tangiʹ ke vakaiʻi ha tuʻutuʻuni toe ala liliu ‘i he kupu 180(3)(a), (d) pe (n) kapau kuo tohi kole ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ki he Sino Puleʹ ke toe fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 182(1) pea ko e Sino Pule kuo ne —

(a) fanongonongo ki he taha koleʹ pe ‘e toe fakakaukauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 181(3); pe

(b) kuo lau ‘e he Sino Puleʹ kuo ne fakapapauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 181(4).

(3) Kuo pau ko ha tohi kole ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1) te ne —

(a) fakamahinopau ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e tohi kole;

(b) fakamahinopau ‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘oku fakahoko ai hono vakaiʻiʹ pea mo e ola ‘oku fiemaʻu mei he penolo tangi; pea

(c) ke fakahū atu ki he sekelitaliʹ ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 20 mei hono pulusi ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ki he sekelitaliʹ ‘i haʻane maʻu ha tohi kole ‘oku totonu ke vakaiʻi ‘i he kupu-siʻi (1), ke ne ‘ave ‘a e tohi koleʹ ki he ‘Ateni-Seniale, ‘a ia kuo pau ke ne fili ha penolo (penolo tangi) ‘oku ‘i ai ha mēmipa pe kau mēmipa mei he lesisita ‘o e kau mataotaoʹ ‘i hano fakakau —

(a) ‘a e tuʻunga feʻunga ‘o e mataotaoʹ ke ne fakamāuʻi ‘a e kaveinga hono vakaiʻi; mo e

(b) faingamalie ‘o e mataotao.

(5) Kuo pau ki he ‘Ateni Senialeʹ ‘o kapau kuo fili ha mēmipa ‘o laka hake ‘i he tahaʹ ki he penolo tangi, ke ne fokotuʻu ha mēmipa ‘e taha ‘o e penolo ko e Sea

‘a ia kuo pau ko ‘ene fakakaukau te ne fakapapauʻi ‘aki ‘a hono vakaiʻi ‘i he hoko ‘o ha māhanga.

(6) Ko e penolo tangi —

(a) kuo pau ke ne ‘oatu ha tatau ‘o e tohi koleʹ ke vakaiʻi ki he Sino Puleʹ mo ha kakai ‘oku uesia fakahangatonu ‘e he tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku vakaiʻiʹ mo ha taha kehe kuo ne fakahoko ha fokotuʻu ki he Sino Puleʹ ‘i ha alea ‘o felaveʻi mo e tuʻutuʻuni;

(b) te ne ‘oatu ki he kakai ‘e ngalingali ke uesia ‘i he tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Sino Puleʹ ha faingamalie fakapotopoto ke fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi fokotuʻu ki he meʻa ko ia;

(c) ‘e ngofua ke nau fakapapauʻi ha tohi kole ‘i he ngaahi pepa pe fakatahaʻi ha hopo ‘ia kinautolu pē ‘i he fakataha ‘i he ope (teleconference) pe ngaahi ‘uhinga kehe ‘okuʹ ne fakaʻatā ‘a e kotoa ‘o kinautolu ‘oku kauʹ ke nau fetuʻutaki fakaekinautolu fakataha pea mo e taimi pē ko ia;

(d) kuo pau ke nau fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 60 mei hono fili ‘i he kupu-siʻi (4); pea

(e) ‘i he ngaahi meʻa kotoa, ‘e ngofua ke nau fakapapauʻi ‘enau ngaahi foungangāuepē‘akinautoluʹ‘okunaupehē‘okufeʻunga,kakoengaahi founga ngēue pehē ‘oku ‘ikai ke ne ‘oatu ki ha taha ‘oku uesia ‘e he tuʻutuʻuni ha faingamalie ‘i ha taha kehe kuo uesia ‘e he tuʻutuʻuni.

(7) Kuo pau ko hono vakaiʻiʹ ‘e toe fakamāuʻi ‘a e fakamoʻoni ‘i he ‘ao ‘o e Sino Pule. Ko e penolo tangi —

(a) kuo pau ke naulau‘ae ngaahifakamatalanaʻetoki ‘oatu ki heSinoPuleʹ mo e fakamatala kuo fakahū atu ‘i he kupu-siʻi (6)(b) ‘a ia ‘okuʹ ne vakaiʻi ke fakaaʻu (updates) ‘a e fakamatala naʻe toki ‘oatu ki he Sino Pule; pea

(b) ‘e ngofua, ‘i he ngaahi tūkunga makehe, ke nau lau ki he fakamatala foʻou kuo fakahū atu ‘e ha faʻahi ki hono vakaiʻi.

(8) ‘E ngofua ki he penolo tangiʹ ke toloi ‘a e ngēue ‘o e tuʻutuʻuniʹ lolotonga ‘a e lau ki he tohi kole.

(9) ‘E ngofua ki he penolo tangi, ‘aki ha fanongonongo tohi, ke fakahoko ‘a e taha

‘o e ngaahi meʻa ni —

(a) fakapapauʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku vakaiʻi;

(b) tuku ki hetafaʻaki‘aetuʻutuʻuni ‘oku vakaiʻipea‘avefakafoki‘ae meʻa ko iaʹ ki he Sino Pule;

(c) fakafaikehekeheʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku vakaiʻi; pe

(d) tuku ki he tafaʻakiʹ ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ‘oku vakaiʻiʹ pea fakahoko ‘enau tuʻutuʻuni pē ‘a kinautolu, ‘a ia kuo pau ke lau ko e tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Sino Pule.

(10) Kuo pau ki he penolo tangiʹ ke ‘oatu ha tatau ‘o ‘enau tuʻutuʻuni mo e ngaahi

‘uhinga ‘o e tuʻutuʻuniʹ ki he Sino Puleʹ mo e taha takitaha ‘oku uesia fakahangatonu ‘e he tuʻutuʻuni.

(11) Kuo pau ki he Sino Puleʹ ke ne pulusi ha tuʻutuʻuni mo e ngaahi ‘uhinga ki he tuʻutuʻuni kuo ‘oatu ki ai ‘i he kupu-siʻi (10) ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ngaue ‘e 5 mei hono maʻu ‘o e tuʻutuʻuni.

(12) Kuo pau ko e ngaahi totongi ‘o e penolotangiʹ ‘ihono fakahoko ‘a hono vakaiʻi (kau ai, kae ‘ikai fakangatangata ki ha, fefolauʻaki pe ngaahi fakamole kehe kuo fakatupu ‘e he pe ko e fakafofonga ‘o e penolo tangiʹ fekauʻaki mo hono vakaiʻi) ‘e tali ‘e he taha ‘okuʹ ne fakahoko ‘a e tohi koleʹ ke vakai’i.

(13) ‘E ngofuaki hepenolotangiʹke naufakahoko hatuʻutuʻuni totongi‘i hengaahi tuʻunga makehe pea, kapau ‘e ‘ikai fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni pehē, kuo pau ke fua pē ‘e he faʻahi takitaha ‘a e ngaahi totongi fakalao ‘a kinautolu.

(14) Ko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e penolo tangiʹ ‘oku aofangatuku mo fakalao, fakatatau ki he kupu 180(1)(c).

#### 184 Vakaiʻi ‘o e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni Fakaminisitā toe ala liliu

‘E ngofua ki ha taha ‘oku uesia fakahangatonu ‘e ha tuʻutuʻuni Fakaminisitā toe ala liliuʹ ke ne tohi kole ki he sekelitali ‘o e penolo tangiʹ ke vakaiʻi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuniʹ ‘o fakatatau ki he founga ngāue ‘oku tuku atu ‘i he kupu 183, ka —

(a) ko e ngaahi lave ki he Sino Puleʹ ‘i he kupu 183 ‘e lau ko e ngaahi lave ki he Minisitā; pea

(b) kuo pau heʻikai maʻu ‘e he penolo tangiʹ ‘a e ngaahi mafai ‘i he kupu 183(9)(c) pe (d).

#### 185 Ngāue ‘o e ngaahi fanongonongo

Tukukehe ‘o kapau ‘oku tuʻutuʻuni mahino ‘i he Laoʹ ni, ko ha tohi ‘oku fiemaʻu pe fakamafaiʻi ‘e he Laoʹ ni ke ‘oatu ki he pe tufa ki ha taha, ‘e ngofua ke ‘oatu pe tufa ki he taha ‘oku ‘uhinga ki aiʹ ‘aki hono —

(a) tufa atu ki he taha ko iaʹ pe ki ha kau mēmipa matuʻotuʻa pe taha ngāue ‘o e famili ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ‘i he ʻapi nofoʻanga fakamuimui ‘a e taha ko ia;

(b) tuku ‘i he ‘api nofoʻanga angamaheni pe fakamuimui ‘o e taha ko iaʹ pe feituʻu fakapisinisi ‘i ha takafi (cover) ‘oku fakatuʻasila ki he taha ko ia;

(c) fakapipiki ki ha konga ‘oku ala ‘ilo ‘i he ‘api nofoʻanga fakamuimui ‘a e taha ko ia;

(d) ‘ave atu ‘aki ‘a e meili ‘osi lesisita ‘o fakatuʻasila ki he taha ko iaʹ ‘i he ‘api nofoʻanga fakamuimui pe feituʻu fakapisinisi ‘a e taha ko ia; pe

(e) kapau ko e taha ko iaʹ ko ha sinoʻi fakakautaha —

(i) tufa atu ki he sekelitaliʹ pe ‘ofisa tatau kehe ‘o e sinoʻi fakakautahaʹ ‘i heʻene ‘ofisi ‘osi lesisitaʹ pe tefito; pe

(ii) ‘aveatu‘aki‘aemeili‘osilesisita‘ofakatuʻasilakihesinoʻifakakautaha

‘i heʻene ‘ofisi ‘osi lesistaʹ pe tefito.

VAHE 5 - NGAAHI TUʻUTUʻUNI MO E NGAAHI TUʻUTUʻUNI FAKATAIMI

#### 186 Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni

ʻE ngofua ki he Minisitā, ʻi ha fakangofua ʻe he Kapineti, ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ke fepaki mo e Laoʹ ni mo e ngaahi lao kehe, ʻo tuʻutuʻuni ai ki ha meʻa pe ngaahi meʻa kotoa pē ʻa ia ʻoku fiemaʻu pe fakangofua ʻe he Laoʹ ni ke tuʻutuʻuni ki hono fakahoko pe ngāueʻaki ʻo e Laoʹ ni, pe ki he anga ʻa hono fakahoko ʻa e pisinisi ʻa e Potungāueʹ pe ko e kau maʻu laiseni.

#### 187 Liliu ‘o e laiseni

(1) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (3), ko ha taha (taha maʻu laiseni lolotonga) ‘okuʹ ne, kimuʻa ‘i he kamataʻanga pē ‘o e kupuʹ ni, maʻu ha laiseni ‘i he Konga V (laiseni lolotonga) pe naʻe lesisita ‘oku kau ki ha ngāue ‘okuʹ ne fiemaʻu ha kalasi laiseni ‘i he Konga V (lesisita lolotonga) kuo pau ke —

(a) ‘i ai hano totonu ke hoko atu hono ‘oatu ‘a e ngaahi ngāue pehē ‘i hono fakangofua ‘e he laiseni lolotonga ko iaʹ pe koeʻuhi ko e lesisita lolotonga ko iaʹ ‘o fakatatau ki he, pea ‘i he ngaahi tuʻunga ‘o e, laiseni lolotongaʹ pe lesisita lolotonga ko ia;

(b) fakatatau ki ha taha ‘okuʹ ne, kimuʻa pē ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni, maʻu ha laiseni lolotonga, ke lau ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue netiueka koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e Laoʹ ni (tukukehe ‘a e ngaahi taumuʻa

‘o e kupu 36 mo e ‘uhingaʻi lea ‘o e “taha maʻu laiseni kole”);

(c) ki ha vahaʻa taimi taʻu ‘e 2 hili ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni ke ‘i ai haʻane totonu ke kole ha laiseni pe lesisita fakahoko ngāue netiueka ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘i he Konga V ‘a ia kuo pau ke fakamafaiʻi ‘a e taha ko iaʹ ke hoko atu hono ‘oatu ‘a e ngaahi ngāue fetuʻutaki tatau ‘o hangē ko ia kuo ‘oatu ‘e he taha ko iaʹ kimuʻa pē ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni; pea

(d) ‘i hano kole ‘e he taha ko iaʹ ha laiseni pe lesisita fakahoko ngāue netiueka ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘i he palakalafi (c), ke lau ‘oku malava fakapaʻanga mo fakatekinikale ‘o aʻusia ‘ene ngaahi fatongia fakalao (legislative) mo fakatuʻutuʻuni (regulatory).

(2) Ko ha laiseni lolotonga pe lesisita lolotonga naʻe ngāueʻaki kimuʻa pē ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni kuo pau ke fakataʻeʻaongaʻi pe toʻo mei he lesisita (‘i heʻene feʻunga) kapau kuo foaki ‘ehetaha koiaʹ ‘ae laiseni ko iaʹ pe lesisita ko iaʹ pe ‘oku foaki ha laiseni fakahoko ngāue netiueka pe ‘oku lesisita ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue.

(3) ‘E ngofua ki ha taha maʻu laiseni lolotonga ‘oku ‘ikai fiemaʻu ke ne maʻu ha laiseni fakahoko ngāue netiueka pe lesista ko ha taha fakahoko ngāue ‘i he kupu 26 ke tukulolo ‘ene laiseni lolotongaʹ pe fakafoki ‘ene lesisita lolotongaʹ (‘i heʻene feʻunga) ‘aki ‘a e fanongonongo tohi ki he Sino Pule.

(4) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā, ‘aki hono tuʻutuʻuni, ke ne fakalōloa ‘a e vahaʻa taimi

‘i he kupu-siʻi (1)(c) ki ha vahaʻa taimi ‘oku ‘ikai laka hake ‘i he taʻu ‘e 2.

(5) Ko e kupu 25, 38, 37 mo e 44 ‘o e Laoʹ ‘e kei ngāueʻaki ‘o hangē ko ia ko ‘enau hā kimuʻa ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni kae ‘oleva kuo fakataʻeʻaongaʻi (pe

‘osihonotaimi)‘oengaahilaisenilolotongaʹpeamoengaahilesisitalolotonga kotoa pē kuo fakafoki (pe ‘osi hono taimi).

#### 188 Ngaahi fokotuʻutuʻu fakataimi kehe

(1) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā ke ne faʻu ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he kupu 186 ‘oku ngāue ki he ngaahi fokotuʻutuʻu fakataimi, kau ai kae ‘ikai ke fakangatangata ki he —

(a) liliu ‘o e ngaahi laiseniʹ mo e ngaahi lesisitaʹ ‘i hono fakamatalaʻi ‘i he kupu 187; mo e

(b) ngaahi fokotuʻutuʻu fakapaʻanga mo fakangāue ‘oku fiemaʻu pe ala fiemaʻu ke fakapapauʻi ‘a e liliu ‘a e ngaahi fatongia ‘o e Sino Puleʹ mei he Potungāueʹ ki he Komisoni Fetuʻutaki.

(2) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisitā, ‘aki hano tuʻutuʻuni, ke ne lau ha alea ‘a ia kuo fakahoko ‘i he kupu 23 ‘i ha meʻa mahino ‘o kapau ‘oku fiemalie ‘a e Minisitā kuo hoko ha alea fakapotopoto ki he meʻa ko iaʹ kimuʻa ‘a e kamataʻanga ‘o e kupuʹ ni.

#### 189 Fakapekia

Kuo pau ke fakapekia ‘a e Lao ki he Ngaahi Fetu’utaki 2000 ‘i he ‘aho kamata’anga

‘o e Lao ni.

## TEPILE 1

NGAAHI TU’UNGA LAISENI MAHENI Fika Fakanounou (Summary) Ngaahi Tu’unga Laiseni Maheni

1. Kuo pau ke faipau ‘a e taha ma’u laiseni ki he ngaahi tu’utu’uni ‘o e Laoʹ ni mo e ngaahi tu’utu’uni kehe ‘i he Laoʹ ni Kuo pau ke faipau ‘a e taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ki —

(a) he Laoʹ ni;

(b) ha lao fakalahi mo e ngaahi tu’utu’uni

‘oku fakahoko ‘i he Laoʹ ni; mo

(c) ha ngaahi tu’utu’uni (rules),ngaahi tuʻunga, ngaahi tu’utu’uni, pe tu’utu’uni kehe (kehe mei he ngaahi fakahinohino) ‘oku fakamafai’i ke fakahoko ‘e he Sino Pule pe Minisita ‘i he Laoʹ ni (kau ai ‘a e Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua, palani fakafika mo e founga ngaue fakalukufua

2. Kuo pau ke faipau ki he ngaahi aleapau/konivesio fakavaha’apule’anga Kuo pau ke fakahoko mo tokanga’i ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ‘a e ngaahi fiema’u

‘o e ngaahi konivesio mo e ngaahi aleapau fakavaha’apule’anga felave’i mo e ngaahi naunau fetu’utaki, ngaahi ngaue fetu’utaki mo e ivi fakamafola fakaletio ‘a ia ‘oku kau ‘a Tonga ki ai, tukukehe ki he ngata’anga ‘e ngofua ai ki he Potungāue, ‘aki hono fakahaa’i, ke faka’ata ‘a e taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka mei he faipau ko ia.

3. Kuo pau ke totongi huhu’i

‘e he taha ma’u laiseni ‘a e Pule’anga Kuo pau ke totongi huhu’i ‘e he taha ma’u laiseni ‘a e Pule’anga ki ha ngaahi ngaue, ngaahi faka’ilo, ngaahi ‘eke pe ngaahi hopo

‘oku tupunga mei he pe ‘i he’ene felave’i mo ha maumau pe ‘ikai fakahoko ‘i he tafa’aki ‘o e taha ma’u laiseni.

4. Kuo pau ke ne ‘oatu ‘a e ngaahi ngaue ta’etotongi ki he Tu’i Kuo pau ke fakapapau’i ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ko ha telefoni, ngaahi ngaue

‘initaneti ‘oku ‘oatu ‘e he taha ma’u laiseni ki he ‘Ene ‘Afio ‘oku ‘oatu ta’etotongi.

5. Kuo pau ke ne faka’ata ‘a e fehokotaki ‘o e me’angaue kasitomā ‘i he ngaahi tu’unga ‘oku faitatau mo e Lao Kuo pau ke faka’ata ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ha taha ke ne fehokotaki ‘a e me’angaue kasitomā ki he’ene ngaahi naunau netiueka ‘i he ngaahi teemi mo e ngaahi tu’unga ‘e ngofua ke fakapapauʻi ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ka ko e ngaahi teemi mo e ngaahi tu’unga ‘oku faitatau mo e Laoʹ ni mo ha lao fakalahi, ngaahi tu’utu’uni pe ngaahi tu’utu’uni ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he Lao.

6. Kuo pau ke ne fakahoko

‘a e ngaahi founga malu’i fe’unga mo taau ke malu’i

‘a e mo’ui pe koloa Kuo pau ki he ‘opoleita netiueka, ‘o felave’i mo e ngaahi naunau fetu’utaki kotoa pe, ke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga malu ‘oku fe’unga mo taau kotoa pe ke malu’i ‘a e mo’ui pe koloa, kau ai ‘a e ‘asi ki ha fa’ahinga fakamafola faka’uhila pe ivi ‘uhila (radiation)

‘oku tupu mai mei he naunau ke ngaue’aki, me’angaue pe ngaahi fokotu’u kuo ngaue’aki.

## TEPILE 2

NGAAHI TU’UTU’UNI KI HE TU’UNGA ‘O E TAHA FAKAHOKO NGAUE Fika Fakanounou (Summary) Tu’utu’uni ki he taha fakahoko ngaue

1. Kuo pau ke faipau ‘a e ma’u laiseni ki he ngaahi tu’utu’uni

‘o e Laoʹ ni mo e ngaahi tu’utu’uni kehe ‘oku fa’u ‘i he Lao Kuo pau ke faipau ‘a e taha fakahoko ngaue ki —

(a) he Laoʹ ni;

(b) ha lao fakalahi mo e ngaahi tu’utu’uni ‘oku fa’u ‘i he Laoʹ ni; mo

(c) ha ngaahi tu’utu’uni, ngaahi tu’unga, ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, ngaahi tu’utu’uni pe ha tu’utu’uni kehe (kehe mei he ngaahi fakahinohino)

‘oku faka’ata ki he Sino Pule pe Minisita ke fa’u ‘i he Laoʹ ni (kau ai

‘a e Palani ‘o e Ivi Fakamafola Fakafonua, palani fakafika mo e founga ngaue fakalukufua).

2. Kuo pau ke faipau ki he ngaahi aleapau/konivesio fakavaha’apule’anga Kuo pau ke fakahoko mo tokanga’i ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue ‘a e ngaahi fiema’u

‘o e ngaahi konivesio mo e ngaahi aleapau fakavaha’apule’anga felave’i mo e ngaahi naunau fetu’utaki, ngaahi ngaue fetu’utaki mo e ivi fakamafola fakaletio ‘a ia ‘oku kau ‘a Tonga ki ai, tukukehe ki he ngata’anga ‘e ngofua ai ki he Potungāue,

‘aki hono fakahaa’i, ke faka’ata ‘a e taha fakahoko ngaue mei he faipau ko ia.

3. Kuo pau ke totongi huhu’i ‘e he ma’u laiseni ‘a e Pule’anga

‘Oku totongi huhu’i ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue ‘a e Pule’anga ki ha ngaahi ngaue, ngaahi faka’ilo, ngaahi ‘eke pe ngaahi hopo ‘oku tupunga mei he pe ‘i he’ene felave’i mo ha maumau pe ‘ikai fakahoko ‘i he tafa’aki ‘o e taha fakahoko ngaue.

4. Kuo pau ke ne ‘oatu ‘a e ngaahi ngaue ta’etotongi ki he Tu’i Kuo pau ke fakapapau’i ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue ko ha telefoni, ngaahi ngaue ‘initaneti hu ‘oku ‘oatu ‘e he ma’u laiseni ki he ‘Ene ‘Afio ‘oku ‘oatu ta’etotongi.

5. Kuo pau ke ne faka’ata ‘a e fehokotaki ‘o e me’angaue kasitomā ‘i he ngaahi tu’unga ‘oku faitatau mo e Lao Kuo pau ke faka’ata ‘e he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ha taha ke ne fehokotaki

‘a e me’angaue kasitomā ki he’ene ngaahi naunau netiueka ‘i he ngaahi teemi mo e ngaahi tu’unga ‘e ngofua ki he taha fakahoko ngaue netiueka ka ko e ngaahi teemi mo e ngaahi tu’unga ‘oku faitatau mo e Laoʹ ni mo ha lao fakalahi, ngaahi tu’utu’uni pe ngaahi tu’utu’uni ‘oku fakahoko ‘i he Lao.

6. Kuo pau ke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga malu’i fe’unga mo taau ke malu’i ‘a e mo’ui pe koloa Kuo pau ki he taha fakahoko ngaue, ‘o felave’i mo e ngaahi naunau fetu’utaki kotoa pe, ke ne fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi founga malu ‘oku fe’unga mo taau kotoa pe ke malu’i ‘a e mo’ui pe koloa, kau ai ‘a e ‘asi ki ha fa’ahinga fakamafola faka’uhila pe ivi ‘uhila (radiation) ‘oku tupu mai mei he naunau, me’angaue pe ngaahi fokotu’u kuo ngaue’aki.
